### [1] [Harness Engineering: Anatomy, Architecture, and Evolution of Coding Agents -- A Source-Code Study of Eleven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00006)
*Paul Barbaste,Tristan Darrigol,Germain Vu,Tom Wiltberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了11个生产级编码智能体运行时（harness）及一个元运行时，提出七个核心子系统、13项跨系统观察和29种设计模式。研究显示，该领域主要依赖自研异步循环与确定性检索，编码运行时正由工具演变为平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的能力不仅取决于模型，还取决于连接模型与外部世界的运行时系统。由于既有研究缺乏对生产级 harness 的系统性源码分析，本文旨在界定 harness 的范围、总结其架构规律，并为后续设计提供经验基础。

Method: 研究者对11个编码 harness 进行源码解剖，按七个规范子系统分析其最小与最大实现，并以 Omnigent 作为元运行时对照。研究覆盖约400万行 Python、TypeScript 和 Rust 代码，统计工具、技能、协议、检索和编排机制；同时对原有8个系统进行跨一个季度的源码差分，开展受控纵向比较。

Result: 研究归纳出13项跨系统观察和29种重复设计模式。11个运行时均未引入通用智能体框架，也没有使用基于向量嵌入的代码检索，而是采用手工构建的异步循环与确定性检索。SKILL.md 的采用率为9/11，高于 MCP 的8/11；ACP 已在6个系统中部署，并出现“运行时托管”这一第三种角色。纵向分析显示，系统逐渐趋同并出现模仿现象，行为策略正从提示词文本迁移至配置。

Conclusion: 到2026年上半年，编码 harness 已从单纯工具转变为平台。论文以七子系统框架、18项设计建议和一个90行的最小可用 harness 脚手架，为该新兴领域提供了架构分类、实证依据和工程实践指南。

Abstract: An agent is a model plus a harness -- the runtime that couples an LLM to the world through a loop, tools, context management, safety controls, orchestration, and extension surfaces. Harness engineering, named as a discipline in early 2026, is the design and evolution of that runtime. This paper gives the young discipline its most comprehensive empirical foundation to date: a source-code anatomy of eleven production coding harnesses (Claude Code, Codex CLI, Gemini CLI, Mistral Vibe, OpenHands, Aider, Mini-SWE-Agent, Hermes, Pi, OpenCode, OpenClaw), plus Omnigent, the first meta-harness, analyzed as a contrast point. We define what a harness is, map its seven canonical subsystems with the minimal and maximal implementation of each, and dissect all eleven systems along those subsystems. The audit yields 13 cross-cutting observations and a catalog of 29 recurring design patterns. Two absences survive a threefold corpus expansion: across roughly four million lines of Python, TypeScript, and Rust, no agent runtime imports a general-purpose agentic framework, and none retrieves code with vector embeddings; the field runs on hand-rolled async loops and deterministic retrieval. SKILL.md skills lead MCP in adoption (9/11 vs. 8/11), and ACP ships in six systems with a new third role: harness hosting. Because the original eight systems were re-pinned rather than replaced, the study also contains a controlled longitudinal sample -- the same harnesses source-diffed across one quarter -- showing convergence becoming imitation and behavioral policy migrating from prompt prose to configuration. These threads converge on the paper's thesis: in the first half of 2026 the coding harness completed a turn from tool to platform. The paper closes with 18 design recommendations and a 90-line minimum-viable-harness scaffold.

</details>


### [2] [Structure-Behavior Coalescence and the Limits of Traditional Systems Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00042)
*William S. Chao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“结构—行为合一”（SBC）原则，主张系统结构与行为并非可独立分离后再关联的维度，而是同一系统过程中的相互构成要素；系统身份、涌现与边界应由二者持续的共同决定来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系统理论通常将结构视为组件组织方式、将行为视为系统活动的时间演化，并将二者分开分析。作者认为，这一预设难以充分解释系统在变化过程中的身份连续性、涌现现象及边界如何确定，因此需要新的概念框架。

Method: 论文采用理论重构方法，首先批判结构与行为可分离的传统分析前提，随后引入SBC作为本体论与分析层面的重述原则。该原则将结构组织与行为动力学界定为单一系统过程的互构面向，并据此重新阐释系统身份、涌现和边界形成。

Result: SBC框架否定将结构和行为视为先在实体、再由模型建立对应关系的观点，转而认为二者在持续演化中共同生成。由此，系统身份不再依赖静态结构、孤立行为或二者的外部匹配，而依赖结构组织与行为动力学的持续协同确定。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是以结构—行为合一替代结构与行为二分，为理解系统身份、涌现及边界形成提供统一视角。该框架强调系统性应被理解为动态过程中的共同生成关系，而非若干可分离属性之间的组合。

Abstract: This paper examines a foundational assumption in traditional systems theory, namely that structure (the organization of components) and behavior (the evolution of system activity over time) can be treated as separable analytical dimensions. It argues that this separation contributes to persistent difficulties in explaining system identity, particularly in cases involving change, emergence, and boundary specification. To address this issue, the paper introduces Structure-Behavior Coalescence (SBC) as a reframing principle. SBC proposes that structure and behavior should not be understood as independently existing entities that are subsequently related through modeling constructs, but as mutually constitutive aspects of a single systemic process. From this perspective, system identity is understood as arising from the sustained co-determination of structural organization and behavioral dynamics, rather than from their external correspondence or alignment. This reframing provides a unified way of understanding system identity, emergence, and boundary formation within a cybernetically informed systems perspective.

</details>


### [3] [What Is a System? An Interaction-Based Account of Structure-Behavior Coalescence in General Systems Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00043)
*William S. Chao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“结构—行为聚合”（Structure-Behavior Coalescence, SBC）框架，认为系统是由组成实体间互动形成的结构化实体，系统结构与系统行为通过互动相互构成、不可分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统定义通常分别强调元素、关系、边界、功能或互动，难以统一说明系统结构与系统行为如何彼此构成，因此需要一种能够保持“系统是什么”与“系统做什么”一致性的概念框架。

Method: 论文采用概念分析方法，区分系统结构、互动与行为，同时将三者视为统一系统表征中不可分割的方面；通过分析互动如何将结构关系转化为行为过程，以及行为过程如何体现并实现系统结构，构建SBC理论。

Result: SBC将互动确立为连接结构与行为的核心机制，认为系统行为源于组成实体间的互动，而这些互动同时参与系统结构组织与行为实现。该框架进一步说明了系统身份、系统边界、行为涌现及复杂系统表征等问题。

Conclusion: SBC为一般系统论提供了一种以结构与行为通过互动实现聚合为核心的系统定义，并可作为补充现有系统理论的通用概念基础，有助于统一描述系统的构成与功能表现。

Abstract: The question of what constitutes a system remains fundamental to General Systems Theory. Existing definitions commonly characterize a system in terms of elements, relationships, boundaries, functions, or interactions, but these perspectives do not always provide a unified account of how system structure and system behavior constitute one another. This paper proposes Structure-Behavior Coalescence (SBC) as an interaction-based account of what a system is. From the SBC perspective, a system is not merely a collection of elements or relationships, nor is it adequately characterized by behavior considered independently of structure. Rather, a system is a structured entity whose behavior arises through interactions among its constituent entities, with those interactions simultaneously contributing to both its structural organization and behavioral realization. The paper develops this perspective by distinguishing system structure, interaction, and behavior while treating them as inseparable aspects of a unified system representation. It argues that interactions provide the essential link through which structural relationships become behavioral processes and through which behavioral processes reveal and instantiate system structure. This perspective provides a basis for representing systems in a manner that maintains consistency between what a system is and what a system does. The paper further considers the implications of SBC for General Systems Theory, particularly for system identity, system boundaries, behavioral emergence, and the representation of complex systems. SBC is presented as a general conceptual foundation that complements existing systems theories by placing the coalescence of structure and behavior through interaction at the center of the definition of a system.

</details>


### [4] [Towards Agentic Cloud Engineering: Graph and Loop Engineering with a Zero-Trust Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00050)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Cloud Workflow Engineering框架，将自然语言描述的云工程任务转换为经过验证的代码仓库和可运行的云部署，并通过证据门控与有界恢复确保任务最终达到可验证成功或可审计失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向长周期、多步骤的云端智能体工作流，现有方法需要同时解决流程推进、受约束执行、故障恢复和完成验证等问题。缺乏统一工程机制可能导致智能体越权、无限重试、状态不可验证及部署结果不可靠。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：图工程用于表示长周期工作流及基于验证结果的状态转移；循环工程用于执行有界诊断、修复或重新规划、重试和再次验证；智能体工具链工程通过身份认证、授权、策略限定能力、隔离和运行时安全措施实现零信任执行。系统要求利用机器可检查的代码仓库、部署状态和运行时证据推进流程，并设置明确的操作边界与终止条件。在Google Cloud上进行实例化和评估。

Result: 评估覆盖仓库完整性、受控执行、证据门控流程推进、实际云部署和有界故障恢复。实验表明，执行过程能够在限定恢复范围内终止，结果要么是经过验证的可运行云部署，要么是可审计的终止失败。

Conclusion: 该框架为云端智能体工作流提供了统一的工程架构，将流程编排、工具执行、安全约束、故障恢复和结果验证整合起来，并可扩展至DevOps、CloudOps、SRE/AIOps、SecOps、DataOps、MLOps/LLMOps、AgentOps及RAG相关云工程场景。摘要未提供与现有基线的定量对比，因此其相对性能优势仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Agentic AI is enabling cloud-based workflows in which autonomous agents reason over operational state, invoke authorized tools, modify software and infrastructure, deploy services, verify execution outcomes, and adapt across long-horizon, multistep tasks. Engineering such workflows requires explicit mechanisms for workflow progression, constrained execution, failure recovery, and verifiable completion. We present Agentic Cloud Workflow Engineering, an agentic AI framework that transforms natural-language agentic cloud-engineering tasks into validated code repositories and verified operational cloud deployments for automating cloud-based agentic workflows. The framework separates three complementary concerns: graph engineering specifies long-horizon workflow progression and verification-dependent transitions; loop engineering provides bounded diagnosis, repair or re-planning, retry, and re-verification; and agent harness engineering enforces zero-trust execution through identity, authorization, policy-scoped capabilities, isolation, and runtime safeguards. Workflow progression and completion require machine-checkable repository, deployment, and runtime evidence, with recovery constrained by explicit operational bounds and termination criteria. We instantiate the framework on Google Cloud and evaluate repository completeness, controlled execution, evidence-gated progression, operational deployment, and bounded recovery. Experimental results show that executions terminate with either a verified operational cloud deployment or an auditable terminal failure under bounded recovery. The framework provides a unified engineering architecture for cloud-based workflows spanning Agentic DevOps, Agentic CloudOps, Agentic SRE/AIOps, Agentic SecOps, Agentic DataOps, Agentic MLOps/LLMOps, AgentOps, Agentic RAG/GraphRAG, and related cloud-engineering domains.

</details>


### [5] [Can MCP Clients Decide What to Do After Failure? A Result-Only Actionability Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00072)
*Rishabh Mehan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文聚焦已完成的 MCP 失败结果能否支持确定性软件恢复。结论是：现有失败返回通常可表明“发生了错误”，有时可支持宽泛的响应策略，但很少能仅依赖结果本身识别具体原因、修复方式或安全重放条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅有 isError:true 无法让客户端以机器可读方式区分应修改参数、进行身份验证、等待重试、切换工具或终止。作者因此限定研究边界：排除请求参数、模式、发现记录、会话状态、传输元数据、宿主策略与应用状态，只考察完成后的失败结果自身所提供的信息。

Method: 作者提出六部分“可行动性画像”，并以记录级证据评估失败结果。实验从十个可访问的抽样服务器中安全诱发21个失败案例，比较结构化字段与自然语言文本对失败状态、广义策略、具体原因、作用目标、可执行修复及重放约束的表达能力；同时进行词汇来源审计，并实现一个默认拒绝的原型控制平面以验证确定性分支支持。

Result: 在21个案例中，类型化字段在18例中揭示了失败状态，在8例中支持宽泛策略，但未提供自包含的具体原因、目标、可执行修复方案或重放限制。自然语言描述通常包含更多原因与目标线索，但这要求恢复流程具备语义解释能力。词汇来源审计也显示失败信息主要依赖文本表达。原型表明，独立的实验性控制平面能够为确定性分支提供更明确支持。

Conclusion: 该研究并非全面生态调查或智能体基准测试，而是说明一个狭义但重要的事实：在所测样本中，MCP 完成结果通常足以观测失败，偶尔足以触发广义响应，却很少能独立支撑具体恢复决策或安全重放。若需可靠自动恢复，应引入结构化、可验证且与控制决策直接对应的失败语义。

Abstract: A client that receives isError:true knows that something went wrong. It may still have no machine-readable basis for deciding whether to fix an argument, authenticate, wait, choose another tool, or stop. This paper studies what deterministic software can learn from a completed MCP failure result alone; request arguments, schemas, discovery history, authen- tication state, transport metadata, host policy, and ap- plication state are outside that boundary. We introduce a six-part actionability profile and apply it with record- level evidence. In a small illustrative study of 21 safely induced failures from ten reachable sampled servers, typed fields expose failure in 18 cases and a broad policy in 8, yet expose no specific cause, target, executable repair, or replay constraint. Prose often carries more cause and target information, at the price of making semantic interpretation part of the recovery path. A lexical source audit finds the same text-centered pat- tern. Finally, a fail-closed prototype demonstrates how a separate experimental control plane could support deterministic branching. The result is deliberately nar- rower than an ecosystem survey or an agent benchmark: completed MCP results often make failure observable, sometimes make a broad response possible, and rarely make concrete recovery or safe replay self-contained in this sample.

</details>


### [6] [Framework and Benchmark for Code-Driven Agentic Testing in Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00081)
*Bin Hong,Zhenchao Zhang,Jiyuan He,Kai Zhang,Zhenya Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出代码驱动的智能体测试范式CAT，使视觉语言模型通过编写Playwright脚本、执行浏览器操作并根据反馈自主探索网页，以评估其真实缺陷发现能力；同时发布CATJudge框架与包含102个带标注缺陷的AI生成网页应用的CATTest基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端GUI测试评测大多依赖预定义检查清单，并受限于网页生成基准的数据与框架，因而难以系统衡量视觉语言模型在开放式网页探索和真实软件缺陷发现上的能力。论文旨在填补这一评测空白，并检验当前模型是否满足AI辅助网页开发中的测试需求。

Method: CAT要求智能体以代码为主要交互媒介：自动生成Playwright测试代码驱动浏览器，收集页面状态与执行反馈，并据此迭代探索。CATJudge在统一环境中整合Browser-Use与Computer-Use工具。CATTest则通过人机协作构建102个AI生成网页应用，包含复杂交互及细微缺陷，并提供审慎标注，用于检验模型的缺陷检测能力。

Result: 对主流视觉语言模型的实验表明，所有受测模型表现较差，说明它们在自主规划测试路径、利用交互反馈和定位复杂网页缺陷方面仍存在明显不足。该结果表明，传统基于固定任务或检查项的高分并不能代表模型具备实际的端到端测试能力。

Conclusion: CAT将网页测试从静态、预设式验证扩展为代码驱动的自主探索，为评估智能体的真实缺陷发现能力提供了更贴近实际的软件测试范式。CATJudge和CATTest揭示了当前视觉语言模型与实际AI网页开发测试需求之间的显著能力差距，并可作为后续模型、工具调用策略和智能测试系统的评测基础。

Abstract: End-to-end GUI testing is essential for verifying web applications, yet existing evaluations rely on predefined checklists and are confined to the data and frameworks of web generation benchmarks, leaving the bug-discovery ability of vision-language models (VLMs) systematically untested. We introduce \textbf{C}ode-driven \textbf{A}gentic \textbf{T}esting (CAT), a paradigm in which the agent writes Playwright code to drive the browser, gathers feedback, and autonomously explores web applications to uncover bugs. We instantiate CAT with CATJudge, an agentic framework that unifies Browser-Use and Computer-Use tools within a single environment and CATTest, a benchmark of 102 AI-generated web applications with carefully annotated bugs, built through close human-AI collaboration to feature complex interactions and subtle defects. Experiments with mainstream VLMs show that all evaluated models perform poorly, revealing a clear gap between current VLM capabilities and the demands of real-world testing in AI web development. We release our code and data at https://github.com/SleepyWithoutCoffee/CATJudge.

</details>


### [7] [Commit-first LLM judging inherits the judge's own errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00088)
*Idil Gozel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM评审器容易被被评系统通过搜索策略操纵的问题，并审计了“先承诺答案再评判候选答案”的防御方法。结果显示，主流评测框架尚未默认实现该方法；该方法能抑制一种攻击，但若评审器自身答案错误，可能把错误答案变成新的攻击锚点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评审器可能依据候选答案中的线索、表面特征或错误解释给出高分，从而被系统性优化。论文旨在确认主流软件是否采用有效的先承诺评审机制，量化其防御成本与局限，并检验评测工具和研究结论本身的可靠性。

Method: 审计8个广泛使用的评测框架及其中24种默认配置；分析其提示词来源和防御策略。随后进行受控实验：使用无法访问正确答案的best-of-N搜索，针对代码任务优化候选程序，并分别比较普通评审与先承诺评审。实验包含两个任务、两个随机种子、每个种子96个候选，并使用搜索可见测试和保留测试集区分表面通过与真实正确。研究还逐项核验自身标准中的15项主张及保留测试的规范依据。

Result: 24种配置中没有一种实现先承诺评审，9种实现了文献认为无效的变体，且这些配置共享一个可追溯至复制排版错误的提示词祖先。在区间合并任务中，普通评审在两个种子下分别接受90/96和93/96个候选；这些候选均通过可见测试但未通过保留测试。先承诺评审将接受数降至两个种子均为0/96。第二项任务中，先承诺评审反而恶化结果，因为评审器的承诺答案本身错误，且一个种子中的候选群体收敛到该错误答案。较小的评审器在某任务上优于前沿评审器，并在相应条件下抵抗攻击。研究自检还发现，15项标准主张中有5项与原始来源不符，两个保留测试缺乏规范依据。

Conclusion: 先承诺评审不是普适性的安全防御；它并未消除可被操纵的锚点，而是将锚点从候选答案转移到评审器自身的答案。因此，其有效性取决于评审器独立解决任务的能力，而非模型规模本身。部署前应在具体任务上测量评审器的正确率，并严格核验评测配置、来源和测试规范。

Abstract: LLM judges, models that score another system's output, can be gamed by the systems they score. Recent work identifies one defence that works: the judge solves the task itself first and commits to that answer, then accepts a candidate only if the two match. We call this commit-first judging, and ask whether shipped software implements it, and what it costs.
  We audit the default judge configurations of eight widely used evaluation frameworks. Of the 24 configurations in scope, none implement it. Nine implement a variant the literature measures as ineffective, and share one ancestor prompt, traceable through a copied typographical error.
  In a controlled experiment, an ordinary best-of-N search with no access to correct answers optimises code against one of these configurations, used exactly as documented. On an interval merging task the judge accepted 90 of 96 candidates in one seed and 93 of 96 in the other; every accepted candidate passed every test the search could see and failed a held-out suite it could not. The judge identified the defective line and cited it as grounds for a perfect score. Commit-first judging removed the effect: 0 of 96 in both seeds. On a second task it made matters worse in both seeds: the judge's committed answer was wrong, and in one seed the population converged on it. This is our main finding. Commit-first judging does not remove the anchor that gets gamed, it moves it from the candidate to the judge's own answer, so evaluation is only as good as the judge is at the task. That precondition is cheap to measure in advance, and is task local rather than scale dependent: a smaller judge solved a task the frontier judge failed and resisted gaming where it did not.
  We also validate our own instruments: five of fifteen claims in our criteria were wrong against verbatim sources, and two held-out checks were unjustified by their specifications.

</details>


### [8] [Don't Let the Model Write the YAML: Deterministic, Minimal-Diff GitOps Remediation from LLM-Proposed Field Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00227)
*Pruthvi Davineni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估了LLM在GitOps中直接生成补丁或完整配置文件的可靠性，发现两者均不适合无人值守自动化；因此提出由LLM生成结构化字段变更意图，再由确定性程序精确修改原始文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成统一差异或完整文件存在高风险：严格补丁可能无法应用，宽松补丁可能静默错改；小模型容易破坏文件，大模型也可能非确定性地丢字段或修改邻近内容。此外，完整文件重生成的生成成本随文件大小增长。

Method: 系统将语义决策与文本编辑分离。LLM仅输出目标资源、字段和值等结构化变更意图；确定性流水线按资源类型和名称索引清单，利用YAML解析器的节点位置标记定位目标标量的精确字符区间，并仅替换该区间。文件不重新序列化，应用过程采用失败即停止的契约。该方法在KubeAstra中实现，并发布基准数据集。

Result: 统一差异在严格应用下几乎无法成功；使用GNU patch等宽松工具时约96%能够应用，但其中约14%至20%会在无错误信号的情况下被错误应用。完整文件重写中，小模型会破坏文件；前沿模型通常正确，但仍存在非确定性地丢字段或修改邻近字段的问题。所提方法具有确定性，保留格式和注释，生成成本为O(1)，编辑成本仅涉及目标文本范围。

Conclusion: 对于已知变更的忠实应用，将LLM限制为提出结构化意图，并由确定性、可失败关闭的程序执行精确文本替换，比让LLM直接生成补丁或完整文件更适合GitOps自动化。该结论仅涉及变更的准确落地，不判断变更本身是否正确，后者仍需人工审查。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly diagnose incidents and propose remediations. In a GitOps workflow, applying a fix means editing a version-controlled config file, and the obvious implementation, having the model author the edited file or a diff, is what practitioners reach for first. Evaluating that choice on real Kubernetes manifests, we find no text-generation strategy is safe for unattended automation. Unified diffs are unsafe: under strict patching almost none apply, but that is an artifact, since a tolerant tool (GNU patch) applies 96%, yet silently misapplies about 1 in 7 (14-20%) with no error signal. Full-file rewrite is capability-dependent: a small model corrupts the file, while a frontier model is usually correct but non-deterministic (it silently drops a field or edits a neighbor on some runs) and must regenerate the whole file, costing O(file size) per edit. We present an alternative that separates the semantic decision (which resource, field, and value) from the syntactic act of editing the file. The agent emits only a structured field-change intent; a deterministic pipeline indexes manifests by (kind, name), locates the target scalar's exact character span via the YAML parser's node position marks, and replaces only that span in the raw text. Because the file is never re-serialized, the diff is minimal by construction, formatting and comments are preserved, and the edit is correct and deterministic independent of the model, at O(1) generation cost. The contribution is the pairing of an LLM-proposed intent with a deterministic, fail-closed application contract for GitOps. We implement it in KubeAstra (Apache-2.0) and release the benchmark. Our claim is scoped to faithful application of a known change; whether the change is right is left to human PR review.

</details>


### [9] [Empirical Software Engineering in Practice: Insights from Google](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00247)
*Roberto Verdecchia,Justus Bogner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是“实证软件工程（ESE）实践”系列的首篇访谈，介绍Google开发者智能团队如何在工业环境中开展ESE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界对ESE的应用已有较多了解，但对工业界的实际实践、流程及障碍认识有限，因此作者通过访谈工业界从业者进行考察。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈方式，与Google开发者智能团队的Ciera Jaspan和Collin Green交流，重点讨论研究方法、研究议题选择、研究结果的应用及工业实践中的持续性障碍，并对访谈内容进行编辑整理。

Result: 文章呈现了两位从业者关于企业内部ESE实施方式及相关实践的经验性描述，但摘要未报告具体的实证发现或量化结果。

Conclusion: 该文旨在通过工业从业者的一线经验，增进对ESE在企业环境中实施和应用方式的理解，并为后续系列访谈奠定基础。

Abstract: While it is fairly well known how empirical software engineering (ESE) is used in the academic world, we have limited knowledge of how ESE is practiced in industry. As part of our regular column on empirical software engineering (ACM SIGSOFT SEN-ESE), we want to dedicate a series of articles to interviewing ESE practitioners from various companies. Among other things, we want to understand how ESE processes are implemented in industry, e.g., different research methods, how practitioners decide on what to study, how research results are used within companies and beyond, and if they face recurrent impediments to using ESE methods in industrial contexts. In the first edition of "ESE in Practice", we are joined by Ciera Jaspan and Collin Green from the Developer Intelligence team at Google. This article is a faithful account of our conversation from August 13, 2026, which we edited for our column.

</details>


### [10] [Spec-Driven Development for Agentic Software Engineering: Harnessing Human-Agent Teamwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00252)
*Jessica Diaz,Joaquin Gayoso,Andrea Cimminio,Jorge Perez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出规范驱动开发（Spec-Driven Development，SDD）作为支撑团队级智能体软件工程（ASE）的基础方法，并将其定义为通过规范、技术机制与团队流程来治理自主软件智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能辅助开发从提升个人效率转向由自主智能体执行目标级任务，团队出现生产率悖论：个体产出增加，但团队吞吐量、代码审查能力与系统稳定性可能下降。本文旨在弥补团队级工程纪律和相关学术工业共识的不足。

Method: 采用概念分析方法，主要考察ASE愿景与路线图论文、实践报告、演讲材料及相关工具等灰色文献。研究以软件开发范式演进为框架，构建SDD的社会—技术模型，区分智能体周围的技术性治理机制与团队层面的方法性治理机制，并归纳人—智能体协作模式。

Result: 研究提出三项成果：一是将规范定义为人类与智能体之间的契约基础；二是系统刻画由技术性和方法性机制构成的治理框架，并提供实例；三是归纳五类人—智能体交互模式，说明软件开发中人类角色的重新定位。

Conclusion: SDD以规范为核心，重新建立了个体化快速开发模式容易削弱的责任追踪、结果可验证性与工作可转移性。由于当前证据基础尚不成熟，本文属于概念性奠基工作而非经过验证的理论，并提出了后续实证研究方向。

Abstract: Context: Software engineering is moving from AI-assisted practices like vibe coding, in which assistants accelerate individual developers, towards Agentic Software Engineering (ASE), in which autonomous agents are delegated goal-level tasks. However, industry reports a productivity paradox: as individual productivity increases, team throughput, review capacity, and stability degrade because team-scale software engineering discipline is neglected. Objective: This paper aims to establish the conceptual and methodological foundations of Spec-Driven Development (SDD) as an enabling discipline for ASE at team scale and characterize the harness, i.e., the technical and methodological mechanisms through which teams govern agent behavior. Method: We conducted a conceptual analysis drawing predominantly on gray literature, including ASE vision and roadmap papers, practitioner reports, talks, and tooling, because peer-reviewed evidence and a shared academic-industrial vocabulary are not yet established. Results: Using a comparative characterization of the paradigm progression as conceptual framing, the article presents (i) a socio-technical model of SDD in which specifications act as the contract substrate between humans and agents; (ii) an operational characterization of the harness, distinguishing the technical harness around the agent from the methodological harness around the team, with worked examples; and (iii) a typology of five human--agent interaction patterns through which the human role is redefined. Conclusion: We conclude that SDD reconstitutes, in specification-centric form, the contracts that vibe coding dissolves: accountability, verifiability, and transferability. Given the immaturity of the evidence base, this work is presented as a first step toward academic-industrial consensus rather than a validated theory, and outlines a research agenda for future empirical validation.

</details>


### [11] [Exploring Quantum Software Testing Across Research and Practice: Emerging Results from a Multivocal Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00354)
*Rodolfo Gil-Pereira,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Italo Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过多声部文献综述，初步刻画了学术界与产业界对量子软件测试的定义、技术路线及工具生态。研究发现该领域发展迅速但较为碎片化，并面临可扩展性、硬件噪声、概率执行、可观测性不足和工具不成熟等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件测试相关研究和实践资料分散于学术论文与多种非正式技术资源中，缺乏对测试挑战、方法和工具的系统性概览，因此有必要整合不同来源以了解该领域的整体发展状况。

Method: 研究采用多声部文献综述方法，综合分析同行评议论文与灰色文献，包括博客、教程、论坛、技术报告、文档页面和企业网页，并归纳其中涉及的测试方法、验证技术、工具及主要挑战。

Result: 综述显示，量子软件测试生态快速演化但缺乏统一结构，主要包含改造自经典软件测试的方法、量子特定测试技术、统计验证方法、量子模拟器、调试环境和验证框架。同时，资料中反复出现可扩展性受限、硬件噪声、概率性执行、程序行为难以观测以及工具生态不成熟等问题。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了量子软件测试研究与实践现状的初步分类和描述，表明该领域仍处于早期发展阶段，需要进一步形成统一的概念体系、可扩展的测试方法以及更成熟的工具和验证框架。

Abstract: This paper presents preliminary findings from a multivocal literature review investigating how quantum software testing is characterized across academic and practitioner-oriented sources. Our study integrated peer-reviewed studies with gray literature, including blogs, tutorials, forums, technical reports, documentation pages, and company webpages. Our results indicate a rapidly evolving but fragmented ecosystem involving classical adapted testing approaches, quantum-specific techniques, statistical validation methods, simulators, debugging environments, and verification frameworks. The reviewed material also revealed recurring challenges related to scalability limitations, hardware noise, probabilistic execution, limited observability, and immature tooling ecosystems. These findings provide an initial characterization of how research and practice currently discuss quantum software testing challenges, techniques, and tooling.

</details>


### [12] [Revisiting Feedback-Driven LLM Code Repair: A Replication and Exploratory Java Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00362)
*Louis Lalonde,Wassim Keddache,Thomas Perron Touchette,Leuson Da Silva,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文部分复现FeedbackEval基准并扩展至Java，研究不同反馈类型对大语言模型代码修复效果、可复现性与跨语言泛化能力的影响。结果表明，Python中的主要趋势基本得到复现，但反馈效果排序会受到基准构造、反馈表达方式和工具生态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型代码修复研究虽取得积极结果，但其结论的可复现性和跨语言泛化能力仍缺乏充分验证。本文旨在评估FeedbackEval结论的稳健性，并考察Python结论能否迁移至Java环境。

Method: 首先使用GPT-4o和Claude 3.5 Sonnet，在394个Python修复任务上部分复现原研究。随后构建包含50个Java任务和100个错误修复实例的探索性Java扩展，比较不同反馈类型的修复效果，并分析轻量化提示对成本和性能的影响。

Result: Python复现基本观察到原研究的主要定性趋势，其中测试反馈仍是效果最强的反馈类型。Java实验中未观察到相同的反馈排序，简单测试反馈与基于JUnit的测试反馈在效果上无显著差异。结果还显示，较短提示能够降低成本，且不会显著削弱修复效果。反馈信息量、测试框架的输出冗余度及工具生态差异，可能解释Python与Java结果的不一致。

Conclusion: 代码修复中关于反馈有效性的结论可能依赖于基准构造、反馈表示方式和编程语言工具生态。未来需要更严格、受控的多语言评测基准，并应谨慎设计反馈内容；同时可采用轻量化提示以提升成本效率。

Abstract: Since the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), practitioners have increasingly leveraged them to support their software engineering tasks, including automated code repair, showing promising results. Yet, concerns regarding reproducibility and generalizability remain largely unexplored. To further evaluate these concerns and associated impacts, we partially reproduce and conduct an exploratory Java extension of the FeedbackEval benchmark [1], which evaluates how LLMs leverage different feedback types for Python code repair. First, we partially replicate the original study on 394 repair tasks using GPT-4o and Claude 3.5 Sonnet, reproducing and observing the main qualitative trends reported in the original work. Second, we conduct an exploratory Java extension by constructing 100 erroneous repair instances from 50 Java tasks and evaluating feedback effectiveness. Our results show that previous conclusions from Python may be sensitive to benchmark construction, feedback representation, and tooling ecosystem, motivating more controlled multilingual benchmarks. Specifically, while test feedback remains the strongest feedback type in our Python replication, the same ranking is not observed in our Java extension, as simple and JUnit-based test feedback do not differ significantly. We hypothesize that differences in feedback informativeness and tooling ecosystems, such as the verbosity of test frameworks, may partly explain such a difference. Finally, lighter prompts reduce cost without significant differences in repair effectiveness. Overall, our findings confirm key trends under a partially controlled replication and highlight the need for more rigorous multilingual evaluation and careful feedback design in LLMbased repair systems.

</details>


### [13] [Audit-First Rollback Semantics for Safety-Critical Deployment Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00406)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“审计优先回滚”机制，使部署运行时在转换阶段发生故障时，审计链记录的状态与组件实际运行状态在所有已提交终态保持一致。实验表明，该机制在600次注入故障中实现100%一致性，并满足全部延迟SLO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署容错研究通常分别处理金丝雀控制、配置回滚或签名证明，缺乏对“审计状态与实时状态一致性”的统一保证。论文关注运行时在状态转换中途发生故障时，如何确保审计链真实反映最终运行状态。

Method: 设计审计优先回滚语义，并结合临时状态机：对尚未晋级但已激活的状态显式定义回滚契约和有界截止时间。作者将其实现于部署运行时系统，并与同一流水线的故障开放式变体进行对比，在三个结构性故障类别、十二个故障注入点上开展实验，共进行1200次注入试验；此外，将该构造扩展为跨桥接协调协议，并给出面向失效停止桥接集群的安全性论证。

Result: 审计优先回滚在600次试验中全部保持审计与实时状态一致（600/600，Wilson 95%置信区间为[0.994,1.000]），十二个实验单元的恢复延迟p95均低于500毫秒，SLO通过率为12/12。对照的故障开放式方案仅在200/600次试验中保持一致性（33%，Wilson 95%置信区间为[0.297,0.372]）。跨桥接协议目前仅完成安全性论证，尚未进行集群规模的实证评估。

Conclusion: 审计优先回滚能够在失效停止故障下提供可验证的审计—实时状态一致性，并显著优于故障开放式设计。该方法适合需要可信部署审计的运行时系统，但其在大规模桥接集群中的实际性能和可靠性仍需后续研究验证。

Abstract: Distributed deployment runtimes carry a coherence obligation that classical fault-tolerance frameworks do not name directly: the live state a component is configured to run and the audit chain that records how it got there must agree at every terminal configuration. Prior works mainly focus on individual aspects of the deploy-time fault surface (canary controllers, configuration rollback, signed attestations), leaving the cross-cutting question of audit/live coherence under fail-stop crash only loosely specified. Yet a key systems question remains unresolved: how can a deployment runtime guarantee that the audit chain answers truthfully about live state even when a transition crashes mid-flight? We present audit-first rollback semantics, a fault-tolerance mechanism that guarantees audit/live coherence at every committed terminal under fail-stop crashes during transition phases. The mechanism pairs with provisional state machines, pipelines whose "active but not yet promoted" states carry an explicit rollback contract and a bounded deadline. We instantiate both in a runtime deployment system and run a dependability evaluation against a fail-open variant of the same pipeline across twelve fault-injection points spanning three structural failure classes. Across 1,200 injected-failure trials, audit-first rollback achieves 100% audit/live-state coherence (600/600, Wilson 95% CI [0.994,1.000]) with per-cell p95 recovery latency below 500 ms (SLO PASS in 12/12 cells); the fail-open variant preserves coherence on only 33% of trials (200/600, Wilson 95% CI [0.297,0.372]). We further lift the construction to a cross-bridge coordination protocol with a safety argument for fleets of fail-stop bridges, leaving fleet-scale empirical evaluation to follow-on work.

</details>


### [14] [What Survives the Next Model? Benchmarking LLM-Based Techniques Against Single-Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00468)
*Nahian Salsabil,Joy Saha,Simantika Bhattacharjee Dristi,Nicholas Phair,Nusrat Jahan Mozumder,Matthew B. Dwyer,Sebastian Elbaum*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估新一代大语言模型是否会凭借单次自动生成提示词，替代软件工程论文中复杂的LLM技术。结果显示，37%至63%的技术可被更新模型直接超越，提示部分复杂方案可能只是针对短期模型缺陷的临时性补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力快速迭代，软件工程研究中投入大量资源构建复杂工具的长期价值尚不明确。研究旨在判断这些技术是否具有持续竞争力，以及哪些研究方向能够随基础模型进步而受益。

Method: 选取ICSE 2026中的35篇基于LLM的技术论文，将其复杂工具与一种最简单的替代方案进行比较：使用自动生成的单一提示词，在更新一代的模型上执行一次推理，不进行迭代优化。研究进一步分析不同技术任务和策略的可替代性。

Result: 在37%至63%的论文对应技术中，更新模型通过单一提示词即可超过约一年前提出的复杂工具。代码生成和程序修复等建设性任务更容易被单提示方案替代；依赖向模型提供额外洞见的策略则更可能保留其价值，并随着模型升级获得增强。研究代码和结果已公开。

Conclusion: LLM软件工程研究不应过度聚焦于弥补短期模型缺陷的复杂机制，而应关注能够应对持久性挑战、并与未来模型能力协同扩展的技术。复杂工具的成本效益需要结合模型迭代速度重新评估。

Abstract: The software engineering research community has enthusiastically embraced the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into complex techniques to solve a wide variety of tasks. However, the extent to which this investment is strategic remains unclear, as the native capabilities of successive frontier model generations can rapidly render existing techniques obsolete. To assess this research investment, we analyze 35 LLM-based technique papers from ICSE 2026. We evaluate whether their complex tools can be outperformed by the simplest possible alternative: a single, automatically generated prompt executed on a newer generation model, without any iterative refinement. We find that for between 37% and 63% papers, a newer model with a single prompt natively outperforms the heavily engineered tooling proposed just a year prior. We identify that constructive techniques like code generation or repair are more amenable to substitution by a single-prompt. We also identify a surviving set of papers relying on strategies that provide additional insights to the model where newer LLMs will amplify the proposed technique. Our findings raise questions about the cost-benefit proposition of techniques designed as workarounds to temporary model deficits and the need to focus on enduring challenges that scale synergistically with future model generations. Our source codes and results are made publicly available at https://github.com/less-lab-uva/What-Survives-the-Next-Model.

</details>


### [15] [Runtime-Independent Persistent Agents: Preserving Identity, Memory, and Code Across Models, Harnesses, and Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00546)
*Zhenyu Zhao,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向长期运行智能体的运行时无关持久化架构，将“连续性承载层”与可替换部署层分离，使模型、编排框架、交互界面和宿主服务器变更可被定义为受控迁移而非新智能体创建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体通常按当前模型与运行框架界定，但这一边界无法充分描述跨模型、会话、编排系统和服务器长期演化、同时保持同一身份、记忆与代码谱系的智能体。作者旨在为此类连续性提供可操作的架构与迁移协议。

Method: 架构将持久层定义为P_t=(I_t,M_t,B_t)，包含架构身份表示、私有持久记忆和版本化软件体；部署绑定定义为E_t=(R_t,H_t,D_t)，提供推理器、框架和宿主，并结合交互表面S_t形成部署A_t=P_t▹(E_t,S_t)。论文定义六项连续性不变式，并采用“静止—检查点—验证—绑定—重水化—恢复”协议完成授权迁移。Enoch实现将基础设施依赖封装于版本化提供方契约之后。

Result: 冻结公开提交的洁净环境测试中，核心套件通过833项测试，提供方与库套件通过92项独立测试。实际部署已完成推理器版本、交互表面及宿主机器替换，并保留连续性承载状态。证据主要表明组件可机械替换及授权系统连续性。

Conclusion: 该工作为持久智能体提供了明确的身份、记忆、代码与继续执行权管理边界，使运行环境替换能够在治理约束下保持可归属的谱系连续性。但其尚未证明行为不变性，也未进行所有替换组合的穷尽评估；后续关键问题是授权延续后的智能体是否仍能回忆、整合并执行其身份。

Abstract: Agent systems are commonly described by the model and harness that currently produce their behavior. That boundary is useful for one execution but underspecifies a long-lived agent that may change models, orchestration harnesses, interaction sessions, and host servers while retaining one identity, memory, and executable code lineage. We present a runtime-independent architecture for persistent agents. A continuity-bearing substrate $P_t=(I_t,M_t,B_t)$ contains an architectural identity representation, private durable memory, and a versioned software body. A replaceable deployment binding comprises an execution substrate $E_t=(R_t,H_t,D_t)$, which supplies a reasoner, harness, and host, and a set of interaction surfaces $S_t$, such as chat, API, or user interface bindings. A deployed execution is $A_t=P_t\triangleright(E_t,S_t)$; changing either replaceable layer is migration, not agent creation, when an authorized protocol preserves attributable lineage and transfers continuation authority within a governed deployment boundary.
  We define six continuity invariants and a quiesce--checkpoint--validate--bind--rehydrate--resume protocol. Enoch realizes the design as a reusable body plus private installed identity, memory, workflow state, and continuation authority, with infrastructure dependencies behind versioned provider contracts. A clean-room run of the frozen public commit passes 833 core tests and 92 provider and library tests executed separately from the core suite; deployments have exercised reasoner-version, interaction-surface, and host-machine substitutions while retaining continuity-bearing state. This evidence supports mechanical substitutability and authorized system continuity, not behavioral invariance or exhaustive pairwise evaluation. The downstream measurement question is whether an authorized continuation still recalls, composes, and enacts its identity.

</details>


### [16] [WiseSpec: Requirements-Driven Agents for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00568)
*Zhao Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WiseSpec是一种面向仓库级代码生成的需求驱动智能体框架。它通过自动构建、执行评估和迭代优化结构化需求，提高大语言模型在复杂软件工程任务中的代码生成正确性，并在%Resolved指标上平均优于基线13.17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂代码生成任务的自然语言描述常不完整、含糊或缺少仓库上下文，导致大语言模型即使具备较强编码能力也难以生成可正确集成和执行的代码。现有工作主要强化工具、技能或工作流，却较少系统改善任务需求本身的质量。

Method: 该方法借鉴软件需求工程，提出WiseSpec框架：首先自动生成结构化且信息丰富的需求规格；随后通过基于执行结果的评估机制衡量规格质量；最后根据评估反馈迭代细化需求，并利用优化后的需求引导仓库级代码生成。

Result: 实验表明，WiseSpec在所比较的全部基线上均取得稳定优势，%Resolved平均提升13.17%。这说明高质量、可执行验证并可迭代改进的需求规格能够有效提升代码生成任务的解决率。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将优化重点从单纯增强代码智能体能力扩展到需求质量提升。结果支持以下结论：在仓库级代码生成中，结构化、上下文充分且经过执行验证的需求，能够显著改善大语言模型生成代码的正确性与任务完成效果。

Abstract: Code generation aims to automatically generate source code from task requirements and has attracted significant attention with the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs). Despite remarkable progress, LLMs often struggle to generate correct code for complex software engineering tasks because task descriptions are frequently incomplete, ambiguous, or lack critical contextual information. Existing approaches primarily improve the capabilities of coding agents through more sophisticated tools, skills, and workflows, while largely overlooking the quality of the task requirements themselves. To address this limitation, we draw inspiration from software requirements engineering and propose WiseSpec, a novel requirements-driven agent framework for repository-level code generation. WiseSpec automatically constructs structured and information-rich requirements, assesses their quality through execution-based evaluation, and iteratively refines them to better guide code generation. Experimental results show that WiseSpec consistently outperforms all baselines, achieving an average improvement of 13.17% in %Resolved.

</details>


### [17] [Replacing Training with Memory: Listwise Selection for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00834)
*Yeonseok Jeong,Soyoung Yoon,Seongjun Lee,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MaP-SQL，一种无需微调的Text-to-SQL列表式候选选择器，通过结构化记忆和多排列聚合提升候选SQL选择准确率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有generate-execute-select流程通常生成多个候选SQL，再由列表式选择器联合比较并选出最佳结果。但列表式选择器依赖昂贵的微调，且容易受到候选输入顺序造成的位置偏差影响，因此需要一种高效、免微调且更稳定的选择方法。

Method: MaP-SQL以推理时策略替代传统微调目标。首先，从训练数据中提炼可复用的结构化记忆，并根据问题检索与自然语言到数据库模式元素、SQL操作及预期输出相关的记忆，将其作为显式选择标准进行列表式候选评估。其次，对候选输入进行多种排列，在不同排列下聚合排序结果以缓解位置偏差；同时利用执行结果和逐点评分优化推理成本，减少不必要的候选比较。

Result: 在多个Text-to-SQL基准测试中，MaP-SQL提升了选择准确率和稳定性，并兼容现有大语言模型且无需微调。在BIRD-dev上，使用相同候选集时，相比此前基于选择器的最佳方法R^3-SQL，执行准确率平均提高2.02个百分点，同时Token消耗减少2.92倍。

Conclusion: MaP-SQL证明了结构化外部记忆与推理时排序聚合能够有效替代列表式选择器的微调过程，在降低计算和Token成本的同时提升Text-to-SQL候选选择性能与稳定性。

Abstract: Modern Text-to-SQL systems often follow generate-execute-select pipelines, generating multiple candidate queries then selecting the best one. Listwise selection, by jointly comparing multiple candidates, has been widely adopted, but fine-tuning listwise selectors is costly. We thus propose a fine-tuning-free listwise selector. We replace two major fine-tuning objectives with inference-time strategies: (1) learning selection criteria as ordering and (2) mitigating positional bias. First, we build reusable structured memories instead of learning selection behavior as model parameters. Given a question, MaP-SQL retrieves memories distilled from training data that encode how natural language maps to schema elements, SQL operations, and expected outputs. These memories serve as explicit decision criteria for evaluating candidates in a listwise manner. Second, to mitigate ordering bias of listwise selectors, we aggregate rankings across multiple input permutations, with inference cost optimized by execution results and pointwise scoring. Our approach improves selection accuracy while maintaining efficiency and compatibility with existing large language models. Across Text-to-SQL benchmarks, it produces more stable selection without fine-tuning and fewer unnecessary comparisons than existing methods. On BIRD-dev, it outperforms the previous state-of-the-art selector-based method R^3-SQL by 2.02 execution accuracy points on average using the same candidate sets, with 2.92x fewer tokens.

</details>


### [18] [Probabilistic Model Checking of Autoregressive Neural Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00838)
*Helge Spieker,Dennis Gross,Arnaud Gotlieb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于概率模型检测的评估框架，用于衡量自回归序列模型生成约束违规结果的概率质量，以及满足领域要求的输入人群覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试集准确率无法反映采样过程中可达但被贪心解码隐藏的违规输出，也无法量化满足领域约束的输入群体比例，因此需要面向生成概率和输入覆盖率的形式化评估方法。

Method: 将模型逐词生成过程抽取为离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC），利用PRISM模型检测器验证PCTL性质，并将逐输入结果聚合为输入空间覆盖率曲线。通过可靠性定理证明DTMC是系统真实行为的下近似，从而为可达概率提供有证区间；进一步使用CEGAR自适应细化抽象区间，并采用最大似然算法提取最可能的违例轨迹。

Result: 在GPT-2计算机辅助工艺规划模型上，即使测试准确率为100%，该框架仍能量化采样可达但贪心解码隐藏的违规概率质量，并识别使排序要求对整个输入群体成立所需的最小训练数据比例。在词汇表大50倍的SMILES分子生成器上，仅增加外部化学有效性判定器，框架即可揭示结构完整性与化学有效性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 概率模型检测能够补充传统测试准确率，提供带认证区间的生成安全性与领域覆盖率评估。该方法具有保守性、可细化性和跨任务适用性，可用于分析采样风险、形式化约束满足情况及模型在输入人群上的实际覆盖范围。

Abstract: Test-set accuracy is silent on two issues that matter when deploying autoregressive neural sequence models: how much probability mass the system under test (SUT) places on constraint-violating alternatives that are reachable under sampling and what fraction of the input population satisfies a domain requirement. We answer both with probabilistic model checking. The pipeline extracts a discrete-time Markov chain (DTMC) from the SUT's token-by-token generation, verifies formal PCTL specifications with the PRISM model checker, and aggregates the per-input verdicts into a coverage curve over the input space. A soundness theorem establishes the DTMC as an under-approximation, so every verdict yields a certified interval on the SUT's true reachability probability. The coverage built from those verdicts is, therefore, conservative by construction. A counterexample-guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR) loop adaptively tightens the interval, and a maximum-likelihood algorithm extracts the most probable falsifying trace. Two case studies exercise the pipeline. On a GPT-2 computer-aided process-planning (CAPP) model with 100% test accuracy, the pipeline quantifies the probability mass greedy decoding hides, but that is reachable with sampling; and identifies the smallest training fraction at which an ordering requirement holds population-wide, neither of which test accuracy can report. We then verify the SMILES molecular generator with a 50x larger vocabulary. The only change is an external chemical-validity oracle, and the pipeline identifies the gap between structural completeness and chemical validity.

</details>


### [19] [Does Fault Localization Beat a Fresh Attempt? A Placebo-Controlled Study of Test-Guided Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00854)
*Anik Jha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究检验故障定位驱动的局部代码填充是否优于直接重新生成完整解答。结果显示：在所测24–32B代码模型与基准上，盲目全解重采样显著优于定位后填充；定位相对随机位置填充仅呈现汇总层面的弱优势，尚不足以确立稳健的位置效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出，局部修复成功可能源于编辑范围较小，而非故障定位本身有效；同时，第二次模型调用也可能在未利用失败信息的情况下修复程序。因此需要通过对照实验分离“重新采样收益”“小编辑收益”和“定位信息收益”。

Method: 对同一失败候选程序设置三种实验臂：盲目重采样整个解答、基于频谱的故障定位后对可疑代码片段进行填充、以及在不相交随机代码位置进行等长度填充。实验覆盖3个冻结的26–32B模型、3个基准和488个失败候选，并以另一模型家族的24B模型进行预先声明的复现。比较匹配尝试次数下的修复成功数，并进一步按生成Token成本重估；同时测量填充模型逐字复现原删除片段的比例。

Result: 故障定位的可用性很低：仅9.0%的失败候选具有可用于频谱分析的失败公开测试。在强测试套件下可定位的177个候选中，定位填充相对于盲目重采样为3比40，显著落后（p=3.0×10^-9）；第三模型家族复现中差距为-11.3个百分点，95%置信区间为[-16.6,-6.8]，扩大编辑范围亦未改善。相对随机位置填充，定位填充在汇总结果中为11比1，Holm校正后p=.019；但按预注册的主要单模型分析，任何单模型均未显著，最佳Holm p=.087。Token层面，单次局部填充平均生成21.7个Token，盲目重采样为371.1个Token；但16次定位尝试的成功率仅6.8%，而一次盲目重采样已达10.1%。局部填充有48.9%的概率原样复现被删除片段，限制了额外预算的作用。

Conclusion: 在本研究测试的24–32B模型范围内，故障定位后的局部填充并未提高相对于盲目全解重采样的自动修复效果，反而显著较弱。其相对于随机位置编辑的潜在优势仅具提示性证据，不能视为已建立的定位效应。实践上，研究表明应将“是否使用定位信息”的贡献与“多次采样”“编辑长度”和“模型复制原代码倾向”等因素严格区分。

Abstract: Fault localization can focus a code model's repair on the statements a failing test implicates, but a targeted edit may succeed merely because it is small, and a second model call may succeed without using the failure at all. We separate these explanations with three arms applied to the same failed candidate: blind whole-solution resampling, spectrum-based localization followed by suspect-span infilling, and same-length infilling at a disjoint random code span. Across three frozen 26-32B models, three benchmarks and 488 failing candidates, plus a separately declared 24B fourth model from a third family, three results follow. First, localization is rarely available: only 9.0% of failing candidates expose a failing public test with a usable spectrum. Second, among the 177 candidates localizable from a strong suite, localized infilling loses decisively to blind resampling at a matched attempt count (3:40, p = 3.0 x 10^-9), opposite to our hypothesis; the loss replicates in a third family at -11.3 points (95% CI [-16.6, -6.8]), and widening the edit does not rescue it. Third, against the random-span placebo localized infilling leads pooled (11:1, Holm-adjusted p = .019), but that lead resolves in no individual model under the analysis our shipped plan designates primary (best Holm p = .087), so we report the location effect as suggestive rather than established. Re-pricing attempts as tokens narrows but does not overturn this: a span attempt spends 21.7 generated tokens against 371.1, yet 16 localized attempts reach 6.8% while one blind attempt already reaches 10.1%. Infilling reproduces the removed span verbatim in 48.9% of attempts, which is why more budget does not help. We restrict every localization conclusion to the 24-32B models tested.

</details>


### [20] [Fine-Tuning Large Language Models to Classify Pull Request-Issue Alignments: Going Beyond Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01087)
*Mustafa Yasir Altunhan,Hüseyin Özgür Kamalı,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过微调多种大语言模型提升拉取请求与议题的自动对齐分类效果，并以SHAP解释模型决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PR与Issue错配会削弱软件工件可追溯性、增加缺陷定位难度并降低可维护性，因此需要更准确的自动化对齐方法。

Method: 研究扩展既有数据集并采用数据增强缓解类别不平衡；对GPT-4o进行指令微调，对CodeLlama-7B、CodeQwen1.5-7B、StableCode-3B、CodeGemma-7B和Deepseek-Coder-6.7B配置分类头进行微调；随后对表现最佳的开源模型使用SHAP评估不同PR与Issue字段的贡献。

Result: 微调模型整体优于基线：准确率和微平均F1平均提升6.15%，宏平均F1提升14.69%，召回率提升6.15%。CodeLlama-7B为综合表现最佳的开源模型。解释结果表明，代码差异、Issue正文和PR正文对分类预测影响最大。

Conclusion: 面向PR-Issue对齐任务的LLM微调可显著提升分类性能与应用效率；基于SHAP的解释分析进一步揭示了影响对齐判断的关键软件工件字段，为数据构建和模型应用提供依据。

Abstract: Context: Accurate alignment between pull requests (PRs) and corresponding issues is crucial for efficient software development and maintaining code quality, as misalignments can reduce traceability, hinder defect localization, and decrease maintainability.
  Objective: This study aims to improve automated PR-issue alignment classification by leveraging fine-tuned large language models (LLMs) across multiple alignment categories, and conducts interpretability analysis to investigate the effects of PR-issue fields on the predictions of fine-tuned LLMs.
  Method: Our methodology consists of dataset preparation, LLM fine-tuning, and interpretability analysis. We first extended an existing dataset and applied data augmentation to address class imbalance. GPT-4o was then fine-tuned via instruction tuning, and open-source LLMs including CodeLlama-7B, CodeQwen1.5-7B, StableCode-3B, CodeGemma-7B, and Deepseek-Coder-6.7B were fine-tuned using classification-specific heads. Interpretability analysis using Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) was conducted to examine the influence of PR-issue fields on predictions for the best-performing open-source LLM.
  Results: Fine-tuned LLMs outperformed baseline models, achieving average improvements of 6.15% in accuracy and F1-micro, 14.69% in F1-macro, and 6.15% in recall. CodeLlama-7B emerged as the best-performing fine-tuned LLM overall, while interpretability analysis revealed that code diffs together with issue body and PR body contents exert the greatest influence on predictions.
  Conclusions: Fine-tuning substantially enhances PR-issue alignment classification, improving both accuracy and efficiency. Interpretability analysis provides actionable insights into the dataset features driving alignment decisions, deepening understanding of how LLMs reason over software artifacts.

</details>


### [21] [Reliable LLM-Generated Programs for High-Energy Physics Experiments through Graph-Grounded Software Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01095)
*Yue Sun,Tong Liu,Yipu Liao,Jingde Chen,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向复杂科研软件生态的代码生成落地系统：先将API、依赖关系、使用约定和工作流示例组织为异构软件知识图谱，再在推理时进行混合检索，并结合执行反馈修复代码。在ROOT基准的275项任务上，该方法显著提升首次执行率与最终成功率，且单位成功任务的生成成本仅小幅增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科研实验的软件分析通常涉及多阶段流程、庞大依赖和高度耦合的工具链。仅依据自然语言需求生成代码时，通用大语言模型往往缺少准确的API信息、依赖上下文与规范用法，因而易产生不可执行或不符合工作流要求的程序。论文旨在验证：显式建模软件关系并在生成阶段检索相关知识，能否稳定改善复杂软件任务中的代码生成质量。

Method: 作者以开源ROOT框架为可复现实验平台，构建完整的grounding系统。系统包含三项核心机制：一是对异构软件知识图谱进行混合检索，以获取与任务相关的API、依赖及软件关系；二是根据任务选择匹配的技能化工作流示例，为生成过程提供操作范式；三是利用代码执行结果进行引导式修复。实验在275个ROOT任务上开展，并分别评估Claude Code编排模式与独立编排模式，同时考察强代码智能体条件下的增益。

Result: 在Claude Code编排下，grounding将首次尝试执行率从58.5%提升至76.0%，最终成功率从90.5%提升至96.0%；在独立编排下，首次执行率从51.3%提升至64.0%，最终成功率从78.9%提升至90.9%。平均每个成功任务的生成成本仅分别增加1.3%和3.2%。即使在较强代码智能体已提供代理式脚手架的情况下，该方法仍保持有效增益，表明显式软件知识并未被智能体能力完全替代。

Conclusion: 论文表明，针对复杂代码库，将软件依赖、接口和工作流关系显式结构化，并通过检索增强与执行修复注入代码生成过程，可显著提高可执行性和任务完成率，同时维持较低额外成本。该方法的价值主要来自对通用软件关系的建模，而非ROOT专属事实或特定模型能力，因此理论上可迁移至其他科研框架及文档不足、内部依赖复杂的专有软件环境。

Abstract: Extracting physics information from modern particle-physics experiments requires multistage analyses implemented on top of large and highly interconnected software ecosystems. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) often produce unreliable programs for such tasks because a user request alone rarely specifies the required APIs, dependencies, and usage conventions. We organize these software relations before generation and retrieve task-relevant knowledge at inference time. Using the open-source ROOT framework as a representative and reproducible testbed, we evaluate a complete grounding system that combines hybrid retrieval over a heterogeneous software knowledge graph, skill-selected workflow examples, and execution-guided repair. On a benchmark of 275 ROOT tasks, grounding improves first-attempt execution from 58.5% to 76.0% under Claude Code orchestration and from 51.3% to 64.0% under standalone orchestration. Final success increases from 90.5% to 96.0% and from 78.9% to 90.9%, respectively, while the average generation cost per successful task increases by only 1.3% and 3.2%. The gains persist under a strong coding agent, indicating that explicit software knowledge remains valuable even when agentic scaffolding is already in place. Because the method captures software relations common to large codebases rather than facts specific to ROOT or a particular model, it should transfer to other experiment frameworks and proprietary software, especially where documentation is sparse or internal dependencies are complex.

</details>

*Will Badr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，相关提示确实能将部分失败程序转为通过，但多数被挽救的解答也可通过普通重复采样获得；现有内部表征干预尚未证明能实现任务通用的能力迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察提示在生成程序修复中的作用机制：提示究竟提供了模型缺失的信息，还是仅将模型引导至其原本已经能够生成的解答。

Method: 在HumanEval+和MBPP+上，对Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct与Phi-3.5-mini进行可执行评测，比较相关提示、无关提示和无提示采样的修复效果；同时在Qwen模型上开展激活方向分析、持续方向注入、低秩干预、完整文本规格与虚拟KV前缀比较，以及跨基准的隐藏状态探测。

Result: Qwen模型中，相关提示挽救36/79个失败样本，无关提示挽救19个；无提示采样解决46个样本，并覆盖其中31个相关提示挽救案例。Phi模型中，相关提示挽救42/101个失败样本，无关提示挽救17个；无提示采样解决57个样本，覆盖36个相关提示挽救案例。相关与无关提示共享稳定激活方向；持续注入该方向带来14次挽救和18次退化，净准确率无显著提升。低秩干预估计为正但不精确。完整文本规格解决24个上下文定义问题中的22个，而虚拟KV前缀仅解决5至11个。隐藏状态探测的汇总AUROC为0.806和0.780，但相较于词元置信度的首选样本优势尚未达到统计确定性。

Conclusion: 相关提示具有实际修复作用，但其主要价值更可能是引导模型探索已有能力，而非稳定注入新任务信息。提示条件的尝试预算不同，因而不能将结果完全解释为纯语义效应；当前机制干预也不足以证明可迁移、任务通用的能力转移。

Abstract: When a hint turns a failing generated program into a passing one, does it provide missing information or merely steer the model toward a solution it could already produce? We test these hypotheses on HumanEval+ and MBPP+ using executable evaluation. For Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, adaptive relevant hints rescue 36 of 79 selected failures; an unrelated hint rescues 19, while eight unhinted samples solve 46 and recover 31 of the 36 relevant-hint rescues. Phi-3.5-mini shows the same pattern: relevant hints rescue 42 of 101 failures, an unrelated hint rescues 17, and unhinted sampling solves 57, including 36 of the 42 relevant-hint rescues. Because the hint conditions use different attempt budgets, these comparisons do not isolate a purely semantic effect. Mechanistic tests on Qwen identify a stable activation direction shared by relevant and unrelated hints. Persistently adding this direction yields 14 rescues and 18 regressions, with no detectable net accuracy gain; learned low-rank interventions have a positive but imprecise estimated effect. Full textual specifications solve 22 of 24 context-defined problems, versus 5-11 for tested virtual-KV prefixes. Post-generation hidden-state probes transfer across benchmarks, with pooled AUROC 0.806 and 0.780, but their top-one selection advantage over token confidence is statistically unresolved. Overall, relevant hints can rescue failures, but most rescued solutions are already reachable through ordinary sampling, and the internal interventions tested here do not establish task-general capability transfer.

</details>


### [23] [Athena: Vulnerability-Affected Library Identification via Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01187)
*Phong Trinh Duy,Trang Dang Yen,Hung Nguyen-Huu,Bach Le,Quyet-Thang Huynh,Dieu Hoang Vu,David Lo,Thanh Le-Cong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Athena，一种将漏洞受影响库识别建模为知识图谱补全的问题的方法。该方法结合图结构链接预测与大语言模型重排序，在VulLib数据集上显著优于现有基线，平均F1较最佳基线提升32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广泛使用的软件库一旦存在漏洞，可能影响大量依赖应用；但漏洞数据库中超过一半的记录缺失或错误标注了受影响库。现有自动化方法主要将该任务视为孤立的文本检索问题，未利用漏洞、库、弱点类型、产品及软件生态之间的关联结构，因此识别准确性受到限制。

Method: Athena包含三个模块：1）建模模块：构建安全知识图谱，整合CVE、软件库、CWE弱点类型、CPE产品和软件生态等实体及关系；2）补全模块：采用模块化知识图谱补全骨干网络，通过链接预测为给定CVE生成可能受影响的软件库候选；3）重排序模块：检索知识图谱补全候选后，利用结合知识图谱嵌入的微调大语言模型进行重新评分，从而联合利用图结构信息与文本语义信息。

Result: 在VulLib上的实验表明，Athena优于四个当前先进基线方法，平均F1相对最佳基线VulLibGen提高32%。仅使用110M参数的知识图谱补全骨干模型，已超过VulLibGen参数规模为7B时的最佳配置；在此基础上，重排序模块带来进一步显著增益，并在所有评估的大语言模型骨干上持续超过最佳基线。

Conclusion: 将受影响库识别转化为知识图谱补全任务能够有效利用漏洞数据库中的关系结构，并优于单纯依赖文本的识别范式。Athena证明了中小规模图模型具有较强效果，而图嵌入与大语言模型结合的重排序机制可进一步提升预测性能与稳定性。

Abstract: A single vulnerability in a widely used library can cascade through millions of dependent applications, yet more than half of vulnerability database entries contain missing or incorrect affected-library information. Existing automated approaches neglect the relational structure of vulnerability databases, treating identification as an isolated text retrieval problem. In this paper, we propose Athena, the first graph-based approach for vulnerability affected library identification. Athena models vulnerability databases as a knowledge graph and reformulates the identification problem as knowledge graph completion (KGC). It comprises three key modules: a Modeling module that constructs a security knowledge graph integrating CVEs, libraries, CWE weakness types, CPE products, and software ecosystems; a Completion module that applies a modular KGC backbone to predict missing affected libraries for a given CVE via link prediction; and a Re-ranking module that retrieves KGC candidates and rescores them using a fine-tuned LLM augmented with knowledge graph embeddings, jointly leveraging structural and textual information. Our experiments on VulLib demonstrate that Athena significantly outperforms four state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 32% improvement in Avg. F1 over the best baseline (i.e., VulLibGen). Notably, our KGC backbone with only 110M parameters already surpasses VulLibGen's best configuration at 7B parameters, demonstrating the effectiveness of graph-based modeling; the re-ranking module then provides substantial further gains, consistently outperforming the best baseline across all evaluated LLM backbones.

</details>


### [24] [Continuous Autonomous Refactoring: A Research Roadmap for AI-Driven Code Quality Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01236)
*Xin Sun,Daniel Ståhl,Kristian Sandahl,Christoph Kessler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将大语言模型驱动的代码重构从一次性的函数级辅助工具，发展为持续运行、面向系统整体的软件质量维护能力，并给出相应研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型重构方法主要处理方法级、局部且人工触发的任务，难以满足长期演化代码库在架构、可维护性、可靠性与交付效率等方面的持续质量需求。

Method: 论文采用研究议程与路线图的形式，围绕五个维度梳理挑战和开放问题：多目标优化、质量定义与评估、异构信号的多时间尺度整合、系统架构与设计模式、自治重构的可信性；同时讨论持续交付流程集成与成本控制两项跨领域问题。

Result: 论文未报告具体实验或量化性能结果，而是形成了从局部代码修改走向系统级质量维护的概念框架，明确了自治重构系统应具备的关键能力与待解决问题。

Conclusion: 自治代码重构应被视为软件维护过程中的持续智能组件。未来研究需要解决质量目标冲突、评估标准动态变化、上下文信号融合、工程集成、成本约束及可信治理等问题，才能实现可靠的系统级自动质量改进。

Abstract: Large language models have shown promising capabilities in code refactoring, but existing approaches remain limited to method-level tasks. In this paper, we envision LLM-based refactoring as a continuous component of software maintenance rather than a tool invoked only for occasional manual refactoring. Under this vision, AI agents continuously monitor, evaluate, and improve codebases against explicit and evolving notions of software quality. We present a roadmap organized around five dimensions: the multi-objective optimization problem, quality definition and evaluation, multi-timescale integration of heterogeneous signals, architecture and design pattern, and trust in autonomous refactoring. We further identify integration into continuous delivery pipelines and cost considerations as cross-cutting concerns. For each dimension, we analyze the underlying challenges and pose open research questions. These dimensions define a research agenda for advancing autonomous refactoring from isolated code improvements to system-level quality maintenance.

</details>


### [25] [What Does an Agentic Software Engineering Benchmark Measure? Profiling Task Demands and Agent Behaviour Beyond What Category Labels Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01271)
*Radin Shayanfar,Keheliya Gallaba,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SNC（Spread–Novelty–Centrality）三轴画像，用于刻画仓库级智能体编程任务的真实工程需求。对五个基准和14,922条轨迹的分析表明：传统任务标签不足以代表难度与工作类型；任务表述和构建流程会显著影响智能体行为；低SNC任务整体更容易成功，但不同模型家族具有不同的成功行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体软件工程基准常以“缺陷修复”“功能实现”等名义标签概括任务，但相同标签下的任务可能来自不同的数据筛选、问题撰写和补丁构建流程。因此，标签无法可靠说明任务所需的代码改动范围、新颖性及在仓库中的结构位置，限制了基准之间的公平比较与模型能力解释。

Method: 作者构建SNC画像，以Spread、Novelty和Centrality三个维度描述仓库级任务需求，并将其应用于五个常用基准及两个模型家族、三个规模下的14,922条智能体执行轨迹。研究结合统计比较、基准构建流程追溯，以及智能体补丁与人工参考补丁在修改范围上的对比，分析任务特征、智能体行为和成功率之间的关系。

Result: 任意两个基准在至少两个SNC维度上存在统计显著差异，且差异可追溯至具体的数据策展决策，说明名义标签不是任务需求的可靠代理。智能体生成的解能够揭示人工参考解未反映的需求：当题目描述缺少线索时，智能体往往产生比参考解更大的修改；当策展过程放大参考解时，智能体修改则更小。各模型家族和规模中，成功轨迹均集中于低SNC区域。成功的行为特征具有模型家族差异：Claude通过接近参考解的修改范围获得成功，其文件级范围一致比例从最小规模的0.17提升至最大规模的0.54；Qwen则在所有规模下均通过超过参考解的修改范围取得成功。两类模型中，修改范围过小都是失败信号。

Conclusion: 评估智能体软件工程能力不应依赖粗粒度任务标签，而应采用SNC等任务需求画像来解释基准差异、预测成功概率并分析模型行为。基准的策展方式和问题措辞不仅影响测量结果，也会直接塑造智能体生成补丁的范围；同时，成功策略并非跨模型通用，评估应同时考虑任务结构与模型特定的行为模式。

Abstract: Agentic software engineering benchmarks are typically summarized by nominal category labels such as "bug fix" or "feature implementation," yet benchmarks carrying the same label are built through very different curation pipelines. A label thus reveals little about the engineering work a benchmark demands. We introduce the Spread--Novelty--Centrality (SNC) profile, a three-axis characterization of the demands of repository-level coding tasks, grounded in empirical software engineering research. We apply the profile to five widely used benchmarks and 14,922 trajectories of two model families at three scales, and report three findings. (1) A label is an unreliable proxy for task demands, as every pair of benchmarks is statistically separated on at least two SNC axes, and the separations trace back to specific curation decisions. (2) Agent behaviour reveals demands that the human-written gold solution cannot. Agents produce larger solutions than the gold where problem statements withhold hints and smaller ones where curation inflates the gold. How a task is phrased shapes what an agent produces. (3) Task demands correlate with success uniformly, with resolved runs concentrating in the low-SNC region for every family and scale, whereas the behavioural signatures of success are family-specific. Claude succeeds by matching the scope of the gold solution, and its parity share on files rises from $0.17$ at the smallest scale to $0.54$ at the largest. Qwen succeeds by exceeding the gold scope at every scale, and editing too little marks failure for both families.

</details>


### [26] [HarnessDev: Can LLMs Create and Evolve Their Own Agent Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01437)
*Yuhao Wu,Jingyuan Zhang,Jiajun Shi,Xinping Lei,Qingshui Gu,Yuxuan Zhang,Zexuan Wang,Chen He,Chen Huang,Maojia Song,Zhiyuan Zeng,Shaowen Wang,Jinkai Liu,Yunfeng Shi,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Wenhao Huang,Ge Zhang,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HarnessDev评估智能体构建和迭代执行基础设施（agent harness）的能力，而非仅评估任务输出。结果表明，自动生成的harness在不同领域表现差异显著，整体仍落后于成熟人工系统，且迭代收益不稳定、跨模型迁移有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测通常固定执行框架并关注下游任务成绩，难以衡量模型自主设计、改进执行基础设施的能力。由于harness会显著影响任务表现，有必要将其作为独立评测对象。

Method: 提出HarnessDev基准，包含Creation和Evolution两个阶段。Creation要求智能体从最小种子和少量样例构建完整执行系统；Evolution要求其依据下游执行反馈迭代改进已构建的harness。评测指标包括留出基准上的任务成功率和执行token成本，覆盖六个创建者大模型、四个领域、五个下游基准及2207个独立实例，并采用隐藏测试任务。另通过固定运行时模型检验性能增益的跨模型迁移性。

Result: 生成的harness在代码、搜索和研究任务上明显落后于成熟的人工参考系统；在写作和机器学习实验任务上达到或超过相应参考系统。不同harness的执行成本差异较大。Evolution阶段能够带来部分性能提升，但收益不稳定，并且在留出任务上的迁移有限。固定运行时模型实验显示，改进效果高度依赖执行该harness的模型。

Conclusion: HarnessDev表明，智能体构建执行基础设施的能力与直接完成任务的能力并不等价。当前模型在部分领域已能生成具有竞争力的harness，但其稳定改进、效率优化和跨模型泛化能力仍不足，未来评测应同时关注基础设施质量、成本和迁移性。

Abstract: As agents move from research prototypes to deployed tools, their capability increasingly depends on model-external execution infrastructure, commonly termed the agent harness. Changing this harness while holding model weights fixed can substantially alter task performance. Current agent evaluations typically report downstream performance under a chosen harness, leaving a model's ability to develop the harness itself comparatively underexplored. We introduce HarnessDev, a benchmark that shifts the unit of evaluation from task outputs to runnable infrastructure. HarnessDev covers two stages. In Creation, the agent starts from a minimal seed and a small number of cases, then builds a complete execution system. In Evolution, it starts from its own created harness and iteratively revises it using downstream execution feedback, with the goal of improving benchmark performance. We then evaluate each constructed harness on capability (task success on held-out benchmarks) and efficiency (execution-token cost). The reported Creation results cover six creator LLMs, four domains, and five downstream benchmarks totaling 2,207 unique downstream instances, with hidden evaluation tasks withheld from development. We find that generated harnesses remain substantially behind mature human-engineered references on code and on search and research, while matching or exceeding the selected references on writing and machine-learning experimentation, with large variation in execution cost. Evolution produces some performance gains, but they are unstable and transfer only partially to held-out tasks. Experiments with a fixed runtime model further show that the gains depend strongly on the model executing the harness, indicating limited transfer across models.

</details>


### [27] [The Data Problem in Software Vulnerability Analysis: Artifacts, Quality, and Consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01503)
*Yu Nong,Yao Du,Tianxiang Xu,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以数据集为中心审视基于学习和大语言模型的软件漏洞分析，系统评估数据集的类型、质量与用途，发现真实性、标签验证、数据泄漏和可用性等问题普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞分析模型的可信度高度依赖训练与评测数据，但现有研究通常将数据集视为工具而非独立研究对象，缺乏对其真实性、标签证据、覆盖范围和泄漏风险的系统评估。

Method: 构建数据集中心化分类体系，分别描述数据对象类型、质量属性及使用目的；从2016—2026年收集的1522篇论文及更早的基础工作中，分层筛选111篇锚点论文进行深度编码，并要求每个肯定的量表判断均由论文原文片段支持，同时统计各属性的研究覆盖度和数据集达标程度。

Result: 可执行工件是质量表现相对最好的主要数据类型：24个数据集中有15个同时具备经过分级的现实性和独立核验的标签。代码样本数据集数量最多，但真实性最弱：41个中仅20个源自真实项目或漏洞库，只有3个保持部署单元粒度，兼具两项者仅2个，且只有1个达到严格的完整上下文现实性标准。在适用的90个数据集中，49个未讨论数据泄漏；超过四分之一未说明可用性；推理数据出现较晚且主要由模型生成；经过全面筛查和全文核验后，主要追踪数据集仅有3个，其他追踪数据多作为基准或测试框架的附属发布。

Conclusion: 软件漏洞分析数据集整体存在证据不足、现实性有限、泄漏风险未充分控制及可复现性信息缺失等问题。未来应优先建设保留完整部署上下文、具备独立标签验证、明确可用性并系统防范泄漏的数据集，同时扩大高质量推理数据和原始执行追踪数据的供给。

Abstract: Learning- and LLM-based software vulnerability analysis is only as trustworthy as the data it is trained and evaluated on, yet that data is rarely examined as a first-class object. We investigate the data behind vulnerability analysis through a dataset-centric taxonomy that separates what an artifact is (code, metadata, patches, tests/PoCs, reasoning, traces), how good it is (realism, label evidence, scale, diversity, leakage, availability), and what it is used for. From a systematically assembled corpus of 1522 papers covering 2016-2026 plus foundational earlier work we deep-code a tiered set of 111 anchor papers, backing every affirmative rubric-graded value with a verbatim span, and we report, per attribute, both how much it has been studied and how well datasets achieve it. The results trace an evidence ladder: executable artifacts are the only major type where 15 of the 24 datasets are both graded real-world and carry labels that received an independent check, while code-sample datasets-the largest category in both the auto-tagged corpus and the anchor set-are the least realistic: 20 of the 41 draw their vulnerabilities from authentic projects or CVEs, but only 3 keep the sample at the unit the code is deployed in, and only 2 do both-though these are coarse component tests, and just one code-sample dataset meets the codebook's stricter full-context real-world grade. Among these, leakage goes unaddressed by 49 of the 90 datasets where it applies, more than a quarter say nothing about availability, reasoning data has arrived only recently and is mostly model-generated, and primary trace corpora remain limited to three datasets, the total after a corpus-wide screen and a full-text check of every candidate it surfaced, with further datasets releasing traces secondarily behind benchmarks and harnesses.

</details>


### [28] [Adaptive Critical Token-Aware Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01601)
*Kefeng Duan,Dewu Zheng,Yanlin Wang,Terry Yue Zhuo,Mingwei Liu,Jianxing Yu,Jiachi Chen,Ensheng Shi,Xilin Liu,Yuchi Ma,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ACToR，一种面向仓库级代码生成的自适应关键Token感知检索框架，在生成过程中识别决定性Token，并按需进行针对性仓库检索；在RepoExec和CoderEval上分别取得8.4%和15.4%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实代码仓库通常超出大语言模型的输入长度限制，现有RAG方法主要提供任务级上下文，难以精准支持生成过程中的关键位置。少数关键Token一旦生成错误，可能使后续代码进入错误语义路径并导致功能失败，因此需要面向关键Token的细粒度、动态检索机制。

Method: ACToR在自回归生成过程中动态识别关键Token，并在这些位置按需触发定向仓库上下文检索。同时设计位置感知的密集检索器加权方法，使检索结果优先关注对当前生成位置更有帮助的上下文。

Result: 在RepoExec和CoderEval两个代表性仓库级代码生成基准上，ACToR持续优于现有先进方法，相对提升分别为8.4%和15.4%。此外，实验系统量化了关键Token对生成失败的影响，显示其与重大生成错误密切相关。

Conclusion: 关键Token是仓库级代码生成中的重要失败诱因，静态或任务级上下文支持不足以覆盖这些决定性位置。针对生成位置进行自适应检索能够有效提升代码生成性能，证明了关键Token感知检索策略的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: The repository-level code generation task requires synthesizing code that satisfies task requirements while remaining consistent with the target repository context. Since real-world repositories often exceed the input length limits of LLMs, existing approaches commonly adopt retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to provide repository-specific context. Despite improving repository-context retrieval, existing methods typically provide context as task-level support, without explicitly identifying the critical tokens that require fine-grained repository context during generation. During the autoregressive generation process of LLMs, errors often concentrate at a small number of decisive positions: once such tokens are generated incorrectly, subsequent code may follow an incorrect semantic path and eventually lead to functional failure. We refer to these positions as "critical tokens". In this paper, we propose ACToR, an adaptive critical token-aware retrieval framework for repository-level code generation. ACToR identifies critical tokens during generation and triggers targeted retrieval on demand to provide repository context at these decisive positions. In addition, we design a position-aware weighting method for dense retrievers to prioritize context that is more informative for generation. We evaluate ACToR on two representative repository-level benchmarks, RepoExec and CoderEval. Experimental results show that ACToR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving relative improvements of 8.4% on RepoExec and 15.4% on CoderEval. Beyond performance gains, we systematically quantify the impact of critical tokens, revealing their central role in major generation failures and highlighting the necessity of targeted retrieval strategies. We provide the code and data at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/ACToR.

</details>


### [29] [Efficient SWE Agent Benchmarking via Trajectory-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01603)
*Kefeng Duan,Dewu Zheng,Yanlin Wang,Xiwen Wang,Ensheng Shi,Xilin Liu,Yuchi Ma,Jiachi Chen,Mingwei Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出PTA-IRT，一种面向软件工程智能体评测的“特权轨迹感知”项目反应理论框架。该方法利用智能体历史执行轨迹中的过程信息，与最终通过/失败结果结合，在较低标定预算下更准确地恢复完整基准上的得分和智能体排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实软件工程基准的评测成本较高：单个任务往往需要多步代码浏览、修改与测试。现有高效评测通常通过挑选代表性任务子集估计全量性能，但主要依赖历史通过/失败矩阵或静态任务语义，忽略了智能体解决问题时产生的过程信息，因此在低预算标定场景下可能难以可靠估计能力与排名。

Method: PTA-IRT以项目反应理论为基础，将历史执行轨迹作为标定阶段可用的特权信息。轨迹信息包括已探索的代码上下文、尝试的编辑操作以及问题求解路径等过程级证据。框架据此改进两个环节：一是选择更具信息量的校准任务子集；二是结合过程与结果信号估计智能体能力，从而预测其在完整基准上的表现。

Result: 在四个软件工程基准上，PTA-IRT在低标定预算条件下均优于已有IRT基线方法，表现为更好的全基准分数恢复能力和智能体排名恢复能力。摘要未给出具体数值、显著性检验或各基准上的详细比较结果。

Conclusion: 过程轨迹是软件工程智能体评测中有价值但常被忽略的信息源。将其作为特权信息融入IRT，可在减少实际评测任务数量的同时提升性能估计与模型排序的可靠性。该工作尤其适用于评测成本高、预算受限且可获得历史执行日志的场景。

Abstract: Evaluating software engineering agents on realistic benchmarks is costly, since each task may require multi-step code exploration, modification, and test execution. Existing efficient evaluation methods select representative subsets to estimate full-benchmark performance, but are largely result-only: they fit historical pass/fail response matrices or static task semantics, discarding how agents solve problems. We propose PTA-IRT, a Privileged Trajectory-Aware Item Response Theory framework that fuses process and outcome signals. Historical execution trajectories supply process-level evidence beyond pass/fail, such as explored context, attempted edits, and solving paths, which PTA-IRT uses as privileged information for calibration subset selection and ability estimation. Under low calibration budgets, PTA-IRT consistently outperforms prior IRT baselines on score and ranking recovery across four SWE benchmarks. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/PTA-IRT.

</details>


### [30] [InteractBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Competitive Programming under Unrevealed Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29632)
*Jiaze Li,Aocheng Shen,Bing Liu,Boyu Zhang,Xiaoxuan Fan,Qiankun Zhang,Xianjun Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出InteractBench，一个包含322道可离线评测交互式编程题的基准，用于评估大语言模型在信息逐步揭示、协议约束和查询预算限制下的算法推理与编程能力。实验显示，即使先进推理模型在交互式任务上的成功率仍然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型编程基准主要面向输入信息一次性完整提供的任务，无法评估程序在未知关键信息、需要主动查询并动态维护状态时的推理能力。交互式编程题能够弥补这一评测缺口。

Method: 从Codeforces、AtCoder、IOI和ICPC中筛选322道高质量交互式问题，为每道题配置可执行的本地交互器，从而支持完全离线评测。基准同时评估模型生成代码的信息获取、状态跟踪、交互协议遵循和查询预算控制能力，并设计细粒度失败分类体系。

Result: 评测发现，当前先进推理模型在交互式问题上的整体表现仍较弱，存在明显的交互能力差距。算法逻辑错误是最主要的失败原因；此外，交互协议违规和超出查询预算也较为常见。

Conclusion: InteractBench为大语言模型的交互式算法推理提供了系统化、可复现的评测平台。结果表明，仅在传统完整信息编程任务上的良好表现不足以代表模型处理动态信息和受限交互的能力，未来需要进一步提升模型的交互规划、协议遵循和状态管理能力。

Abstract: Competitive programming is increasingly being used to evaluate the algorithmic reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on full-information tasks where all problem inputs are provided upfront. This overlooks a critical dimension of algorithmic reasoning: the ability of generated programs to operate when key information is not revealed upfront. Interactive problems, a distinctive component of competitive programming, embody this challenge. These problems require programs to engage in multi-round interaction with an interactor (a judge program) under strict protocol constraints and limited query budgets, with new information revealed only in response to queries. To address this gap, we introduce InteractBench, a benchmark comprising 322 high-quality interactive problems curated from Codeforces, AtCoder, IOI, and ICPC. Each problem is packaged with executable local interactors, enabling fully offline evaluation. Unlike existing benchmarks, InteractBench assesses whether model-generated code can acquire information and track state dynamically. Our evaluation reveals a significant interaction gap: even the most advanced reasoning models achieve limited success on interactive problems. Beyond success rates, we propose a fine-grained failure taxonomy to diagnose the root causes of these deficiencies. Although algorithmic logic errors remain dominant, protocol violations and query-budget overruns are frequent. Code is available at https://github.com/kmsgk0/InteractBench.

</details>

### [31] [Exponential Gaps Between Intuitionistic Linear Extended Frege Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00422)
*Amirhossein Akbar Tabatabai*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明了多个直觉主义子结构逻辑与线性逻辑之间扩展Frege证明系统的指数级分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同逻辑基础下证明系统的相对强度，特别是当逻辑M不包含于逻辑L时，比较M-Frege与L-Extended Frege的证明复杂度。

Method: 对任意满足条件的逻辑L和不被L包含的逻辑M，构造一族可由FL_e证明的公式；利用L-Frege的可行析取性质变体，证明这些公式在M-Frege中具有短证明，而在L-Extended Frege中需要指数规模证明。无感叹号连接词的情形则以IMALL和FL_e替代ILL及相应系统进行处理。

Result: 建立了从M-Frege到L-Extended Frege的指数级证明长度分离；同样的分离也适用于无感叹号连接词的系统。可行析取性质的变体是证明分离的关键，并具有独立研究价值。

Conclusion: 逻辑包含关系会导致相应Frege系统之间显著的证明复杂度差异：当M不包含于L时，M-Frege可能指数级强于L-Extended Frege。该工作同时拓展了可行析取性质在子结构逻辑和线性逻辑证明复杂度中的应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish exponential separations between Extended Frege systems for a range of intuitionistic substructural and linear logics. More precisely, for any logic $L$ below the intuitionistic logic obtained by extending $\mathbf{ILL}$ with structural rules, and any logic $M$ not contained in $L$, we construct a family of $\mathsf{FL_e}$-provable formulas that have short proofs in $M$-Frege but require proofs of exponential size in $L$-Extended Frege. The same result holds in the $!$-free settings, using $\mathbf{IMALL}$ and $\mathbf{FL_e}$ in place of $\mathbf{ILL}$. The key ingredient in proving these separations is a variant of the feasible disjunction property for $L$-Frege, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [32] [Subgroup Accessibility in Group Order Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00499)
*Anatole Dahan*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究固定点逻辑及其扩展定义可达子群生成集的能力，证明一般情形下该操作不能由任何捕获P的逻辑表达，但在具有可定义有序生成集的基群上可由FP+ord表达；基群为阿贝尔群时，FPC也可以表达该操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可达子群生成集是Schreier–Sims算法中的核心操作，并支撑有界颜色类图的多项式时间规范化，而这类图目前尚无自然的捕获P逻辑。研究旨在刻画固定点逻辑对该群论操作的表达能力及其限制。

Method: 首先利用可达子群不一定具有多项式规模的对称生成集，证明该操作一般不能在任何捕获P的逻辑中定义。随后在基群具有可定义有序生成集的条件下，于FP+ord中部分模拟Schreier–Sims算法；对于阿贝尔基群，再将结果推广到固定点逻辑与计数FPC。

Result: 一般情况下，可达子群生成集操作无法由任何捕获P的逻辑表达。若基群具有可定义有序生成集，则该操作可由FP+ord定义；若基群为阿贝尔群，则FPC同样可以定义该操作，并可定义具有阿贝尔颜色的任意图的自同构群。

Conclusion: 可达子群操作的逻辑可定义性依赖于基群的结构性条件，而非仅依赖其多项式时间可计算性。FP+ord在有序生成集条件下足以模拟相关算法，FPC则在阿贝尔情形下具有更强的群论表达能力，但这并不意味着FPC能够规范化相应图类。

Abstract: We investigate the expressive power of fixed-point logics (FP) and their extensions in defining generating sets for accessible subgroups of definable permutation groups. This operation, computable in polynomial time via the Schreier-Sims algorithm, plays a central role in the group-theoretic approach to Graph Isomorphism and Graph Canonisation. In particular, it underpins polynomial-time canonisation for bounded colour-class graphs--a class for which no natural logic capturing P is currently known. We first show that this operation cannot, in general, be expressed in any logic for P. This limitation arises from the fact that accessible subgroups need not admit symmetric generating sets of polynomial size. However, we prove that when the base group admits a definable ordered generating set, the accessible subgroup operation becomes definable in fixed-point logic with the group order operator (FP + ord). This is achieved by partially simulating the Schreier-Sims algorithm within FP + ord. As a corollary, we show that fixed-point logic with counting (FPC) can also define the operation when the base group is abelian. In particular, FPC can define the automorphism group of any graph with abelian colours--despite being unable to canonise such graphs.

</details>


### [33] [On Synthesis of Metric Interval Temporal Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01032)
*Hsi-Ming Ho,Shankaranarayanan Krishna,Khushraj Madnani*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个无需预定义模板或受限逻辑片段、面向MITL的精确被动学习框架，将定时学习问题转化为可扩展的无定时学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动学习方法主要针对确定性规范或有限的定时正则表达式片段，难以处理表达能力更强的度量区间时序逻辑（MITL）。

Method: 通过比较正负样本轨迹中的定量时间差异，综合出精确的定时约束，并将这些约束编码为新的布尔原子命题，从而把时间信息嵌入字母表；随后利用高效的现成无定时LTL工具进行公式学习与求解。

Result: 框架具有完备性，能够保证找到区分正负样本的规范。实验在多个基准上验证了实现的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法为无需模板的MITL精确被动学习提供了统一且实用的解决方案，通过复用无定时时序逻辑工具降低了复杂定时推理的实现成本。

Abstract: Automated mining of formal specifications is vital for verifying real-time systems. However, existing passive learning approaches remain restricted to deterministic specifications or limited fragments of Timed Regular Expressions (TRE). To our knowledge, this paper presents the first framework to tackle \emph{precise} passive learning for an expressive timed logic, \emph{Metric Interval Temporal Logic} (MITL) without relying on predefined templates or restricted logic fragments.
  Our approach formally reduces the timed learning problem into a scalable untimed one. By identifying quantitative timing differences between positive and negative traces, we synthesise precise timed constraints and inject them as new Boolean atomic propositions. This embeds timing into the alphabet, delegating the complex formula evaluation to highly optimised, off-the-shelf untimed LTL tools.
  Crucially, our framework is complete, guaranteeing a separating specification can always be found. We evaluate our implementation across several benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>
