### [34] [Beyond Locks and Thread IDs: Static Data Race Detection Off The Beaten Path (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00246)
*Daniel Bund,Julian Erhard,Michael Petter,Michael Schwarz*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文扩展Digest执行历史摘要框架，以支持线程屏障、pthread_once及祖先线程锁集，从而提升静态数据竞争检测的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态数据竞争分析通常忽略部分并发构造和同步机制，导致对线程间依赖关系建模不足、检测结果精度受限。

Method: 为常用线程屏障和pthread_once设计摘要机制，并将框架实例化为对祖先线程所持锁集的抽象；同时构建一组针对这些特性的litmus测试，并与现有先进工具进行比较。

Result: 实验比较表明，所实现的分析能够处理上述同步特性，而对比的主流工具普遍缺乏相应支持。

Conclusion: 扩展后的Digest框架可更完整地表示并发执行历史和同步关系，有助于提高静态数据竞争检测能力；所提出的测试套件也可用于评估相关分析工具的支持程度。

Abstract: Maintaining an abstraction of the execution history of threads can improve the precision of data race detection in static analysis. Here, we extend the digest framework to handle concurrency constructs and synchronization mechanisms that have been ignored in static race detection. We introduce mechanisms for the commonly used thread barriers, as well as pthread_once, which allows to ensure that an action is executed only once. We also instantiate the framework with an abstraction of locksets held by ancestor threads. We propose a suite of litmus tests to evaluate analyses for these features and compare our implementation to state-of-the-art tools, finding that they lack support.

</details>


### [35] [Predicting Program Exit Code with LLMs and Programming Language Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00579)
*Lara Marinov,Aditya Thimmaiah,Jayanth Srinivasa,Junyi Jessy Li,Milos Gligoric*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出程序可执行性预测任务（PrEx），研究大语言模型在判断程序语义有效性时，是依赖预训练先验还是遵循给定的操作语义规则。实验表明，模型主要依赖先验知识，在语义修改和程序复杂度提升时表现明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在代码生成与翻译等软件工程任务中表现出色，但其是否真正理解并应用编程语言语义仍不明确。现有评估难以区分模型对形式规则的推理能力与对训练数据模式的依赖，因此需要专门的语义遵循性测试。

Method: 提出PrEx任务：给定程序语法和操作语义，预测程序是否语义有效；若无效，还需识别违反的形式规则。研究者从有效程序出发，通过系统化的无效变换构建包含正负样本的数据集，并在两种语义形式化、两种语义变换，以及人工编写、LLM翻译和模糊测试生成的程序划分上评估开源代码大语言模型。

Result: 实验显示，LLM通常依赖预训练先验，而不是系统地应用给定语义规则。在修改后的语义环境中，模型性能尤其差；随着程序复杂度增加，性能进一步下降。该趋势在不同程序来源和评估设置下具有一致性。

Conclusion: 当前开源代码大语言模型的程序语义理解和规则遵循能力有限，其判断更多基于预训练形成的模式先验。PrEx为评估模型的程序可执行性预测、语义推理及对语义变化的适应能力提供了系统化基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown proficiency in various software engineering tasks, such as code generation and translation. However, a key limitation in their performance may be their (lack of) understanding of programming-language semantics. Even when explicit semantics are given, it remains unclear whether LLMs apply those rules or lean on priors learned during pre-training instead. We study if LLMs lean on priors or given semantics with a novel task--Program Executability Prediction (PrEx)--that asks models to predict whether a program is semantically valid or invalid (and, if invalid, which formal rule it violates) given the program's syntax and operational semantics. Because PrEx requires both valid and invalid programs, we build a dataset with systematically generated invalid transformations derived from valid programs. We evaluate open-source coding LLMs under two semantic formalisms and two semantic shifts across Human-Written, LLM-Translated, and Fuzzer-Generated program splits. Our findings show that LLMs lean on pre-training priors rather than systematically applying the given rules, performing especially poorly on modified semantics and degrading further as program complexity increases. PrEx is available at https://github.com/EngineeringSoftware/prex.

</details>


### [36] [A Dynamic Intermediate Representation for Hybrid Quantum-Classical Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01037)
*Alex Rice,Chris Heunen,Tobias Grosser*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向混合量子—经典程序的新型中间表示（IR），将量子门提升为可动态创建、组合和控制的一等值，以突破传统固定门序列电路模型的表达限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子编译器以静态量子电路为核心，难以自然表示运行时数据、测量结果或经典控制流决定量子操作的程序。因此，需要一种能够统一描述经典计算驱动量子行为的表示机制。

Method: 作者设计了将量子门作为一等对象的IR，使门能够在运行时生成、传递、组合及受控执行。该IR用于表达随机门选择、自适应纠错和测量驱动计算等动态行为，并通过噪声建模、随机化编译、量子纠错及基于测量的量子计算等案例验证其适用性；同时在混合量子—经典基准程序集上评估表示与编译优化能力。

Result: 案例分析表明，该IR能够以较紧凑的形式表达传统电路模型难以直接描述的动态量子程序，并支持原有电路表示下无法实现或难以实现的编译分析与变换。基准评估进一步显示，该表示具有良好的程序紧凑性，并有助于编译器开展优化。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子门值化并纳入统一的混合编程表示，为动态量子程序提供了比静态电路模型更强的表达能力。其主要贡献在于为量子—经典协同计算建立可分析、可变换的编译基础，但摘要未给出具体性能指标、优化幅度或与现有IR的定量比较。

Abstract: Quantum compilers typically follow the circuit model, representing programs as fixed sequences of gates. This static view breaks down in hybrid quantum-classical applications, where gate choices depend on runtime data or measurement results. We introduce a new Intermediate Representation (IR) that elevates gates to first-class values, enabling their dynamic creation, composition, and control. This unified representation allows classical computation to steer quantum behaviour, capturing phenomena including stochastic gate selection, adaptive error correction, and measurement-driven computation within a single framework. Case studies in noise modelling, randomised compilation, error correction, and measurement-based quantum computing show that our IR expresses these programs compactly and supports optimisations that were not possible in the circuit model. Evaluation on a benchmark suite of hybrid quantum-classical programs indicates that our IR represents programs compactly and facilitates compiler analysis and transformation.

</details>


### [37] [Griotte: Verified Compartmentalisation via Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01110)
*June Rousseau,Aïna Linn Georges,Jean Pichon-Pharabod,Lars Birkedal*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出Griotte与Griotte OS，分别是CHERIoT硬件和实时操作系统的理想化模型，用于形式化并验证其基于能力的隔离、跨隔间调用和栈安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHERIoT的安全性依赖能力硬件与特权切换器的协同，但其“隔间化”概念及整体安全保证尚缺乏严格形式化与验证。论文旨在回答如何定义隔间化安全语义，以及实际设计是否满足这些安全性质。

Method: 构建忠实且理想化的Griotte机器与Griotte OS；使用基于延续的逻辑关系形式化切换器和能力机器的组合行为；定义满足安全保证的Griotte切换器规范，并证明其实现符合规范。采用模块化验证方法，先分别验证各隔间，再组合其规格。

Result: 论文证明了切换器实现满足既定安全规范，并通过多个场景展示Griotte对CHERIoT关键机制的刻画，包括在与未知代码共享内存时保护本地状态完整性。结果表明，能力机制与切换器能够协同实现隔间隔离、跨隔间调用控制和栈安全。

Conclusion: Griotte为CHERIoT的安全设计提供了形式化基础。其逻辑关系和模块化组合验证方法不仅刻画了隔间化保证，也支持对CHERIoT整体实现进行严格安全论证。

Abstract: CHERIoT is a novel hardware-software co-design that leverages hardware capabilities to define a notion of compartment, in a minimalistic capability-based OS, CHERIoT RTOS. By default, compartments are isolated to limit damage in case of bugs or malicious behaviour. To allow cross-compartment communication, the OS provides a privileged component, called the switcher. The switcher provides an interface for cross-compartment calls, while enforcing isolation between compartments and guaranteeing stack safety. Together with hardware capabilities, the switcher is critical to enforce the security guarantees of the CHERIoT compartment model. The design of CHERIoT raises two questions: First, how can one formalise the informal notion of compartmentalisation that CHERIoT compartments are designed to provide? And second, given that the safety properties of CHERIoT hinge on the complementary roles of the capability machine and of the switcher, does the design of CHERIoT enforce the desired security properties?
  In this paper, we introduce Griotte and Griotte OS, idealised but faithful versions of the CHERIoT machine and the CHERIoT RTOS, which we use to answer these two questions: First, we formally capture the aforementioned security guarantees in the form of a continuation-based logical relation which captures the combined behaviour of the switcher and of the capability machine. And second, we define a specification for the Griotte switcher that enforces those guarantees, and prove that the implementation meets the specification. We demonstrate Griotte on a range of key scenarios illustrating different aspects of CHERIoT, including integrity of the local state in the presence of memory sharing with unknown code. Our approach is modular: we verify compartments individually, and then compose their specifications. Together, our contributions give a solid formal foundation to the design of CHERIoT.

</details>


### [38] [Relational Task Generation Language: A Declarative Specification Framework for Relational Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01292)
*Oleksii Kolesnichenko,Jakub Peleška,Gustav Šír*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出开源声明式语言RTGL，用于规范化生成关系型深度学习任务，减少手工SQL定义带来的高成本与数据泄漏风险，并可与现有关系型深度学习框架集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表关系数据上的预测任务通常依赖人工编写SQL来定义样本、标签、时间边界和关联逻辑。这一过程繁琐、难以复用，且容易因目标定义或时间约束不严谨而产生数据泄漏及基准任务不一致问题。

Method: 作者设计Relational Task Generation Language（RTGL），以声明式方式描述关系型预测任务，屏蔽底层SQL实现细节。论文通过RTGL重建已有关系型深度学习基准任务，比较其与原始人工SQL定义的差异；同时利用RTGL构造多种不同结构和目标类型的新任务，并测试其与既有关系型深度学习框架的兼容性。

Result: RTGL能够复现现有基准任务，并在重建过程中发现部分原始任务因人工SQL编写而存在定义不一致。实验表明，RTGL在任务表达、生成和框架对接方面具备较好的稳健性与可用性，能够支持多样化的关系型预测目标。

Conclusion: RTGL为关系型深度学习任务定义提供了统一、可复用且更安全的声明式抽象，可降低手工SQL造成的实现负担和错误风险。其开源及对现有框架的兼容性，有助于提升任务构建、基准复现和实验比较的规范性。

Abstract: Relational Deep Learning (RDL) has become a powerful paradigm for learning from multi-tabular data. However, manually defining RDL prediction tasks is a laborious process that frequently results in data leakage. To address this issue, we introduce Relational Task Generation Language (RTGL) - an open-source declarative language that streamlines RDL task formulation by abstracting away low-level SQL details. We showcase RTGL by reconstructing existing RDL benchmark tasks and uncovering their inconsistencies stemming from manually crafted SQL definitions of RDL prediction targets, thereby underscoring the value of a dedicated declarative language. In addition, we demonstrate the practical utility of RTGL by designing various new tasks with diverse forms and target types. Our experiments confirm the robustness and usability of RTGL, as well as its seamless integration with the existing RDL frameworks, making it widely accessible to the community.

</details>


### [39] [Support Local Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01502)
*Maxwell Bernstein,Takashi Kokubun,Aaron Patterson,Si Xing "Alan" Wu,Kevin Menard*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文介绍了CRuby的新型方法级JIT编译器ZJIT。其核心贡献是在SSA形式的高层中间表示中将Ruby局部变量提升为SSA值，从而支持比YJIT更丰富的全局与局部优化，同时针对Ruby局部变量的语义复杂性提出正确且高效的编译处理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有YJIT采用惰性基本块版本化，优化能力相对受限；同时，项目希望通过更清晰、模块化的编译器设计促进外部贡献。Ruby局部变量若仅按内存加载与存储处理，会限制数据流优化；若通过复杂偏特化从内存状态恢复SSA值，则实现成本较高。因此，ZJIT需要直接而可靠地将局部变量纳入SSA数据流。

Method: ZJIT仍从CRuby字节码生成机器码，但采用方法级编译组织，并设置多个全局及局部优化遍。其高层IR使用静态单赋值形式：编译过程中将局部变量映射为SSA值，通过控制流合并处的SSA构造来表达变量版本和数据流。论文进一步识别Ruby局部变量导致正确性与性能困难的语言特性，并为这些情况设计相应的编译策略。

Result: 摘要表明，ZJIT成功实现了局部变量向SSA值的提升，这区别于其他Ruby JIT常见的两类方案：保留局部变量为内存读写，或依赖高级偏特化从内存中重建SSA信息。该设计使ZJIT具备执行多种局部和全局优化的基础，并能够处理Ruby局部变量的特殊语义挑战。摘要未提供具体基准测试、性能提升比例或代码规模数据。

Conclusion: ZJIT通过方法级JIT架构和SSA高层IR，为CRuby提供了比YJIT更可扩展的优化平台。直接将Ruby局部变量提升至SSA是其关键技术点；论文的主要价值在于梳理局部变量编译的困难来源，并展示可保证正确性且利于优化的实现方法。

Abstract: Ruby is a dynamically typed and object-oriented programming language. Its primary implementation, CRuby, contains a bytecode virtual machine and a mature lazy basic block versioning (LBBV) just-in-time (JIT) compiler called YJIT.
  In order to both implement more advanced optimizations than YJIT supports and also encourage more outside contributions, we present a new method-based JIT called ZJIT. Like YJIT, ZJIT compiles from bytecode to machine code. Unlike YJIT, ZJIT has multiple global and local optimization passes.
  ZJIT's high-level intermediate representation is in static single assignment (SSA) form. In order to optimize Ruby's local variables, ZJIT lifts local variables into SSA values. This is a departure from how other Ruby compilers handle locals: other JIT compilers either leave local variables as memory loads and stores or do advanced partial evaluation to recover SSA values from memory.
  While implementing locals, we (re-)discovered what features make local variables in Ruby especially challenging to compile correctly and efficiently. We demonstrate these features and illustrate how we solved these problems in ZJIT.

</details>

### [40] [AgentProv: Auditing Agentic LLM API Providers via Tool-use Policy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00052)
*Xun Wang,Bihe Zhao,Michael Backes,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentProv，一种面向智能体式LLM API的模型溯源审计方法。它不依赖文本输出，而是利用工具调用动作的类别分布识别实际部署的模型，并以MMD置换检验作出身份判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM API可能在不透明情况下替换、量化或包装其宣称的基础模型。既有审计方法主要分析文本输出，但智能体API常仅暴露结构化工具调用；同时，服务商注入的系统提示会显著改变文本分布，导致基于文本的审计产生较高误报。因此需要一种对部署上下文更稳健、且在工具调用场景可用的审计通道。

Method: AgentProv基于如下观察：近期智能体后训练将工具使用行为编码进模型权重，因而模型在给定任务下产生的工具调用类别分布可作为身份指纹。该方法收集部署端的工具调用动作，将其经验类别分布与候选模型参考分布进行比较，并采用最大均值差异（MMD）置换检验判断二者是否来自同一模型分布。

Result: 在630个检查点配对实验中，AgentProv检测到全部被替换模型，报告检出率为100%。在系统提示注入条件下，其误报率为7%，低于文中比较的MET（67%）和RUT（53%）。对于第三方API端点，AgentProv与MET的判定分歧可由独立的令牌计数侧信道所揭示的服务商系统提示注入现象解释。

Conclusion: 工具调用动作是审计智能体LLM API模型身份的有效且更具上下文不变性的信号。相较文本输出审计，AgentProv能降低由系统提示和结构化输出接口造成的误判，并为检测API后端模型替换提供了可操作的统计检验框架。其适用性仍依赖于目标服务暴露足够的工具调用行为，以及参考模型与实际服务在任务、工具定义和采样设置上的可比性。

Abstract: Commercial LLM APIs advertise a specific foundation model, but the served backbone may be silently substituted, quantized, or wrapped, for example to save deployment costs. All existing audits decide backbone identity from the text-output channel, which is structurally fragile for agentic APIs because modern serving stacks (OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, Cloudflare Workers AI, LangGraph) discard text and expose only structured actions when the model calls a tool, and provider-injected system prompts can distort text distributions enough that text-channel tests falsely accuse honest providers of substituting the claimed model. We observe that recent agentic post-training internalizes tool-use directly into the weights, opening a new audit channel that the serving stack still exposes and that is largely invariant to deployment context. We introduce Agentic Provenance (AgentProv), the first action-based identity audit for agentic LLM APIs: AgentProv fingerprints a deployed model through its categorical tool-call distribution and decides identity via an MMD permutation test. AgentProv catches every substituted model (100% on 630 evaluated checkpoint pairs), while holding the false-positive rate under system-prompt injection at 7% (vs. 67% for MET and 53% for RUT). On third-party API endpoints, AgentProv's disagreements with MET are consistent with an independent token-count side-channel that detects provider-injected system prompts.

</details>


### [41] [A Formal Analysis of Agent Payment Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00060)
*Ke Jiang,Mohan Yu,Yuan Chang,Mohit Kumar Jangid,Jianyu Niu,Cong Wang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对四种智能体支付协议进行统一形式化验证，发现授权、凭证、结算与履约等跨阶段绑定存在系统性缺口，并提出18项共享安全原则及相应修复模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体代用户发起购买和支付时，用户意图、委托权限、凭证使用、资金结算和服务履约分散于多个参与方与阶段。现有规范和实现中的安全保证多为隐含假设，缺乏系统化的形式验证，容易产生跨主体、跨状态的不一致。

Method: 研究以智能体支付生命周期为共同抽象，使用Tamarin对x402、MPP、ACP和AP2建立基于公开资料的形式化模型，描述角色、状态、信任假设和生命周期转换。作者通过来源支撑的验证问题及反例轨迹识别缺失绑定、状态约束和跨阶段对应关系，归纳为18项原则；对每个保留违规项构造最小增强参考模型并重新验证。对于x402的新发现，还在三个实现中评估，并通过PoC、SDK/模式证据及可执行轨迹进行验证。

Result: 在86个验证案例中，研究复现了46个已知或校准案例，并发现40个此前未文档化的形式一致性问题。作者选取十项代表性发现，在五项安全原则范围内完成实现层验证，表明部分问题不仅存在于抽象模型，也可在SDK、模式定义或实现流程中得到佐证。

Conclusion: 智能体支付协议的核心安全要求是：委托授权必须与其产生的经济结果和服务结果保持端到端一致，并在不同参与方、状态及协议阶段之间具备可验证的关联。统一生命周期建模与形式化验证可用于发现规范中隐含但未被明确约束的安全关系，并为协议和实现的最小化修复提供依据。

Abstract: Agent payment protocols are emerging as a key transaction layer for autonomous commerce, enabling AI agents to purchase goods and services and execute payments on users' behalf. Unlike conventional payment flows, they distribute user intent, delegated authority, credential use, settlement, and fulfillment across multiple actors and stages, creating security dependencies that no single message or participant can enforce. Yet these guarantees remain largely implicit across evolving specifications, schemas, and reference implementations, with little systematic formal analysis.
  We formalize four representative agent payment protocols: x402, MPP, ACP, and AP2 in Tamarin. Using a common abstraction of the agent payment lifecycle, we construct source-grounded models that capture each protocol's roles, state, trust assumptions, and lifecycle transitions. Rather than assuming a complete property taxonomy, we use source-backed verification questions and counterexample traces to expose missing bindings, state constraints, and cross-stage correspondences, consolidating them into 18 shared security principles. Across 86 verification cases, our analysis reproduces 46 known or calibration cases and identifies 40 previously undocumented formal-consistency findings. For each retained violation, we isolate the missing protocol relation, construct a minimally strengthened reference model, and reverify the intended property. We further evaluate the new x402 findings across three implementations and validate ten representative findings through implementation PoCs, SDK/schema-level witnesses, and source-aligned executable traces spanning five security principles. Our results show that delegated authorization must remain consistent with its resulting economic and service effects across actors, states, and protocol stages.

</details>


### [42] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Industrial Cybersecurity: A Review of Methods, Operational Integration, and Research Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00171)
*Amr S. Mohamed,Charlotte Fritz,Ahmad Mohammad Saber,Yiqun Ma,Ratinder Kaur,Mohammed Al-Darwbi,Daniela Friedrich,Deepa Kundur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了可解释人工智能（XAI）在工业网络安全，尤其是安全运营中心（SOC）中的应用，重点分析其对威胁检测、决策透明度、合规性和运营可信度的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业基础设施数字化以及IT与OT融合扩大了攻击面。虽然AI/ML能够提升异常检测、威胁分析和自动响应能力，但其决策过程缺乏透明度，影响运营信任、监管合规和事件处置，因此需要引入XAI。

Method: 论文采用综述与分类分析方法，梳理工业SOC中的AI工作流、工业运营数据类型及AI威胁检测的优势与局限，并系统归纳特征归因、代理模型、规则解释和可视化等XAI方法，进一步从运营、监管和安全要求角度评估其工业适用性。

Result: 研究指出，XAI有助于提升工业网络安全系统的可解释性和决策透明度，但仍面临标注数据有限、模型可靠性不足、可解释性与检测性能之间的权衡，以及难以融入现有SOC工作流等问题。工业环境还对安全性、合规性和实时运营提出了高于传统IT环境的要求。

Conclusion: 未来研究应重点发展可信、可操作且面向工业领域定制的XAI网络安全方案，加强领域知识融合、模型可靠性评估、解释结果验证和SOC流程集成，以支持工业环境中的安全决策与实际部署。

Abstract: The increasing digitalization of industrial infrastructure and the convergence of information technology (IT) and operational technology (OT) have expanded the cyberattack surface of industrial systems. To address the growing complexity of cyber threats, artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) techniques are increasingly deployed within industrial cybersecurity operations, particularly in Security Operations Centers (SOCs). While these approaches improve anomaly detection, threat analysis, and automated response, their opaque decision-making presents challenges for operational trust, regulatory compliance, and incident response. EXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) has emerged as a promising paradigm to improve the transparency and interpretability of AI-driven cybersecurity systems and decisions. This paper provides a comprehensive review of XAI techniques in industrial cybersecurity, focusing on industrial SOC environments and operational security workflows. We examine the role of AI in industrial SOC workflows, the types of operational data leveraged in industrial environments, and the benefits and limitations of AI-based threat detection. We then review major families of XAI approaches, including feature attribution methods, surrogate models, rule-based explanations, and visualization techniques, and analyze their applicability to industrial use cases. We further discuss the operational, regulatory, and safety requirements that distinguish industrial systems from traditional IT environments. Key challenges are examined, including limited labeled datasets, model reliability, explainability-performance tradeoffs, and the integration of XAI tools into SOC workflows. Finally, we identify open research directions and opportunities for developing trustworthy, operationally viable, and domain-specific XAI-enabled cybersecurity solutions for industrial environments.

</details>


### [43] [DUPIN: Attack Learning Is Still Needed! Demonstrating Few-Shot after Unsupervised Pretraining Is A Nimble Forensics Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00259)
*Chanwoo Bae,Hailun Ding,Shiqing Ma,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DUPIN是一种面向攻击取证的学习方法，通过在大规模审计事件溯源图上进行无监督预训练，再利用少量标注攻击样本进行微调，以提升检测能力和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统攻击取证方法通常依赖大量标注数据，难以适应审计日志规模大、攻击样本稀缺以及数据源多样化的问题，因此需要一种能够利用海量未标注数据并支持少样本检测的方案。

Method: DUPIN首先在以溯源图表示的审计事件上进行无监督预训练，预训练数据覆盖38至52天、总量约7.3 TB的审计日志；随后进入少样本学习阶段，利用少量已标注攻击案例对模型进行微调，并与多种基线方法在四类数据源、25个APT活动上进行评估。

Result: 研究构建了大规模、跨数据源的评测设置，用于检验DUPIN的检测能力和可扩展性。摘要未提供具体的性能指标或与基线相比的定量提升，因此无法进一步判断其优势幅度。

Conclusion: DUPIN通过结合无监督预训练与少样本微调，为低标注成本、可扩展的攻击取证提供了新的技术路径；其实际有效性仍需结合完整论文中的实验指标和消融分析进行验证。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to learning-based attack forensics called DUPIN. DUPIN performs unsupervised pre-training on an enormous amount of audit events in the form of provenance graphs. It then proceeds to a few-shot learning stage, leveraging a small number of labeled attack examples to fine-tune its detection capabilities. We pretrain DUPIN on up to 38 - 52 days of audit logs (7.3TB total) and evaluate it against various baselines on 25 APT campaigns across four different data sources, facilitating the scalable evaluation.

</details>


### [44] [Delegation Without Trust: An Empirical Gap Analysis of Identity, Authorization, and Runtime Governance in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00267)
*Panduranga Sai Varma Dantuluri,Jyotirmoy Sundi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究自主LLM智能体在多级委托场景下的授权与隔离问题，提出不可信模型假设，并通过威胁模型、框架评估和授权代理实现证明现有方案存在明显安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体可持有凭据、调用工具并生成子智能体，而语言模型可能遭受提示注入或被劫持。核心目标是确保即使模型完全不可信，智能体也不能超出明确授予的权限。

Method: 构建涵盖混淆代理、令牌窃取与重放、提示注入导致的权限升级、受损子智能体四类威胁的模型，并推导八项安全要求；评估默认代理运行时及LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen和MCP授权模型；实现授权代理并开展对抗性测试，包括11种直接攻击、20万次伪造令牌测试及2000个随机场景下的子智能体权限隔离实验。

Result: 默认运行时无法抵御四类威胁；四种框架中三个缺乏内置权限隔离，一个仅提供部分支持。授权代理成功阻断四类威胁，11种设计攻击均未奏效，20万次伪造令牌尝试全部失败；受损子智能体平均仅可访问1.5个动作，而承载式授权可访问全部8100个动作。单次决策开销约2.6微秒，对模型推理延迟可忽略。

Conclusion: 现有LLM智能体框架和标准不足以独立解决多智能体委托安全问题。将授权控制移出模型、采用可验证的权限代理和细粒度委托隔离，是在不可信模型假设下实现安全自治的有效路径；相关原则已在VotalAI的LLM Shield中落地。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents increasingly act on a user's behalf: they hold credentials, call tools and services, and spawn sub-agents that act further on their behalf. This turns a long-standing distributed-systems question -- who is authorized to do what, on whose authority -- into an urgent and largely unsolved problem, because the component driving each agent is a language model an adversary can hijack. We argue that agent security must be evaluated under an untrusted-model assumption: a correct system is one in which a fully prompt-injected agent still cannot exceed the authority explicitly delegated to it. Against this standard we make three contributions. First, we give a threat model for multi-agent delegation centered on four adversaries -- confused deputy, token theft and replay, prompt-injection privilege escalation, and compromised sub-agents -- and derive eight security requirements a governed agent system must meet. Second, we show the gap is real: a default agent runtime modeling common practice (broad bearer credentials, authorization gated inside the model) fails all four threats, and across four widely used frameworks -- LangGraph, CrewAI, AutoGen, and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) authorization model -- three provide no built-in confinement and one only partial; no existing standard alone covers the requirement set. Third, we implement and adversarially evaluate an authorization broker that closes the gap. It blocks all four threats; it resists 11 direct attacks on its design and accepts 0 of 200,000 forged tokens; it confines a compromised sub-agent to its delegated task (a mean of 1.5 reachable actions versus all 8,100 under bearer delegation, across 2,000 randomized scenarios); and it enforces at microsecond cost (about 2.6 microseconds per decision), negligible against model inference. These principles are also realized in production in VotalAI's LLM Shield.

</details>


### [45] [Workload Identification with Physical Side Channels for AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00309)
*Simone Gargiulo,Gabriel Kulp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: AI compute verification is one of the first tangible and tractable points for international policy aimed at AI governance. Determining whether frontier labs, or any operator, comply with agreements requires the regulating authority to discern how their compute is used. The elementary building block of AI compute is the GPU, and any activity it executes leaves a physical trace. Here, we show that an external observer can identify the class of the workload running on an NVIDIA H200 from its power draw. Unlike on-chip NVML telemetry, which can be spoofed or replayed, such a physical channel can in principle be observed independently of operator cooperation. We recorded $930$ five-second traces at $\sim 10$ MHz, covering seventeen open LLM families and twenty-five non-AI workloads. Over this corpus we separate training from inference and from non-AI computation with an accuracy of $97\%$ and a macro-averaged F1 score of $0.955$, evaluated on model families unseen during training. AI workload spectral content predominantly lies below $\sim 20$kHz and training is particularly recognizable through the memory-bound optimizer update. The GPU operator is then treated as adversarial and able to reshape the physical computation itself. Four evasion strategies are tested to disguise training as inference, producing an additional 680 adversarial traces. A detector hardened against evasion strategies, with the tested strategy held out, catches training $\geq 99\%$ of the time for three of the four strategies. The fourth, diluted low-rank adaptation (LoRA), is detected $48$--$88\%$ of the time with a hardened classifier, rising to $\geq 98\%$ with an additional rescue rule. While these attacks are not a comprehensive evaluation against adversarial behaviour, they offer initial insights beyond genuine activities and a dataset for developing and testing stronger evasion mechanisms.

</details>


### [46] [OreProof: Verifiable Provenance with Limited Disclosure for Critical-Minerals Supply Chains Using Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00340)
*Oleksandr Hrabar,Hossein Arshadi Soufiani,Henry M. Kim,Chien-Chih Chen,Ali Vazirizadeh,Hjalmar Turesson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出OreProof，一种面向关键矿产供应链的隐私保护型可验证追溯平台，通过零知识证明、链下/链上混合存储和批量锚定，在不披露敏感供应商信息、品位/产量及价格的前提下实现来源验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链监管者和采购方需要可验证的来源信息，但上游参与者担心披露供应商身份、检测品位、产量和价格等商业敏感数据，形成可验证性与信息保密之间的结构性矛盾。

Method: 采用设计科学方法构建OreProof原型，以黄金供应链为实例。系统结合链上/链下混合数据模型、Groth16零知识证明、Merkle树批量锚定流程，以及符合UNTP标准的可验证凭证，并部署在公有zkEVM测试网上；同时与透明披露基线及既定攻击者模型进行比较。

Result: 在攻击者模型下，透明基线中可直接推断的机密属性类别由4类中的3类，降至OreProof中的0类；Merkle批量锚定显著提升了处理吞吐量。研究还提炼出四项初步设计原则：基于已承诺数据进行证明而非直接暴露数据；仅对可验证来源签发凭证并标记混合商品中的未知输入；从系统初始阶段即生成标准兼容凭证；按照供应链角色划分信息披露范围。

Conclusion: OreProof表明，零知识证明与标准化可验证凭证能够缓解矿产供应链中的追溯验证与商业隐私冲突。该原型为隐私保护型供应链追溯提供了可行架构，但其实际推广仍需进一步验证可扩展性、跨组织互操作性及真实业务环境中的治理机制。

Abstract: Critical-minerals supply chains face a structural tension: regulators and buyers demand verifiable provenance, yet upstream actors are hesitant to disclose supplier identities, assay grades/yields, and prices that verification appears to require. We report a design science account of OreProof, a prototypical traceability platform addressing this verifiability-disclosure trade-off. Instantiated for gold, OreProof combines a hybrid on-chain/off-chain data model, Groth16 zero-knowledge proofs for selective disclosure, a Merkle-batched anchoring pipeline, and UNTP-aligned verifiable credentials on a public zkEVM testnet. Against a transparent baseline, directly inferable confidential attributes fell from three of four categories to none under a defined attacker model, while batched anchoring substantially improved throughput. Our contributions are the artifact prototype as well as four nascent design principles: prove over committed data rather than exposing it; credential only verifiable origin and flag unknown inputs for blended commodities; emit standards-aligned credentials from the outset; and partition disclosure by supply-chain role.

</details>


### [47] [NeuroPriv: Adversarial Representation Learning for Privacy in Wearable EEG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00390)
*Sarmistha Sarna Gomasta,Bhawana Chhaglani,Prashant Shenoy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究表明，紧凑型可穿戴 EEG 特征虽能有效支持认知状态识别，但仍可泄露性别、年龄和个体身份信息；隐私感知表示学习可在基本保持任务性能的同时显著降低这些推断风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴 EEG 被广泛用于认知监测、神经功能评估和数字健康，但仅传输频谱或空间特征并不等于实现隐私保护。研究旨在评估此类特征的身份与人口属性泄露风险，并探索兼顾效用和隐私的表示方法。

Method: 以 EEGMAT 为案例，使用常见的紧凑型 EEG 特征执行认知状态、性别、年龄和个体身份分类，并以平衡准确率衡量性能。随后采用隐私感知表示学习构建降低敏感信息可分性的特征表示，同时评估其对认知任务性能的影响。

Result: 未经保护的 EEG 特征在认知状态、性别、年龄和个体身份推断上的平衡准确率分别为 0.788、0.858、0.789 和 0.692。隐私感知表示学习后，认知任务准确率为 0.781，而上述三类敏感属性推断准确率分别降至 0.563、0.467 和 0.206。

Conclusion: 紧凑型 EEG 特征仍具有显著的神经隐私泄露风险，不能仅因不传输原始信号而视为安全。可采用目的限定的表示学习和显式隐私审计，在维持主要认知任务效用的同时降低身份及人口属性推断能力。

Abstract: Wearable EEG systems may expose sensitive information beyond their intended health function, creating substantial risks to neuroprivacy. In this work, we show that commonly used EEG features can reveal participant identity and demographic attributes in addition to supporting the intended cognitive task. Wearable EEG is increasingly being explored for cognitive monitoring, neurological assessment, and longitudinal digital-health applications, yet many systems assume that transmitting compact spectral or spatial features instead of raw EEG provides sufficient privacy protection. Using EEGMAT as a motivating case study, we find that compact EEG features achieve a balanced accuracy of 0.788 for cognitive-state classification while enabling gender, age, and subject-identity inference with balanced accuracies of 0.858, 0.789, and 0.692, respectively. We further show that privacy-aware representation learning preserves task performance at 0.781 while reducing these inference accuracies to 0.563, 0.467, and 0.206. These findings motivate purpose-limited representations and explicit privacy auditing in wearable neurohealth systems.

</details>


### [48] [Federated Trust for Embodied Robot Capability Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00404)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向机器人能力市场的联邦式信任架构：由每个机器人部署桥维护本地可信签名者目录，并通过Ed25519签名进行离线安装验证，以替代集中式PKI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人集群具有异构监管要求、隔离网络、小规模运维团队及错误信任可能造成物理后果等特点，集中式证书机构和在线透明日志难以适配，因此需要支持本地决策、离线部署和按部署差异化信任的模型。

Method: 系统采用标准Ed25519分离签名和SSH风格信任文件，构建运行时治理层，包括五个子命令的CLI、注册服务器和每个部署桥的安装门禁。安装时通过本地可信目录进行集合成员检查，并使用最小版本固定扩展抵御降级。评估包含80项测试、跨部署信任目录实验、5000次对抗试验，以及与Sigstore-Cosign和python-TUF的同硬件成本比较。

Result: 同一注册流在不同信任目录的部署桥上产生不同安装结论，验证了联邦信任的核心特性。严格模式对恶意发布者、篡改、伪造和已撤销签名者攻击的拦截率均为100%；加入最小版本固定后，降级攻击拦截率为96.6%。单次验证成本介于Sigstore-Cosign和python-TUF之间，单发布者存储开销低于二者。

Conclusion: 联邦式信任将信任决策下沉至各机器人部署，能够在离线、异构和多运维边界环境中实现差异化安装治理。其主要贡献是针对具身机器人集群的架构组合，而非新的密码学原语；实验结果表明该方案在安全性、部署自治性和资源开销之间具有可行平衡。

Abstract: Robot capability marketplaces, the "app store for robot skills," are emerging as the deployment vector for LLM-driven robot fleets. The default cloud-native answer to "is this package safe to install?" is centralised PKI: one certificate authority, one transparency log, one root of trust. We argue this is the wrong model for embodied robot fleets, where operators face heterogeneous regulatory regimes, air-gapped deployments, tiny operator headcounts, and physical-world consequences for trusting the wrong publisher. We present federated trust: each deployed bridge maintains its own local trust directory of acceptable signers; signers identify themselves with a public key embedded in a detached Ed25519 signature envelope; install-time verification is a local set-membership check rather than a network round trip to a certificate authority. The cryptographic primitives are deliberately standard (Ed25519 detached signatures and SSH-style trust files); the contribution is the architectural commitment that this composition fits embodied robot fleets specifically. We implement the model in a runtime governance layer with a five-subcommand CLI, a registry server, a per-bridge install gate, and 80 tests. A multi-deployment evaluation shows the same registry stream producing divergent install verdicts on bridges with different trust directories, the load-bearing design property. Across 5000 adversarial trials, the strict-mode gate rejects 100% of rogue-publisher, tampered, forged, and revoked-signer attacks, and 96.6% of downgrade attempts under a minimum-version pin extension. A same-hardware comparison against Sigstore-Cosign and python-TUF locates federated trust's per-verify cost between the two and its per-publisher storage footprint below both.

</details>


### [49] [Don't Trust the Code, Check Its Effects: Runtime Refinement for Regenerated Systems Code Under an Adversarial Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00430)
*Jinhao Hu,Ashvin Goel,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向不可逆系统副作用和潜在恶意代码生成器的安全架构：生成代码只能进行规划，所有实际效果均由固定且可信的中介执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的系统代码生成依赖可重新执行来验证实现，适用于副作用可恢复且生成器可信的场景。但对于不可逆写入、设备命令等效果，事后重执行无法验证；若生成器具有对抗性，证明还可能因假设失效而静默失败。

Method: 将生成代码与执行权限分离，构建固定的可信参考监视器。生成代码仅依据规范制定计划，中介负责判断计划是否符合规范，并独占执行所有外部效果。论文进一步用六项条件刻画这种中介机制的适用边界：效果可辨识性、规范输入可观测性、可关联性、完备性、结果可枚举性和持久性显式化。

Result: 作者将该架构实例化为面向可再生成设备驱动的参考监视器，并提出“可中介性范围”这一分析框架。六项条件共同决定某类效果能否被可信中介安全地管理；摘要未报告具体实验数据或性能结果。

Conclusion: 在生成器可能不可信且系统效果不可逆的场景中，安全性不应依赖生成代码本身或事后重执行，而应通过剥夺其执行权限、由固定可信中介控制所有效果来保证。该保证可跨实现再生保持，但仅适用于满足六项可中介性条件的效果词汇。

Abstract: Recent work uses large language models to generate systems code from specifications, treating the specification as the durable artifact and the implementation as disposable. Regenerating the implementation specializes it to each workload and device. However, that work lives in a forgiving setting: a component's externally visible effects, its writes and device commands, are recoverable, and the generator is honest, so trust is discharged by re-execution. We target the unforgiving setting: systems code whose effects are irreversible, produced by a generator that may be adversarial. There, re-execution cannot check an effect after the fact, and a proof fails silently when its assumptions do. We take the position that the only safe way to operate here is to deny the generated code the authority to act. The generated code only plans, while a fixed trusted mediator owns every effect and performs one only when the specification would have produced it. Because the guarantee lives in the mediator, not the code, it survives regeneration. We instantiate this as a reference monitor for regenerated device drivers, and characterize the mediability envelope, six conditions on the effect vocabulary: legibility, spec-input observability, correlatability, completeness, outcome enumerability, and explicit durability. They decide when such mediation is possible.

</details>


### [50] [Capability-Gated Language Models: Security Composes, Utility Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00445)
*Patrikas Vanagas,Augustas Mačijauskas,Laurynas Lopata*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“能力门控部署”框架：在同一组模型权重内部，按主体实施不同访问配置，并将配置组织为格结构。实验显示，限制配置的“交”通常会增强抑制效果，但效用损失可能在组合后累积，且缺乏通用的组合性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署中的安全微调、过滤与遗忘等机制通常通过模型外部的过滤器、服务层级或重新发布的模型版本实现差异化控制；同一权重内的所有请求仍面对相同配置。作者希望在不复制模型权重的前提下，实现按用户或主体区分的模型能力访问控制，并研究此类配置组合时的安全性与效用性质。

Method: 作者将每个主体的访问配置表示为格中的元素：交运算累积限制，连接运算汇聚多个主体可达的能力范围。实现上，方法在既有嵌套分解机制上引入稀疏秩门控，并利用单次归因指导配置搜索。评估采用预注册的独立留出集，且每项结果仅读取一次以减少重复评估带来的偏差。理论上，作者在单调诱导假设下讨论交运算的安全组合性质，同时通过逐点检验尝试证伪该假设。

Result: 在两个模型谱系中，留出集结果的中位数表明，配置交运算通常会加深能力抑制；经多重比较校正后仍显著的效应也支持这一结论。但效用不具备同样的组合稳定性：单独看似无害的配置在组合后可能造成知识保留能力和文本流畅性下降，且作者未找到可保证效用的通用组合界。

Conclusion: 能力门控为单权重模型中的按主体差异化访问控制提供了结构化方案，并显示限制的交运算具有一定安全增强潜力。然而，安全结论依赖于需要经验检验的单调性条件，而效用在配置组合下可能发生不可预测的退化。因此，该框架适合用于精细化能力控制，但实际部署需对组合配置进行独立效用验证，不能仅依据各单独配置的表现推断组合结果。

Abstract: Deployed language model safeguards (safety fine-tuning, filtering, unlearning) vary by principal only outside the model weights: filters are reconfigured, tiers are multiplied, and artefacts are reissued; inside one set of weights every request meets the same model configuration. This motivates us to define capability-gated deployment: per-principal access control inside one set of weights, whose configurations form a lattice - meets accumulate a principal's restrictions and joins pool a coalition's reach. We instantiate it by sparse rank gating over an existing nested-factorisation mechanism, guide profile search with one-pass attribution, and read every result once from a pre-registered held-out split. Security composes: provably at meets under a monotone-elicitation assumption we falsify pointwise. In two lineages the median held-out meet deepens suppression; the one effect surviving correction strengthens it. Utility does not: individually harmless profiles can compose to retention and fluency damage, and no compositional bound exists.

</details>


### [51] [Does Reasoning Mitigate Backdoor Attacks? A Neuro-Symbolic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00464)
*Marco Antonio Corallo,Andrea Agiollo,Mauro Conti,Alberto Giaretta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估神经符号人工智能（NeSy）模型在后门攻击下的鲁棒性，发现其平均表现优于纯神经网络，但鲁棒性高度依赖推理约束的严格程度及其与攻击目标的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeSy模型通常因具备符号推理能力而被视为具有更高可信性和天然鲁棒性，但神经感知与符号推理之间的集成过程也可能引入新的攻击入口。因此，有必要系统研究其在对抗环境下的安全性。

Method: 以DeepProbLog这一代表性NeSy框架为主要研究对象，并与基线神经网络进行比较；在4类推理任务和8种后门攻击设置下开展实验，分析推理约束与攻击目标之间的关系。

Result: 实验表明，NeSy模型整体上比对应的纯神经网络更具后门鲁棒性。然而，这种优势并非稳定一致，其防御能力会显著受到推理过程严格程度以及推理约束与攻击目标兼容性的影响。

Conclusion: NeSy模型并非天然安全，其符号推理机制既可能增强鲁棒性，也可能形成新的攻击面。未来应将对抗鲁棒性作为NeSy模型设计与评估的重要维度，并针对不同推理约束系统优化防护策略。

Abstract: Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) AI has recently emerged as a novel paradigm to enable trustworthy AI, aiming at integrating sub-symbolic neural perception with grounded symbolic reasoning. The neuro-symbolic integration process that characterizes these models has been proven beneficial to achieve more transparent, explainable and efficient AI systems. Meanwhile, their properties under adversarial settings have been overlooked being frequently deemed robust-by-design. However, the neural-symbolic integration process they leverage constitutes an additional layer of complexity that may provide an attack entry-point. Therefore, in this paper, we claim that an in-depth investigation of the adversarial robustness of NeSy models is necessary and provide the first systematic evaluation of backdoor attacks against NeSy. To this end, we compare the most popular NeSy framework, namely DeepProbLog, against baseline neural networks across a total of eight backdoor settings and four reasoning tasks. Our experimental results show that while NeSy models are indeed more robust than their neural counterpart on average, their robustness vastly depend on the strictness of the reasoning process being enforced and its compatibility with the chosen adversarial target. The source code to reproduce our experiments is made available at https://github.com/marcoantoniocorallo/NeSy-Backdoor.

</details>


### [52] [GlitchLab: A Hardware-in-the-Loop Optimizer for Physical Fault Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00502)
*Tanvir Hossain,Abhinav Mahadevan,Jasper Van Woudenberg,Rajesh Velegalati,Arindam Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GlitchLab 是一个面向物理故障注入测试的硬件在环在线搜索平台，通过强化学习、结构化多臂老虎机和基于模型的自适应策略，在有限测试时间内更高效地发现并复现目标故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理故障注入的参数空间庞大，延迟、电压和脉冲时长等变量相互作用；有效故障点稀疏且依赖目标设备；单次硬件测试反馈有限。因此，传统搜索在固定测试预算下难以高效评估设备的故障敏感性。

Method: 平台将延迟建模为时序门控变量，将电压和脉冲持续时间作为故障强度控制变量，并把硬件执行结果表示为结构化反馈。其实现三类策略：基于 Q-learning 的强化学习策略 RL-Q、用于故障发现的结构化 bandit 策略，以及用于故障复现的模型驱动策略 SOBAS。

Result: 在 AES、密码校验和控制流三类实验中，两种主要策略均发现了目标故障。相较基线方法，在 AES 与控制流任务中，所需尝试次数减少 2–85 倍、测试时间减少 26–1,237 倍；在密码任务中，两种策略成功，而基线在 5,000 次尝试内未成功。发现后，SOBAS 的故障复现频率提高 7.3–21 倍；RL-Q 则识别出多 30% 的不同 AES 有效参数设置。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用结构化硬件反馈进行在线自适应搜索，可显著提升物理故障注入测试的发现效率与复现能力。RL-Q 更适合扩大有效参数配置的覆盖范围，SOBAS 更适合稳定复现已发现的故障；二者可形成发现与复现阶段互补的测试流程。

Abstract: Physical fault injection can turn brief hardware disturbances into security failures such as key recovery, authentication bypass, and unintended control flow. Finding effective faults is difficult because many interacting parameters create a large search space, successful settings are sparse and target-dependent, and each hardware attempt provides limited feedback. Under fixed testing time, efficient search is therefore critical for assessing fault sensitivity.
  We present GlitchLab, an online hardware-in-the-loop platform that treats delay as a timing gate, voltage and pulse duration as severity controls, and hardware outcomes as structured feedback. It implements RL-Q (Q-learning-based reinforcement learning), a structured bandit for discovery, and Structured-Outcome-Based Adaptive Search (SOBAS), a model-based policy for fault reproduction.
  Both policies find a target fault in every AES, password, and control-flow campaign. On AES and control flow, they require 2-85x fewer attempts and 26-1,237x less time than the baselines; on password, both succeed while the baselines fail within 5,000 attempts. After discovery, SOBAS reproduces faults 7.3-21x more often, while RL-Q identifies 30% more distinct AES settings.

</details>


### [53] [The Safeguard Worked. Is the LLM System Safer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00519)
*Pingyu Wu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出，LLM防护措施的拒答率、攻击成功率和政策违规率只能反映局部测试表现，不能直接说明部署系统对持续适应或绕过防护的攻击者还提供多少有害帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估往往将局部指标提升等同于部署安全性提升，但部署环境中的攻击者会持续调整策略或寻找替代入口，因此需要统一衡量防护措施对整体系统残余风险的实际影响。

Method: 将不同防护家族的评估结果映射到统一的部署安全标准，并区分正反两类证据：一次成功获得有害帮助的攻击即可证明残余风险存在，而要证明残余风险很小，则必须进一步考察防护执行局部功能后，周边系统仍允许什么行为。

Result: 编码记录中反复出现成功获取有害帮助的攻击案例；相比之下，只有少数深度编码主张提供了能够支持或推导低残余风险的系统性证据，其中仅有一项主张对其限定范围内的残余风险进行了约束。

Conclusion: 更高的局部防护分数并不自动意味着更安全的部署系统。防护研究应从单纯提升拒答或降低攻击成功率，转向验证这些改进是否真正减少了部署环境中的整体残余风险。

Abstract: Safeguards in deployed LLM services are evaluated by refusal, attack success, and policy violation rates. Those rates characterize how a control performed on the requests it was tested on. A deployment has to answer a different question: how much help with harmful tasks the service still gives an attacker who keeps adapting or finds another way in. We determine what each reported result implies for that question, allowing results from different safeguard families to be compared under one deployment criterion. The evidence requirements are strongly asymmetric. One attack that obtains harmful help from the deployed service suffices to establish that such help remains, and such attacks appear repeatedly in the coded record. Establishing that little remains cannot follow from the safeguard's own numbers alone; it also requires evidence about what the surrounding system still allows after the safeguard performs its local function. Such evidence is supported or derived in only a small minority of the depth-coded claims, and one such claim bounds its scoped residual. A better local score is therefore not, by itself, a stronger claim about the deployment. Safeguard research cannot stop at raising local scores; a gain has to be judged by whether it makes a deployed system any safer.

</details>


### [54] [Transferable End-to-End Optimization for Indirect Long-Term Memory Poisoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00523)
*Chuanchao Zang,Jianing Wang,Wenyu Chen,Xiangtao Meng,Li Wang,Xinyu Gao,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出PipePoison，将间接记忆投毒建模为覆盖“写入—检索—利用”全链路的联合优化问题，而非分别优化各阶段；实验显示其在多种智能体框架、记忆机制、未知配置及代表性防御下均具有更高的攻击利用成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备长期记忆的LLM智能体可能将不可信外部内容沉淀为持久记忆，进而影响后续决策。现有方法通常孤立地优化记忆写入、检索或利用中的单一阶段，忽略阶段之间的输入输出依赖和目标冲突：某一阶段的改进可能降低后续阶段效果，上游转换也可能抹除为下游设计的特征。因此，需要以端到端成功为目标进行协同优化。

Method: PipePoison利用本地影子系统收集各阶段的细粒度反馈；以链式结构损失刻画写入、检索与利用之间的依赖关系，从而识别限制整体成功率的瓶颈阶段并进行针对性优化；同时通过稳定性校准，为不同阶段及系统配置分配权重，以减少对特定本地环境的过拟合并提升向未知受害配置的迁移能力。

Result: 在3类智能体框架和4种记忆机制上的评估中，PipePoison将攻击利用率提高19.1个百分点；在完全未知的受害配置上，较最强基线高16个百分点；在8种代表性防御条件下仍保持有效性。结果支持端到端联合优化优于阶段隔离式优化的核心论断。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，长期记忆安全风险的关键不只是单个模块是否可被影响，而是记忆内容能否跨越写入、检索和利用的完整链路并稳定产生决策影响。PipePoison以链式损失和稳定性加权处理跨阶段耦合与迁移问题，揭示了现有记忆型智能体应加强端到端安全评估、记忆来源可信度控制、检索隔离及利用阶段约束。

Abstract: Long-term memory can turn untrusted external content into persistent influence over an LLM agent's future decisions, creating the threat of indirect memory poisoning. A successful attack must survive a multi-stage pipeline comprising memory writing, retrieval, and utilization. Existing attacks largely rely on intra-stage optimization, optimizing individual stages in isolation while overlooking inter-stage coupling. Specifically, these stages impose different requirements on the same poisoning content, and each stage operates on the transformed output of its predecessor. Consequently, optimizing one stage may undermine the effectiveness of other stages, while upstream transformations may erase improvements intended for downstream stages. Indirect memory poisoning should therefore be viewed as an end-to-end optimization problem. Based on this insight, we present \textsc{PipePoison}, which collects fine-grained stage feedback from local shadow systems, uses chain-structured losses to identify and optimize the stage bottlenecking end-to-end success, and applies stability-calibrated stage and configuration weights to improve transferability. Across three agent frameworks and four memory mechanisms, \textsc{PipePoison} improves attack utilization rate by 19.1 percentage points. Even on fully unseen victim configurations, it outperforms the strongest baseline by 16 percentage points and remains effective under eight representative defenses.

</details>


### [55] [SoK: When Safe Agents Fail Together: The Security of Multi Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00595)
*Rui Yang,Junjie Xu,Zhengyu Liu,Neil Fendley,Yang Hong,Ziyang Li,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对多智能体大语言模型系统（MAS）的安全研究进行执行级系统化分析，提出A-I-R攻击分类框架和五部分防御契约，强调应端到端追踪攻击路径并评估系统级影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAS会在不同主体边界之间传递信息、状态、决策与权限，局部安全检查可能无法发现跨智能体的系统性风险。现有研究较为分散，且缺乏能够区分真实多智能体效应与一般执行问题的统一分析视角。

Method: 分析197篇相关研究，覆盖6类交互接口、4类对手位置、7类系统级风险和8条常见攻击路径；提出由对手位置、交互接口和系统级风险构成的A-I-R框架；以路径目标、观测、干预、信任边界和恢复五个要素组织防御；进一步审查44项评测与基准研究。

Result: 研究统一了分散的MAS攻击机制，指出防御中的关键难点是实现攻击路径闭合与有效恢复。对评测工作的审查发现，当前仍缺乏隔离交互效应、设计可比较且具诊断性的指标、支持不同MAS设计间复用，以及评估开放系统运行能力的方法。

Conclusion: MAS安全应从交互感知和执行链路角度进行评估：需要端到端追踪攻击路径，验证防御是否真正阻断路径，并通过恰当的反事实设计衡量系统级安全效果。

Abstract: Safe agents can fail together. Multi-agent LLM systems (MAS) move information, state, decisions, and authority across principal boundaries, creating failures that local checks may miss. Without an execution-level view, a multi-agent setting can easily be mistaken for evidence of a genuinely multi-agent security effect. We thus systematize MAS security through an execution-centered analysis of 197 works, covering six interaction interfaces, four adversary positions, seven system-level risks, and eight recurring attack paths. We introduce an A-I-R framework that organizes attacks by adversary position, interaction interface, and resulting system-level risk, unifying otherwise fragmented attack mechanisms across MAS. We organize defenses through a five-part contract covering path target, observation, intervention, trust boundary, and recovery, and identify path closure and recovery as key challenges. We audit 44 evaluation and benchmark works and identify open challenges in isolating interaction effects, designing comparable and diagnostic metrics, supporting reuse across MAS designs, and evaluating open-system operation. Together, these findings motivate an interaction-aware view of MAS security: trace attacks end to end, test whether defenses close those paths, and evaluate system-level effects with appropriate counterfactuals.

</details>


### [56] [NeuroGraph: An AI Graph-Driven Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Explainable Threat Reasoning in Advanced Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00604)
*Padmeswari Nandiya,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Iqbal H. Sarker,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向先进制造业信息技术与运营技术环境的图谱支撑神经符号威胁情报分析框架。该框架以本体约束的图查询检索为事实基础，再由语言模型生成答案，以提升复杂关联威胁分析的准确性、可解释性与抗幻觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于文本的检索增强生成方法难以对异构威胁实体及其关系进行结构化、多跳推理，且可能产生幻觉。现有图检索增强生成方法又常缺少本体一致性、可执行的符号推理路径和端到端证据追溯机制，因此难以满足先进制造环境中可验证威胁分析的需求。

Method: 框架采用双大语言模型架构：第一模型将自然语言问题转换为符合领域本体的可执行Cypher查询，用于在知识图谱中实施符号化检索；第二模型仅依据检索到的图谱证据生成回答。该设计将自然语言理解、知识图谱多跳查询、本体约束和受证据限制的答案生成结合，并输出可供分析人员核验的图谱推理工件。

Result: 在公开网络威胁情报基准上的实验表明，该方法相较已发表基线在推理准确率方面具有稳定提升；同时表现出更低的幻觉倾向、更强的多跳推理能力及对对抗性扰动的更高鲁棒性。运行时间分析显示其可维持交互式推理性能，可解释性分析则表明其能够展示从查询到证据再到结论的推理过程。

Conclusion: 图谱支撑的神经符号方法能够以本体一致的符号检索约束语言生成，并提供可审计的证据链，因此是构建可扩展、可靠且可解释的先进制造威胁情报系统的有前景路径。摘要的主要局限是未给出具体数据集规模、指标数值、基线细节及失败案例，实际泛化能力和部署成本仍需结合全文进一步评估。

Abstract: The growing complexity of cyber-physical attack surfaces in advanced manufacturing has made cyber threat intelligence analysis increasingly difficult. Although large language models and retrieval-augmented generation have improved CTI workflows, text-based approaches remain vulnerable to hallucinations and provide limited support for structured reasoning over interconnected threats. Graph-based RAG reduces some of these limitations, but existing approaches often lack ontology-consistent multi-hop reasoning and transparent evidence tracing across heterogeneous cybersecurity data. This paper proposes a graph-grounded neuro-symbolic framework that integrates ontology-aware symbolic query generation, knowledge graph retrieval, and neural language generation to support accurate and explainable threat analysis across information technology and operational technology environments. The framework adopts a dual-large language model architecture: the first model translates natural-language questions into executable Cypher queries for symbolic graph retrieval, while the second generates answers strictly from the retrieved graph evidence. Experimental evaluation using publicly available cyber threat intelligence benchmarks shows consistent improvements over the published baseline in reasoning accuracy, while also reducing hallucinations, strengthening multi-hop reasoning, and improving robustness to adversarial perturbations. Runtime and explainability analyses further demonstrate that the framework maintains interactive inference performance and exposes graph-grounded reasoning artifacts that allow analysts to inspect and verify each stage of the analysis. Overall, the results highlight the potential of graph-grounded neuro-symbolic reasoning as a scalable, interpretable, and reliable approach to cyber threat intelligence for next-generation Industry 5.0 environments.

</details>


### [57] [A Version Space Approach for Digital Circuit Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00609)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出统一的“版本空间”框架：将与已观测结果一致的候选对象集合进行精确计数，并以其对数刻画剩余不确定性。该框架被用于概率组合等价检查和逻辑锁定网表的密钥计数，声称均可避免原有方法中的指数枚举或独立性近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字电路分析中的许多任务都可表述为：给定观测结果后，仍有多少候选对象与之相容，以及下一步应选择何种观测。已有关于修改 Haar 谱系数的工作仅解决了两个特殊计数情形，一般情形需随观测数指数增长的枚举；逻辑锁定分析中，标称密钥位数也未必等于实际剩余密钥不确定性。因此需要一种可精确计算候选空间规模的统一方法。

Method: 对于概率组合等价检查，论文将布尔函数候选与修改 Haar 谱系数观测建立对应，通过重参数化为块和，将嵌套谱系数之间的全局依赖转化为局部结构；随后使用和-积递归精确计算满足观测约束的函数数量。文中进一步给出单个系数、系数对及任意祖先闭合集合的闭式表达，并将独立性近似误差表述为可计算的格指数。对于逻辑锁定网表，论文将候选对象替换为密钥、观测替换为预言机查询响应，并在门级因子图上运行同类递归，以计算与查询集合一致的密钥数量。

Result: 在谱系数计数问题上，方法将原先关于观测数量的指数枚举改为关于真值表规模的多项式时间精确计数；所有公式均通过穷举验证，并复现了早期工作的表格结果。在逻辑锁定实验中，作者对 TrustHub 混淆基准中的七十个实例进行分析，发现每个实例在给定查询后得到的剩余版本空间熵均低于其宣传的密钥长度。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将两个通常被视为不同的问题统一为版本空间计数：观测由见证或预言机提供，每条观测排除部分候选，剩余候选数可通过结构化递归精确求得。该结论表明，标称参数规模，例如密钥长度，不能单独代表真实不确定性；精确版本空间计数可提供更可靠的安全性与等价性评估。

Abstract: Many questions about a digital circuit take the same form. A hidden object is consistent with a set of observations, and one wants to know how many remain consistent and which observation to make next. The set of surviving candidates is the version space, and its size, on a logarithmic scale, measures how much the observations have settled. This paper develops the version-space view as one method and applies it to two circuit-analysis problems usually treated as unrelated. The first is probabilistic combinational equivalence checking, where the candidates are Boolean functions and the observations are modified-Haar spectral coefficients. A method proposed in 2002 posed this counting problem and solved only two special cases, leaving the general case an enumeration exponential in the number of observations. We close it. A reparameterization onto block sums turns the dependence among nested coefficients into locality, a sum--product recursion counts the surviving functions exactly in time polynomial in the truth-table size, closed forms follow for a single coefficient, a coefficient pair, and every ancestor-closed set, and the error of the independence approximation the 2002 work resorted to equals a computable lattice index. Every formula is checked against exhaustive enumeration and reproduces the 2002 tables. The second application is key counting for logic-locked netlists, where the candidates are keys and the observations are oracle responses. The same recursion, run over the gate-level factor graph, computes the number of keys still consistent with a set of queries; across seventy instances of the TrustHub obfuscation release the surviving entropy falls below the advertised key length every time. The two applications are one method: a witness supplies observations, each removes candidates, and the version space is counted exactly.

</details>


### [58] [Automating Static Code Analysis Through CI/CD Pipeline Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00676)
*Zachary Wadhams,Ann Marie Reinhold,Clemente Izurieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化流程，将静态应用安全测试（SAST）工具的输出聚合并集成到开发者熟悉的问题跟踪系统中，以提升漏洞发现、沟通和修复效率。基于SonarQube与GitLab的实现表明，该流程有助于增强安全意识和漏洞响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具能够在开发早期识别安全漏洞，但高误报率、缺乏原生流水线支持以及与开发工作流衔接不足，限制了其广泛应用。因此，研究旨在降低工具使用复杂度并改善漏洞信息在开发团队中的传递与处理。

Method: 设计一种通用、自动化的SAST结果聚合流程，将不同扫描结果进行统一处理，并集成至开发者现有的问题跟踪软件。研究使用SonarQube作为SAST工具，在基于GitLab的开发环境中实施和验证该流程，同时收集开发者对结构化实现、实时反馈和工作流影响的评价。

Result: 实际实施显示，该流程能够将漏洞信息更有效地传递给开发者，支持实时反馈和主动式漏洞管理。开发者总体评价积极，但也指出了潜在的学习成本，以及安全编码要求与开发流程连续性之间的权衡。摘要未提供定量指标或对照实验结果。

Conclusion: 将SAST结果自动聚合并集成到现有问题跟踪系统，具有改善漏洞修复效率、提升安全意识和增强软件开发安全性的潜力。后续研究应进一步降低学习门槛、减少误报，并通过定量实验验证其对修复时效和开发效率的实际影响。

Abstract: In the contemporary landscape of software devel-opment, securing sensitive data is paramount to safeguarding organizational reputation, preventing financial losses, and pro-tecting individuals from identity theft. This paper addresses the pervasive challenge of identifying and rectifying security vulnerabilities early in the development process, emphasizing the role of Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools. While SAST tools play a crucial role in detecting vulnerabilities, widespread adoption has been hindered by usability issues, including high false positive rates and a lack of native pipeline support. This paper proposes a novel, generalized, and automated process for aggregating SAST tool outputs and integrating them into developers' familiar issue-tracking software. The process streamlines the identification and communication of security vulnerabilities during the development lifecycle, facilitating more efficient remediation efforts. We demonstrate the successful implementation of the proposed process with the SonarQube SAST tool in a GitLab-based development environment. Developers were positive about the structured implementation, real-time feedback, and proactive vulnerability management. However, despite some challenges such as a potential learning curve and tradeoffs between secure coding and workflow disruption, the overall positive impact on security awareness and responsiveness suggests that the proposed process holds promise in enhancing the security posture of software development practices

</details>


### [59] [PhantomCall: Evading ML Malware Detectors via Function Call Graph Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00705)
*Md Ajwad Akil,Adrian Shuai Li,Imtiaz Karim,Arun Iyengar,Ashish Kundu,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Prior adversarial attacks on Windows PE malware detectors target raw bytes, PE headers, or intra-function control-flow graphs, leaving the function call graph (FCG) unexplored as an attack surface. Yet the FCG structure is an important feature in graph-based malware detectors. We present Phan- tomCall, a black-box attack that perturbs the FCG of Windows PE malware by injecting fully executable dummy functions at targeted call sites, adding new nodes and edges to both the CFG and FCG while preserving program semantics. We pair this structural perturbation with classifier-guided search and tunable injection parameters, effective across three archi- tecturally distinct classifiers. Evaluated on a 2025-collected Windows malware corpus against MalConv (raw-byte CNN), MalGraph (graph-based GNN), and SAFE+GNN (pure FCG GNN trained from scratch on a 2024 corpus) at two FPR thresholds, the best PhantomCall variant achieves 85-100% attack success rate across all configurations, exceeding prior state-of-the-art by up to 14.78 percentage points on MalGraph and 95.5 percentage points on SAFE+GNN, and generating evasive variants up to 2.9x faster on average across all targets. For MalConv and MalGraph, the majority of evasions require only a single call site modification, and 86-97% of evaluated evasive variants preserve the original malicious behavior in sandbox-based semantic testing across all configurations.

</details>


### [60] [Differentially Private Paired Table-Image Multimodal Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00708)
*Kai Chen,Josephine Lamp,Somesh Jha,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出DP-TabImage，用于在差分隐私约束下合成“图像—多变量表格记录”配对数据。方法通过分别建模表格边缘分布与以表格为条件的图像分布，并采用私有原型预训练改善跨模态学习，在三个真实数据集上取得了表格保真度、图像保真度和跨模态一致性之间较好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私合成研究主要分别面向表格或图像数据，而现实数据常同时包含图像及其关联属性。该问题的难点在于：表格与图像适合不同的隐私学习机制；同时，合成结果还必须保留两种模态之间的条件依赖与语义对应关系。直接在DP-SGD的梯度裁剪和噪声扰动下训练条件图像模型，容易削弱图像质量及跨模态对齐。

Method: 方法将联合分布分解为p(x,y)=p_T(y)p_I(x|y)。首先，使用私有概率图模型学习多变量表格分布p_T(y)；随后，使用以表格属性为条件、通过DP-SGD训练的扩散模型学习图像条件分布p_I(x|y)。为缓解私有条件训练的困难，论文构造私有表格—图像原型：利用属性条件图像与由已私有化表格模型生成的表格向量配对进行预训练。由于表格向量来自已完成隐私保护的模型，该步骤不引入额外隐私成本。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的实验表明，DP-TabImage能够同时维持较高的表格统计保真度、图像视觉保真度以及图像与表格属性之间的对应关系。消融分析显示，仅进行视觉预热主要提升图像边缘质量；采用已对齐的表格—图像原型进行预热，则对提升跨模态对应性更为关键。

Conclusion: DP-TabImage为差分隐私配对多模态数据合成提供了模块化方案：以适合表格的私有概率图模型生成条件变量，以适合图像的私有扩散模型生成图像，并通过不额外消耗隐私预算的对齐原型预训练强化条件关联。其核心贡献在于兼顾模态专用建模与跨模态依赖保留；但摘要未说明具体隐私预算、数据集规模及与基线方法的定量差距，这些仍需结合全文进一步评估。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) synthesis has been extensively studied for tabular and image data separately, yet many real-world datasets contain images paired with multivariate tabular records. Synthesizing such data is particularly challenging under DP, as the two modalities favor different private learning mechanisms while their dependence must also be preserved. To address this challenge, we propose DP-TabImage, a modality-specialized framework for private paired synthesis. DP-TabImage instantiates the factorization $p(x,y)=p_T(y)p_I(x\;|\;y)$ using a private Probabilistic Graphical Model for the multivariate table distribution and a table-conditioned diffusion model trained with DP-SGD for the conditional image distribution. To facilitate conditional learning under clipped and noisy gradients, we further pretrain the model on private table-image prototypes, pairing privately constructed attribute-conditioned images with tabular vectors derived from the already private tabular model at no additional privacy cost. Experiments on three real-world datasets show that DP-TabImage achieves a strong balance among tabular fidelity, image fidelity, and cross-modal alignment. Our analysis further reveals that visual warm-up primarily improves marginal image fidelity, whereas aligned table-image warm-up is critical for improving cross-modal correspondence. Our source code is available in the GitHub repository, https://github.com/KaiChen9909/TabImage_Syn.

</details>


### [61] [SoK: Motion Data Privacy in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00711)
*Azim Ibragimov,Alina Vasina,Uliana Polshcha,Eric D. Ragan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于XR运动隐私的系统化知识综述（SoK）。作者审查了134篇相关论文，系统梳理XR头显记录的用户运动数据所带来的隐私风险、攻击路径与防护机制，并提出分类体系、威胁模型和未来评估建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR的沉浸式交互依赖对头部、手部及身体运动的持续追踪，但运动轨迹可能暴露用户可识别特征及其他敏感属性。既有XR隐私综述通常将运动问题分散在多个领域讨论；随着相关研究数量显著增长，作者认为有必要对XR运动隐私进行专门、统一的系统化整理。

Method: 论文以134篇XR运动隐私研究为对象进行文献综述与归纳，重点分析三类问题：运动模式如何被获取、基于这些模式能够推断何种信息、可采用哪些防护方法。在此基础上，作者构建运动模态、数据表示和推断风险的分类法，提出XR运动威胁模型，并对已有攻击与防御方案进行系统化比较，同时识别研究空白。

Result: 综述表明，XR运动数据可由头显及相关交互设备持续采集，并可被用于多种隐私推断。现有研究已覆盖不同运动信号、特征表示、推断攻击和保护技术，但各类方法的威胁假设、评估指标、数据集条件及效用—隐私权衡仍缺乏统一规范。论文据此归纳了当前研究版图，并指出若干尚未充分探索的方向。

Conclusion: XR运动隐私已构成独立且快速发展的研究议题。作者认为，未来工作应采用更清晰的威胁模型、更一致的评估协议以及同时衡量隐私保护效果与XR交互可用性的实验设计，以促进可比较、可复现且具有实际部署价值的运动隐私机制。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) provides immersive, interactive 3D experiences. To enable these experiences, the devices must track user motion so the system can respond to actions such as grabbing, looking at, or moving an object. However, motion tracking has raised privacy concerns since it records a person's motion patterns. These motion patterns have been studied extensively across various fields (i.e., gait identification and profiling) and have been shown to reveal sensitive information. With the adoption of XR, these patterns became easier to record and obtain than ever. This creates a fundamental privacy tension: motion tracking enables core XR functionality yet requires users to compromise their privacy. Prior systematization-of-knowledge (SoK) studies on XR privacy have examined the field broadly, with motion-related research distributed across several privacy domains rather than treated as a distinct area of study. However, XR motion privacy has gained significant momentum since the prior SoK, with the literature nearly quadrupling in size and thereby warranting a dedicated systematization of this topic. This SoK examines 134 relevant papers on privacy concerns in motion patterns recorded by XR headsets, including how adversaries can obtain users' motion patterns, the inferences they can draw from them, and methods for protecting users. Based on this review, we synthesize a taxonomy of motion modalities, representations, and inference risks; develop an XR motion threat model; systematize the attack and defense approaches in the XR motion literature; identify gaps in the literature; and provide guidelines for future studies evaluating motion privacy mechanisms. Together, our SoK clarifies the state of XR motion privacy and provides recommendations for future evaluations.

</details>


### [62] [MROP: Mask-Region Optimized Purification Against Backdoor Attack in Deep JSCC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00786)
*Seongkyu Yang,Hyeonho Noh,Hyun Jong Yang,Jonggyu Jang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究深度联合信源信道编码（Deep JSCC）图像传输中的输入补丁后门防御，提出无需重训练、仅在推理阶段运行的掩码区域优化净化方法（MROP），以降低攻击成功率并尽量保持正常图像重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep JSCC通过端到端神经网络直接将图像映射为信道符号，接收端的重建过程缺乏可解释性。当输入图像被植入小型触发补丁时，解码器可能输出预设目标图像，而现有补丁触发防御大多面向分类任务，难以直接适用于以像素级重建为目标的Deep JSCC场景。

Method: 作者先将梯度掩码防御适配为重建任务基线：依据输入—输出梯度定位可疑触发区域。随后提出MROP：在编码器输入端设置逐像素掩码，利用Gumbel-Sigmoid连续松弛对掩码进行优化，从而定位疑似触发补丁；再对定位区域进行细化与净化，以改善纯净图像的重建效果。该方法不修改或重新训练既有JSCC模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和STL-10数据集上，作者针对DeepJSCC与SwinJSCC进行数值评估。结果表明，MROP能够显著降低攻击成功率（ASR），同时较好维持干净输入图像重建的峰值信噪比（PSNR）。摘要未提供具体的ASR下降幅度、PSNR数值、计算开销或不同攻击条件下的细粒度结果。

Conclusion: MROP为Deep JSCC重建系统提供了一种面向补丁后门的推理期防御方案。其核心贡献在于以可优化的输入掩码替代基于输入—输出梯度的简单定位，并通过区域细化兼顾防御效果与重建保真度；其实际适用性仍需结合完整实验中的攻击泛化性、超参数敏感性和推理成本进一步验证。

Abstract: Deep joint source and channel coding (JSCC) transmits a source by mapping it directly to channel symbols through an end-to-end deep neural network (DNN) and reconstructing it at the receiver. Taking image transmission as an application, this DNN pipeline behaves as a black box: the receiver cannot readily detect security attacks when the transmitted images are corrupted, thereby introducing a new security vulnerability. In this letter, we study defense against input-patch backdoor attacks on deep JSCC, in which a small trigger patch attached to the input forces the decoder to emit an attacker-chosen target image. Most existing patch-trigger defenses are designed for classification, leaving the reconstruction setting of deep JSCC unaddressed. We adapt the gradient mask defense to this reconstruction setting as a baseline and then propose mask-region optimized purification (MROP), which operates at inference and requires no retraining of the JSCC model. Unlike the baseline, which localizes the trigger from the input--output gradient, MROP instead places a per-pixel mask at the encoder input and optimizes it via a Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation to localize the trigger, then refines the trigger region to reconstruct the pure images better. In numerical results, we evaluate the proposed method on CIFAR-10 and STL-10 datasets along with the DeepJSCC and SwinJSCC models. By doing so, we show that the proposed method substantially lowers the attack success rate (ASR) while preserving the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of clean reconstructions.

</details>


### [63] [RISA: Response Inspection and Selective Actions for Refusal Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00790)
*Wenhan Chang,Tianqing Zhu,Ping Xiong,Shiyi Liao,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RISA是一种无需更新模型参数的推理时拒答校准框架，通过检查初始回答并选择性干预，提升有害请求拒答的可靠性，同时尽量保留正常回答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法常在未判断初始回答是否正确的情况下直接干预，可能改变合理拒答或有用回答；固定关键词规则又难以覆盖语义变化，且不同基础模型之间适配性有限。

Method: RISA首先利用固定上下文规则为明确样本计算拒答分数；对规则未覆盖的样本，从模型最后一层的提示隐藏状态中提取表示，并通过校准线性探针预测拒答分数。针对不同基础模型，分别校准探针分数、表示支持边界和动作阈值。运行时结合提示分数与初始回答的拒答状态，通过动作策略仅在必要时执行干预。

Result: 实验表明，RISA能够提高模型拒答行为的可靠性，并在较大程度上保持模型效用，说明其对不同基础模型具有较好的适配能力和实用价值。

Conclusion: 通过响应感知的初始回答检查、模型自适应评分和选择性动作决策，RISA在无需重新训练基础模型的条件下有效减少拒答错误，为推理时安全行为校准提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Reliable refusal behavior requires Large Language Models (LLMs) to reject harmful prompts with only answering benign ones. Incorrect refusal behavior can either expose users to harmful responses or prevent users from obtaining useful answers. Training-time alignment improves refusal behavior by updating model parameters with safety data, but requires additional computation and training. In contrast, inference-time alignment aims to modify LLM behavior during inference without updating the underlying model parameters. Existing inference-time methods mainly rely on in-context safety prompting, activation steering, or decoding control. However, most of them intervene without first determining whether the initial response is already appropriate, potentially altering a correct refusal or a useful answer. Effective selective intervention therefore requires identifying prompt intent beyond sensitive keywords, covering semantic variations that fixed rules may miss, and adapting the verifier to different base models. To address these challenges, we propose Response Inspection and Selective Actions (RISA), an inference-time framework that inspects the initial response and selectively corrects refusal errors without updating the base model. RISA first uses fixed contextual rules to assign refusal scores to clear cases. For unmatched cases, it derives a refusal score from the final-layer prompt hidden state using a calibrated linear probe. To adapt to different base models, RISA separately calibrates the probe score, representation-support boundary, and action thresholds. At runtime, RISA combines the prompt score with the initial refusal status and applies an action policy to intervene only when necessary. Experimental results demonstrate that RISA improves refusal reliability while largely preserving model utility, offering a practical solution for response-aware refusal calibration in LLMs.

</details>


### [64] [Effective Interventions Against AI-Enhanced Scams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00806)
*Kyle Fredrickson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究人工智能驱动的诈骗如何改变诈骗运营的经济机制，并分析哪些干预措施仍然有效。模型表明，提高报告率、集中报告以及报告准确性能够产生乘数效应，显著降低单个诈骗渠道的受害者数量和收益，同时提高运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能扩大了诈骗活动的规模与效率，改变了传统诈骗中的运营瓶颈。随着全球及美国诈骗损失快速增长，有必要评估现有干预措施在人工智能驱动环境下的持续有效性。

Method: 作者构建了一个简化的诈骗利润模型，分析不同干预措施对诈骗渠道预期受害者数量、收入和成本的渐近影响，重点考察报告率、报告集中化程度和报告准确性三项机制的联合作用。

Result: 三项干预因素对每个诈骗渠道的预期受害者数量具有乘法抑制效应，可降低单渠道收入并提高诈骗运营成本。即使报告率较低，只要针对高价值诈骗基础设施，也可能显著削弱其盈利能力；同时覆盖三项因素的综合干预预计效果最强。

Conclusion: 在人工智能驱动的诈骗环境中，提升报告率、集中整合报告渠道并提高报告准确性，是削弱诈骗商业模式的重要杠杆。政策和平台治理应优先针对高价值基础设施实施协同干预，以放大各项措施的乘数效应。

Abstract: In 2025, scams were responsible for an estimated $442 billion in direct losses globally. In the United States, reported losses increased by nearly 400% between 2020 and 2025. Though AI in scamming is a relatively new phenomenon, its use significantly changes the economics of scams as well as the bottlenecks in scam operations. In this paper I investigate what interventions will remain effective under this new AI-driven scamming regime.
  I develop a simple model of scam profits to understand how different interventions asymptotically affect scam operations. I find that three levers--reporting rate, centralization of reporting, and report accuracy--multiply in their effect on expected victims per scam channel, reducing revenue per scam channel while increasing costs. Because effects multiply, interventions affecting all three could have a significant effect on the profitability of the scam business model. My analysis suggests that even modest reporting rates against high-value scam infrastructure could have significant impacts on scam profitability.

</details>


### [65] [Using LLMs to Elicit Security Requirements for Service-Oriented Cyber Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00886)
*Michail Takaronis,Athanasia Kollarou,Georgios Kavallieratos,Vasileios Gkioulos,Sokratis Katsikas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究评估五种大语言模型在服务导向网络靶场安全需求获取中的辅助作用。模型共生成84条需求，经整合形成27条，并由5名网络安全专家评审。结果表明，LLM可有效支持需求获取早期工作，但需求可测试性不足，仍需人工审查与细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务导向网络靶场包含多类组件、架构层与利益相关者，安全需求获取通常复杂、耗时且容易遗漏。研究旨在探索大语言模型能否基于任务目标、利益相关者需求和架构指导原则，快速形成可供设计与开发使用的安全需求基线。

Method: 研究采用SEBoK指导的需求获取流程：先识别安全任务目标与利益相关者需求，再将其连同架构指南构造成提示上下文，输入GPT-5.2、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Grok 4.1、Sonar和Kimi K2.5五个模型。模型输出84条安全需求，研究者将其归并为27条综合需求，并映射至服务导向网络靶场的架构层。随后由5名网络安全专家按必要性、清晰性、完整性、可行性、可测试性及拒绝选项进行评估。

Result: 专家评估显示，整合后的需求在必要性方面获得98.5%的高接受度，清晰性为87.4%，完整性为85.2%，可行性为78.5%，拒绝率仅0.7%。可测试性仅为44.4%，显著低于其他指标，说明模型生成的需求虽然大多有价值且表达较清楚，但常缺少验证方法、验收条件或可度量测试信息。

Conclusion: 大语言模型适合用于安全需求获取的前期辅助，可提高初始需求基线的生成效率并降低遗漏风险。其输出不能替代专业人员：尤其应由人工补充需求的验证准则、测试方法、约束条件和架构适配细节，以提升可测试性及工程可落地性。

Abstract: Cyber ranges are complex environments comprising many interacting components and stakeholders with different security concerns. The Service-Oriented Cyber Range (SOR) is no exception, particularly when it comes to training scenarios targeting critical infrastructure. Security concerns are translated into security requirements, the elicitation of which is usually difficult and time-consuming. This work examines how large language models can assist in eliciting security requirements for a service-oriented range and help produce a useful baseline for designers and developers. The approach follows a SEBoK-guided process in which security mission objectives and stakeholder needs were first identified and then provided as a prompt context along with architectural guidelines to five LLMs: GPT-5.2, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Grok 4.1, Sonar, and Kimi K2.5. The models generated 84 security requirements in total, which were consolidated into a comprehensive set of 27 requirements and then mapped to the architectural layers of the service-oriented range. The final set was evaluated by five cybersecurity experts against the criteria of necessity, clarity, completeness, feasibility, and testability, with an additional rejection option. The results showed a high acceptance rate, specifically for necessity with 98.5%, clarity with 87.4%, completeness with 85.2%, feasibility with 78.5%, and rejection with 0.7%. Testability was lower at 44.4%, indicating a slight lack of information on how these requirements could be tested. These findings show that LLMs can support early stages of the elicitation of security requirements, although human review is still needed, especially to improve or adjust certain aspects of the requirements.

</details>


### [66] [Influence of Logging Frameworks on Bind9](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00954)
*Max Schrötter,Hannes Signer,Bettina Schnor*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出主机型入侵防护系统的日志链路会在高速流量下成为瓶颈，并提出高性能进程间通信机制 FIPS，以降低日志丢失并提升防护响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 IPS 依赖应用日志识别异常行为，但高并发请求可压垮日志管线，使关键记录被丢弃。实验表明，Fail2Ban 监控 BIND9 的常见部署在不足 65 Mbps 的 DNS 流量下即可失效；即使采用 eBPF 与 Hyperscan 等高性能组件，日志后端仍会构成主要限制。

Method: 作者设计 FIPS：一种绕过内核、尽量减少日志消息复制的高性能 IPC。其采用每线程无锁共享内存环形缓冲区，允许多个相互独立的消费者以不同速度读取同一日志流；同时提供原生 API 及兼容 syslog 的替代接口。

Result: 在 BIND 9 上的评估显示，FIPS 相比关闭日志时几乎不引入额外开销，记录的请求量高于所有被比较框架；相较文件日志，可使 IPS 对异常客户端的封禁速度提升 2.5 倍，并在每秒 100 万请求、2^16 个攻击客户端的负载下保持运行。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：在高吞吐 IPS 中，日志系统而非检测与过滤组件可能成为决定性瓶颈。FIPS 通过用户态共享内存、无锁设计和多消费者支持，显著提升了日志记录的吞吐与可用性，从而增强了基于日志的实时防护能力。

Abstract: Host-based Intrusion Prevention Systems (IPS) rely on application logs to detect and block malicious activity. However, on modern high-speed networks the logging subsystem itself becomes a bottleneck: an attacker can hide traces simply by generating enough traffic to overwhelm the application's log pipeline, dropping crucial traces. In this work, we show that widely deployed setups such as Fail2Ban monitoring BIND9 can be defeated with less than 65 Mbps of DNS traffic. Further, we show that when replacing core components of the IPS architecture with their higher-performance equivalent, iptables with eBPF and regex matching with Hyperscan, the logging backends themselves become the bottleneck. Therefore, we present FIPS, a new IPC designed for high-performance logging that bypasses the kernel and reduces copying of the log messages to a minimum. FIPS uses per-thread lock free shared memory ring buffers, supporting multiple independent consumers reading the same log stream at their own pace. FIPS offers both a native API and a drop-in replacement for the syslog interface. Our evaluation with BIND 9 shows that FIPS introduces almost no overhead compared to disabled logging, logs more requests than any other evaluated framework, and enables the IPS to ban malicious clients $2.5\times$ faster than with file logging while sustaining $2^{16}$ attacking clients at one million requests per second.

</details>


### [67] [AKRASIA: Stealthy Backdoor Attack on Reasoning-based Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01023)
*Chou Jin Chua,Sarang Nambiar,Murali Srinivasan,Ezekiel Soremekun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AKRASIA，一种针对具备推理能力代码大模型的隐蔽型推理时后门攻击，可在不修改模型参数的情况下诱导模型执行预设恶意目标，同时规避自动防御与人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码大模型后门研究主要关注训练阶段或显式触发器，而推理型模型的思维过程可能掩盖后门行为。本文旨在揭示此类风险并推动针对推理后门的防御研究。

Method: AKRASIA通过探测目标模型构造代码级触发器，利用上下文学习植入后门，并借助模型不忠实性隐藏触发器及推理步骤，使输出呈现合理的推理过程。实验覆盖4类后门目标、6个推理代码大模型、3项代码任务与数据集，以及3种防御方法。

Result: 在不同模型与任务上，平均攻击成功率最高达99.34%，同时保持最高97.23%的平均任务准确率。在18种防御设置中的14种情况下，攻击仍保持最高98.82%的平均成功率；人工审查场景下，触发器和推理步骤在最高80%的设置中被成功隐藏。

Conclusion: 推理能力与模型不忠实性会增加代码大模型后门的隐蔽性。AKRASIA表明，现有自动防御和人工检查仍可能不足，需要发展针对推理过程、上下文触发器及隐性行为的专门检测与防御机制。

Abstract: We present AKRASIA, a stealthy, inference-time backdoor attack against reasoning-based Code LLMs. AKRASIA aims to achieve a backdoor target (e.g., malicious code execution) in reasoning LLMs while evading automated defenses and human inspection. To achieve this, AKRASIA probes the victim LLM to construct a code-level backdoor trigger. It then employs in-context learning for backdoor learning, and model unfaithfulness to conceal the backdoor trigger, and generate plausible reasoning. We evaluate AKRASIA using four backdoor targets six (6) reasoning LLMs, three coding tasks/datasets and three defense methods. AKRASIA has up to 99.34% average attack success rate on SOTA LLMs and mantains up to 97.23% average accuracy. AKRASIA evades the SOTA defense, retaining up to 98.82% average ASR in most (14/18) defense settings. It evades human inspection, successfully hiding the backdoor trigger and reasoning steps in up to 80% of settings. Our findings motivate the need to defend LLMs against reasoning backdoors.

</details>


### [68] [MultiGait: A Multi-Sensor Multi-Perspective Multi-Session Biometric Inference Benchmark and its Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01036)
*Julian Todt,Felix Morsbach,Philip Dissert,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出MultiGait数据集，用于系统评估热成像、深度相机和激光雷达等新型传感器的步态隐私风险。基准实验表明，通常被视为“隐私友好”的传感器仍可能泄露身份信息，且现有方法的跨会话泛化能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型城市感知设备缺乏可用于隐私研究的统一数据集，导致许多关于其隐私保护能力的说法缺少实证依据。作者希望在统一、可比的条件下量化不同传感器及不同视角下的身份推断风险。

Method: 作者采集了MultiGait多传感器、多视角、多会话步态数据集，覆盖199名参与者、多种步行模式及带标注的个人属性。数据集包含8类传感器、4个观测视角和3次录制会话，并通过多种先进识别系统验证其可用性。研究进一步构建身份推断基准，考察单传感器及跨传感器识别、跨视角表现、跨会话泛化，以及边缘端匿名化等研究需求。

Result: 广泛的身份推断实验显示，不同传感器均可能支持显著的身份识别，即使是常被认为隐私风险较低的热成像、深度或激光雷达类传感器也不例外。同时，现有识别方法在不同采集会话之间的性能泛化较差，说明模型容易受时间、环境或采集条件变化影响。

Conclusion: MultiGait为研究新型视觉与距离传感器的隐私泄露风险提供了标准化基础设施。论文的核心结论是：传感器的表面“低可见性”并不等同于低身份泄露风险；未来应重点提升跨会话鲁棒识别评估，并发展面向边缘部署的有效匿名化方法。

Abstract: A lack of suitable datasets has limited the research into the privacy risks of novel smart city sensors, such as thermal cameras, depth cameras, and lidar. Given the number of unsubstantiated privacy claims and their potential widespread deployment into many people's everyday life, understanding the privacy risks of these sensors -- in isolation and in like-for-like comparisons -- is crucial. With MultiGait, we collected the first multi-sensor, multi-perspective, multi-session gait-focused dataset, for the corresponding, and additional more far-reaching investigations. The dataset, validated with multiple state-of-the-art recognition systems, comprises various walking modes and annotated personal attributes for 199 individuals, to ensure the benefit for advanced studies including cross-sensor recognition and anonymization at the edge. MultiGait represents a foundation for rigorous privacy investigations, demonstrated through an extensive identity inference benchmark across eight sensors, four perspectives, and three recording sessions.
