Our benchmark incidentally reveals that sensors often assumed to be privacy-friendly do still entail considerable identity inference risks, while the poor cross-session generalization of existing methods underscores an important research gap.

</details>


### [69] [HiveTraceGuard-Pro: A Compact Generative Guardrail for Prompt Injection, Jailbreaks, and Adversarial Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01046)
*Nikita Oblakov,Sabrina Sadiekh,Evgeniy Kokuykin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HiveTraceGuard-Pro，一款基于Qwen3-0.6B并经LoRA微调的俄英双语生成式安全防护模型，重点应对提示注入和表面混淆。模型在俄语鲁棒性、提示注入召回率及延迟方面表现突出，但总体聚合得分并非最高，且评测存在训练数据重叠、内部数据集不可复现及序列化方式不一致等限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防护模型对俄语提示注入和俄语表面混淆的实证研究不足。生产环境需要独立防护模型识别试图覆盖系统指令、规避安全策略或诱导不当输出的输入。

Method: 以Qwen3-0.6B为基础进行LoRA微调，训练数据涵盖俄语和英语，并采用safe/unsafe二元评分规则判断最终目标轮次。数据构造将同领域的有害样本及其对应样本与良性样本配对，同时对两类标签应用八种混淆变换。在统一测试框架中，与另外34个防护模型在19组基准上比较，并在15个模型上额外评估俄语清洁鲁棒性、俄语提示注入召回率和推理延迟。

Result: 在19组基准上的聚合得分为0.7432，低于两个最高分0.7641和0.7552；仅统计16组公开基准时得分为0.7153，另有4个模型更高。在15模型比较中，俄语清洁鲁棒性combined-F1为0.88，俄语提示注入召回率为0.999，均为最高；中位延迟为14.3毫秒，为最低。全套评测的误报率为0.268，漏报率为0.156。俄语提示注入评测集至少27.1%的样本与训练语料重叠，且所有响应结果均采用旧式独立回复序列化，而非发布聊天模板中的自然assistant角色路径。

Conclusion: HiveTraceGuard-Pro在俄语提示注入检测、综合俄语鲁棒性和低延迟方面具有竞争力，适合资源受限的生产防护场景。但其整体排名并非领先，较高的误报率、训练测试重叠、内部数据和代码不公开，以及评测序列化方式与实际发布模板不一致，削弱了结果的独立可验证性和泛化结论。

Abstract: Production LLMs must handle inputs that attempt to override system instructions, bypass safety policies or elicit harmful responses. A common mitigation is a separate guardrail model. Existing reports, however, provide little evidence on Russian prompt injection or Russian surface obfuscation. We present HiveTraceGuard-Pro, a 0.6B generative guardrail LoRA-tuned from Qwen3-0.6B. It is trained on Russian and English and uses one binary scoring rule (safe/unsafe) for the final target turn. Its training corpus pairs harmful examples, where a counterpart exists, with benign examples from the same domain and applies eight obfuscation transforms to both labels. In one harness, we compare HiveTraceGuard-Pro with thirty-four other guards on nineteen benchmark groups, sixteen of which are public. Its aggregate key is 0.7432, behind 0.7641 and 0.7552 for the two higher-scoring guards. Over the sixteen public groups alone, its key is 0.7153 and four of the thirty-four other suite guards score higher. In a fifteen-model comparison, HiveTraceGuard-Pro has the highest clean Russian robustness combined-F1 (0.88) and Russian prompt-injection recall (0.999). Both results use Russian sets assembled by our team, and at least 27.1% of the prompt-injection set overlaps the training corpus. Its 14.3 ms median latency is the lowest among those fifteen models in that run. Across the suite, FPR is 0.268 and FNR is 0.156. All reported response results use a legacy standalone-reply serialization rather than the natural assistant-role path of the shipped chat template. We release the merged weights on Hugging Face under Apache-2.0. The corpus, evaluation sets and evaluation code remain internal.

</details>


### [70] [What Limits Robustness in Deep Image Watermarking: An Analysis of Mechanisms and Their Scaling Across Capacities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01050)
*Marta Bistroń,Zbigniew Piotrowski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究深度图像水印中鲁棒性的真正瓶颈，发现限制主要来自提取前的空间同步、编解码失真适应性和嵌入强度窗口收窄，而非负载容量本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着水印负载从较低水平提升至更高水平，鲁棒性下降更加明显。本文旨在判断容量是根本限制，还是仅使其他已有瓶颈更加突出，并系统梳理水印需抵抗的失真及相应策略。

Method: 首先按主导维度组织失真与鲁棒策略：失真按负载容量分析，策略按可微性分析。随后针对将负载映射到空间块网格、且提取器与冻结嵌入器分离训练的一类方案，测量三种限制机制：负载网格去同步、编解码器失真对训练的抵抗，以及可用嵌入强度区间的缩窄。实验覆盖64至16384比特，并评估在编解码器失真上训练提取器的效果，同时提出可验证泛化能力的评估协议。

Result: 三种机制均在提取阶段之前产生，并且其限制取决于失真类别而非容量本身。针对编解码器训练提取器不仅没有改善性能，反而在训练所对应的工作点上降低读取效果。进一步增加提取端训练无法消除这些限制。

Conclusion: 对于该类深度水印设计，鲁棒性瓶颈并非单纯由负载容量决定，也不能仅靠提取器训练解决。应优先处理空间同步、编解码失真适应性和嵌入强度窗口等前置问题；相关结论适用于一类设计，而非某个具体实现。

Abstract: Robustness remains the principal open problem in deep image watermarking, and what limits it becomes sharper as payload grows. This paper asks whether capacity is itself the limit or only makes other limits visible, and answers in two parts. The first organizes the distortions a watermark must survive and the strategies developed to resist them, ordering each by the axis that governs it: payload capacity for the distortions, differentiability for the strategies. The second identifies and measures three mechanisms that limit robustness in schemes mapping the payload onto a spatial block grid with extraction trained separately from a frozen embedder: desynchronization of the payload grid, the resistance of codec-induced distortion to training, and the narrowing of the usable embedding-strength window. Payloads from 64 to 16384 bits are measured, well beyond the range those strategies address. Training the extraction stage against a codec proves not merely ineffective but harmful, degrading the reading at the operating points used in training. The limits follow the class of distortion rather than capacity itself, and none is removed by further training on the extraction side, because all three arise before extraction. An evaluation protocol making claims of generalization verifiable is also contributed. The conclusions are properties of a class of designs rather than of one implementation.

</details>


### [71] [Smart Contracts Claimed Vulnerable by the CVE Database, with Labels and Source Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01186)
*Monika di Angelo,Gernot Salzer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CVE-Smart-Contracts数据集，系统整理截至2026年7月与以太坊智能合约相关的CVE记录，包含漏洞工件、分类标签及函数级定位信息，用于支持智能合约安全研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVE记录缺乏面向以太坊智能合约的结构化工件、统一标签和精确漏洞位置，限制了代码分析与自动修复方法的评估，因此需要一个可复现、经过整理的数据集。

Method: 自动检索CVE记录，收集源代码和运行时字节码等外部证据，验证记录与工件的对应关系，依据三种分类体系分配标签，并定位漏洞涉及的函数；约15%的记录由人工分析完成。数据集保留全部外部输入以支持流水线复现，同时将少量明显错误的记录标记为“已驳回”，但不重新验证原始漏洞声明。

Result: 数据集包含491条与已部署合约关联的记录、26条涉及项目的记录（主要为库）、45条缺少经验证工件的记录，以及6条漏洞声明被驳回的记录。每条可用记录尽可能提供源代码、运行时字节码、分类标签和函数级位置。

Conclusion: CVE-Smart-Contracts为以太坊智能合约漏洞研究提供了规模化、结构化且可复现的数据基础，尤其适合评估智能合约代码分析和自动修复技术；但使用者应注意，该数据集主要整理和关联已有声明，并不等同于对所有漏洞真实性的独立确认。

Abstract: The Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) database catalogs vulnerability claims in hard- and software, among them those pertaining to blockchain programs a.k.a. smart contracts. We present CVE-Smart-Contracts, a curated dataset of CVE records up to July 2026 referring to Ethereum smart contracts. The dataset contains the vulnerable artifacts (source code and runtime bytecode), labels according to three taxonomies, and function-level locations. The retrieval of CVE records, collection of additional evidence, validation of the correspondence between records and artifacts, label assignment, and vulnerability localization are automated, leaving 15% to manual analysis. The dataset does not validate the original vulnerability claims, but marks a few records obviously wrong as `refuted'. For the sake of reproducibility, all external inputs are retained, so that rerunning the pipelines results in the same outputs. The dataset comprises 491 records linked to deployed contracts, 26 referring to projects (mostly libraries), 45 without validated artifacts, and six records with refuted claims. The dataset supports empirical security research, in particular the evaluation of code analysis and repair techniques.

</details>


### [72] [JENGA: Exploiting Counter-Based RowHammer Countermeasures to Break Real-Time Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01077)
*Valentin Abgrall,Marcello Traiola,Ruben Salvador,Maria Méndez Real,Alessandro Palumbo,Angeliki Kritikakou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析DDR5内存中基于硬件计数器的RowHammer防御机制对实时系统最坏执行时间的影响，提出JENGA攻击，并给出一种纳入防御延迟的安全WCET界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键实时系统不仅需要满足安全性，还必须保证时间可预测性。现有RowHammer防御机制通常依据平均性能评估，无法反映其对最坏情况执行时间和既有WCET假设的影响。

Method: 以DDR5标准化的逐行激活计数器（PRAC）为案例，在gem5与Ramulator 2.0仿真环境中实现JENGA。该方法通过攻击者控制的任务操纵RowHammer计数器的内部状态，并使用TACLeBench工作负载评估对受害实时任务执行时间的影响。

Result: PRAC等硬件计数器防御机制会引入显著的时序变化。JENGA可使受害任务的执行时间最高增加至其预期WCET的200%，从而使原有时间安全假设失效。

Conclusion: RowHammer防御机制产生的缓解开销必须纳入实时系统的WCET分析。论文针对采用PRAC-N等硬件计数器防御的DRAM系统，推导了能够覆盖防御诱发延迟的安全解析界限，以提升时间可预测性。

Abstract: Safety-critical real-time systems must satisfy multiple dependability requirements, notably time predictability and security. In such systems, tasks must complete within bounded and known execution times, typically characterised through Worst-Case Execution Time (WCET) analysis. At the same time, DRAM-based platforms are increasingly sensitive to the RowHammer read-disturbance security vulnerability, which has motivated the development of numerous hardware and software countermeasures in both academia and industry. However, the impact of these defences is generally evaluated in terms of average-case performance, a metric that is insufficient for safetycritical real-time systems, where worst-case behaviour is the primary concern. In this paper, we study the impact of RowHammer countermeasures based on hardware counters on the timing behaviour of real-time systems. We use a Per-Row-Activation-Counter (PRAC) countermeasure as a case study, standardised for recent DDR5 memories, and show that it can introduce significant timing variations. Based on this observation, we introduce JENGA, an attack in which an attacker-controlled task manipulates the internal state of the RowHammer countermeasure mechanism to increase the execution time of a victim real-time task beyond its expected WCET. We implement JENGA in a gem5 and Ramulator 2.0 simulation environment and evaluate its impact on TACLeBench workloads. We show that such an attack can delay tasks up to 200% of their WCET, making the initial timesafety assumptions unsafe. To address this issue, we derive a safe analytical bound that accounts for mitigation-induced delays in WCET analysis for DRAM systems protected by hardware countermeasures, such as PRAC-N.

</details>


### [73] [CRSF: Collusion-Resilient Privacy-Preserving Sensor Fusion with Byzantine-Robust Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01096)
*Chao Yin,Haihong Tian,Zheng Yang,Haibin Zhang,Fabio Massacci,Chenglu Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CRSF是一种面向传感器融合的抗串谋隐私保护协议。它通过PBFT一致性机制和受门限保护的输入标签发布机制，缓解现有混淆电路方案在传感器—服务器串谋及传感器参与操纵下的隐私、正确性与活性风险，并在最多261个传感器的云端实验中显示出可接受的在线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器—服务器—客户端架构中的基于混淆电路的隐私保护融合协议，虽然能够隐藏单个传感器输入和最终输出，但仍可能受到传感器与服务器串谋以及恶意操纵传感器参与状态的影响。这些问题会导致诚实传感器被错误排除、融合结果被篡改，甚至泄露诚实传感器的隐私，因此需要同时增强参与决策的可信性与密钥材料的抗串谋保护。

Method: CRSF在传感器提交阶段引入基于PBFT的协议一致性，使服务器不能单方面决定或篡改传感器参与情况；同时采用面向不同服务器、依赖执行状态的电路输入标签发布策略，并对输入标签实施门限保护。该组合设计要求潜在串谋方获得足够门限的秘密材料才能攻击隐私，从而限制可容忍范围内的传感器—服务器串谋能力。协议目标包括输入与输出隐私、带显式中止的正确性，以及在故障条件下的活性。作者还实现了该协议，并在Google Cloud上测量其在线阶段的计算与通信总成本。

Result: 实验将CRSF与最相关的现有基线方案进行比较，覆盖无故障执行及具有代表性的故障执行情形，并测试多类具备容错需求的融合电路。结果表明，在最多261个传感器的规模下，CRSF的在线执行开销保持在较具实用性的范围内。摘要未提供具体的延迟、吞吐量或相对性能提升数值，因此无法进一步量化其相对于基线的性能差异。

Conclusion: 该工作将共识式参与管理与门限化输入标签保护结合，为隐私保护传感器融合提供了更强的抗串谋与抗参与操纵能力。其主要贡献在于：避免单个服务器任意操纵参与集合，并降低允许范围内传感器—服务器串谋对诚实传感器隐私的威胁；实验结果支持其在中等规模传感器网络中的部署可行性。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving sensor fusion enables an untrusted server to compute an aggregate result over distributed sensor measurements without learning either individual inputs or the final output. Recent garbled-circuit-based protocols provide an efficient realization of this functionality in a sensor--server--client architecture, but remain vulnerable to sensor--server collusion and Byzantine manipulation of sensor participation. These weaknesses can compromise honest-sensor privacy, incorrectly exclude honest sensors, and corrupt the computed fusion result, thereby undermining the security guarantees expected from the protocol.
  We present CRSF, a collusion-resilient sensor-fusion protocol that addresses these weaknesses while providing privacy, correctness with explicit abort, and liveness. CRSF introduces a Practical Byzantine Fault Tolerance (PBFT)-based agreement phase for sensor submissions and uses server-specific, status-dependent label release with threshold protection of circuit-input labels. This design prevents any Byzantine server from unilaterally manipulating sensor participation and prevents any admissible sensor-server coalition from obtaining enough secret material to compromise honest-sensor privacy.
  We implement CRSF and compare its online execution time with the most relevant state-of-the-art baseline. Our Google Cloud evaluation measures the total computation and communication cost of the online protocol under fault-free and representative faulty executions. Across a range of fault-tolerant fusion circuits and up to 261 sensors, CRSF demonstrates a highly practical trade-off between robust security and protocol performance.

</details>


### [74] [Johnny Still Receives Spam SMS: Assessing the Robustness of SMS Spam Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01171)
*Muhammad Salman,Muhammad Islam,Muhammad Ikram,Mohamed Ali Kaafar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估真实部署的短信反垃圾系统在对抗性扰动下的鲁棒性，并提出结合对抗训练与异构分类器的集成防御方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短信垃圾检测器在受控测试中准确率较高，但面对真实传输链路中仍可存活的可感知及不可感知扰动时容易失效。论文旨在弥合实验室评测与终端用户实际依赖系统之间的鲁棒性差距。

Method: 研究对象涵盖商业短信应用、第三方反垃圾服务和公开开放权重模型。作者仅采用经真实SMS或RCS投递验证后仍能保留效果的扰动，并在标准与对抗条件下测试。通过显式留出式评估考察不同扰动族之间的泛化；随后构建包含不同模型架构和分词机制的多模型集成，并比较其投票策略及精确率—召回率权衡。

Result: 现有检测器对经对抗性修改的垃圾短信存在明显识别缺口。单独的对抗训练无法覆盖结构差异较大的、编码层面的攻击：其鲁棒性可在同类扰动内迁移，但跨扰动族会显著下降。异构模型集成，尤其是少数投票策略，可在维持有竞争力分类准确率的同时，增强对可感知和不可感知扰动的防御能力。

Conclusion: 短信反垃圾系统应采用覆盖真实传输条件、多个扰动类型和跨族泛化能力的全面鲁棒性评测。相较单一模型或单纯对抗训练，结合异构分类器的集成防御更适合应对现实环境中的对抗性垃圾短信；实际部署还应依据误报敏感度与召回需求选择运行阈值。

Abstract: SMS spam detection systems often achieve high accuracy in controlled environments but struggle against adversarial attacks and increasingly sophisticated spam tactics in real-world deployments. In this paper, we evaluate the robustness of SMS anti-spam systems that end users actually rely on, including commercial messaging applications, third-party anti-spam services, and publicly available open-weight models hosted on Hugging Face. We evaluate these systems under both standard and adversarial conditions, considering perceptible and state-of-the-art imperceptible attacks. We include only perturbations that we verify survive real SMS or RCS delivery, rather than lab-only artifacts. Our experiments reveal significant gaps in existing spam detectors' ability to identify adversarially manipulated messages. We further demonstrate that adversarial training alone is insufficient. Using an explicit held-out evaluation protocol, we find that robustness transfers well within a perturbation family but degrades sharply against structurally distinct, encoding-level attacks. To address these weaknesses, we propose a multi-model ensemble that combines adversarial training with spam classifiers diverse in architecture and tokenization. Our results show that this ensemble, particularly when using a minority-voting strategy, substantially improves robustness against both perceptible and imperceptible adversarial attacks while maintaining competitive classification accuracy. We also characterize the resulting precision-recall trade-off and recommend operating points for false-positive-sensitive and recall-critical deployments. These findings highlight the need for comprehensive robustness evaluations and ensemble-based defenses for building more secure SMS spam detection systems in real-world settings.

</details>


### [75] [A SoK for SoCs: Reading the TI Leaves on AI for Cyber Threat Intelligence Generation and Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01174)
*Saastha Vasan,Hadjer Benkraouda,Jizhou Chen,Leyan Pan,Doguhan Yeke,Shinan Liu,Noah Spahn,Stefano Ortolani,David Evans,Christopher Kruegel,Giovanni Vigna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统梳理了网络威胁情报（CTI）生命周期，并结合从业者调研与大语言模型试验，发现CTI生成与共享环节研究不足、实践中高度依赖人工；LLM可在该环节提供辅助，但当前仍无法替代专家监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施防御依赖可共享、可消费的CTI，但将原始攻击证据转化为标准化情报的过程较为碎片化，尤其“CTI生成与共享”阶段在学术研究中覆盖不足。作者旨在明确该阶段的实际工作流程、核心难题，以及LLM自动化辅助的可行性与局限。

Method: 作者首先进行文献综述，将CTI生命周期划分为威胁数据收集、CTI生成与共享、CTI消费三个阶段；随后对多个组织中实际负责生成和共享CTI的从业者进行调研。基于调研结果，作者将生成与共享细分为情报提取、规范化与富化、编码化、分发四步，并针对每一步开展LLM能力的初步可行性研究。

Result: 文献综述表明，威胁数据收集和CTI消费已有较多研究，而CTI生成与共享相关研究较少。从业者普遍采用人工流程，主要困难包括：避免敏感信息泄露、从噪声攻击数据中提取指标、将行为关联到标准化战术技术与过程（TTPs）、将情报转换为共享平台要求的格式。LLM在四个步骤中均能辅助分析师，但只能找回部分指标，难以确保所有结论均有输入证据支撑，也无法可靠判断共享情报对接收方是否仍具实用价值。

Conclusion: CTI生成与共享是CTI生命周期中的关键研究缺口，也是自动化潜力较高但风险显著的环节。现有LLM适合作为专家的协作工具，而非自主生成和发布CTI的替代方案。未来研究应重点提升指标提取完整性、证据可追溯与事实依据能力，以及面向接收方需求的情报效用评估机制。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) is essential for defending mission-critical infrastructure, yet the process of transforming raw attack evidence into shareable CTI remains fragmented and understudied.
  We conduct a literature survey of academic papers, organizing the CTI lifecycle into three stages: Threat Data Collection, CTI Generation and Sharing, and CTI Consumption. The first and third stages are well represented in the literature, whereas only a small number of papers address CTI Generation and Sharing. To learn how this stage is practiced, we survey practitioners across multiple organizations who routinely generate and share CTI. They describe a largely manual process with four recurring challenges: preventing the exposure of sensitive information, extracting indicators from noisy attack data, correlating observed behavior with standardized tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs), and translating CTI into the formats that sharing platforms require.
  Using the insights from the practitioner survey, we divide the CTI Generation and Sharing stage into four steps: Intelligence Extraction, Normalization and Enrichment, Codification, and Distribution. We then conduct pilot studies that probe the feasibility of current Large Language Models (LLMs) for each step. The pilot studies show that LLMs can assist an analyst in each of the four steps. However, the models recover only a fraction of the indicators the evidence contains, struggle to ground every claim in the supplied evidence, and do not judge what keeps shared intelligence useful to its recipients. Each step therefore requires expert supervision. Based on these observations, we derive three research directions for automating the production of shareable intelligence.

</details>


### [76] [Reveree: Diagnosing LLM Reverse-Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01185)
*Hadjer Benkraouda,Hongyu Cai,Berkay Celik,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Reveree，一个用于诊断大语言模型自主逆向工程能力的评测框架。该框架不仅统计是否解题成功，还衡量模型在八阶段逆向流程中的里程碑进展及行动行为，从而定位失败环节并区分真实分析与可能的记忆复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆向工程智能体评测通常只以 CTF 题目是否被解出作为指标，无法说明模型具体在哪一步失败，也难以判断成功是否源于对二进制的实际分析，还是源于对公开答案的记忆。论文旨在建立更具诊断性的评测方法，以获得对模型能力边界、提示策略影响和记忆效应的细粒度认识。

Method: Reveree 使用三层评测：第一层为最终解题率；第二层为覆盖八个逆向工程阶段的里程碑进度；第三层为智能体操作行为画像。理解类阶段由对最终结果盲化的 LLM 裁判评分，并通过人工专家验证；其余阶段采用确定性规则验证。作者以 88 道 picoCTF 与 NYU-CTF 题目为基准，评测九个前沿模型及四种提示策略，并通过仅提供题目描述、表面扰动等实验分析记忆与真实推理的作用。

Result: 实验显示，基础模型的选择是性能差异的主要来源，提示策略的影响相对较小且依赖具体模型。模型规模、发布时间新旧或使用成本与逆向能力并不存在稳定正相关。失败主要集中于逆向流程中的理解阶段；增加预算、重复尝试或推理投入只能挽回少量失败案例，表明核心瓶颈更接近能力限制而非资源限制。在记忆分析中，模型可仅凭 picoCTF 题目描述复现部分结果，但在 NYU-CTF 上几乎未观察到可测量的记忆效应；多数成功案例在表面扰动后仍能保持，说明真实分析与记忆复现可以同时存在。

Conclusion: Reveree 将逆向工程智能体评测从单一的成败判断扩展为过程、能力与行为层面的诊断。研究表明，当前模型提升逆向能力的关键不在于单纯扩大推理资源或优化通用提示，而在于增强对程序语义、控制流程与关键逻辑的理解能力。该框架的公开有助于后续研究更可靠地衡量逆向工程智能体的真实能力及其记忆影响。

Abstract: Reverse engineering (RE) is critical to security tasks such as malware analysis and vulnerability discovery, and large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly able to perform it autonomously. Capture-the-flag (CTF) RE challenges have become the standard proxy for measuring this capability, but evaluation rests on a single criterion: whether the agent captures the flag. This solve rate reveals neither where in the RE process an agent fails nor whether a success reflects analysis of the binary or recall of a public solution. In this paper, we propose Reveree, a diagnostic framework that scores an LLM RE agent's trajectory at three tiers: solve rate, milestone progress through an eight-stage RE schema, and a behavioral profile of its actions. Comprehension stages are scored by an outcome-blinded LLM judge validated against a human expert; all other stages are verified deterministically. Using Reveree, we evaluate nine frontier models and four prompting strategies on 88 picoCTF and NYU-CTF challenges. We find that the base model dominates performance, whereas prompting strategy is a secondary, model-dependent effect. Surprisingly, larger, newer, or costlier models are not reliably stronger. We also find that failures concentrate at the comprehension stages of the RE process, and that extra budget, persistence, or reasoning effort rescues few of them, pointing to a competence limit rather than a resource limit. Regarding memorization, while models reproduce picoCTF flags from challenge descriptions alone, NYU-CTF shows minimal measurable recall, and most solves survive surface perturbation, indicating that genuine analysis coexists with memorization. We release Reveree to the community.

</details>


### [77] [Identification of Compositional Risks in Data Protection Impact Assessments and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01201)
*Henrik Graßhoff,Meiko Jensen,Malte Hansen,Nils Gruschka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多服务提供商协同处理个人数据场景的协议，用于识别并管理由服务组合结构本身引发的隐私风险，尤其是多个处理方共享底层云服务商而导致的数据非预期可关联性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数据保护影响评估通常聚焦单个数据处理方的风险，难以发现跨处理方、由共同依赖关系产生的组合性风险。因此，服务组合中的共享基础设施可能造成隐私暴露，却未被任何单独评估充分覆盖。

Method: 作者先定义组合性隐私风险问题并提炼协议需求，随后设计协议以发现潜在风险候选项。该协议关注服务提供商及其底层依赖之间的关系，识别可能形成共同数据汇聚点或关联能力的处理方组合，并将识别结果反馈给数据保护影响评估流程。

Result: 该方法能够定位服务组合中可能存在组合性隐私风险的候选关系，例如不同处理方在不知情的情况下使用同一云服务商。识别结果可作为补充信息，帮助评估者扩展原有影响评估的覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将隐私风险分析从孤立处理方扩展到服务组合及其依赖结构层面。所提协议有助于发现传统单体评估容易遗漏的共享基础设施风险，从而提升多处理方数据处理场景下数据保护影响评估的完整性与有效性。

Abstract: When personal data is processed in a distributed manner by cooperating service providers, privacy risks may emerge solely from the choice of data processors included in the composition. For instance, different data processors may unknowingly rely on the same cloud provider, allowing for unintended linkability of personal data at that very provider. As such compositional risks to privacy are beyond the scope of each individual risk assessment, they are likely to be overseen when performing a data protection impact assessment. In this paper, we propose a novel protocol to detect and manage such compositional risks to privacy. Following an initial problem definition and requirements elicitation, we elaborate how our protocol identifies candidates for compositional risks and how this information may be used to improve the results of a data protection impact assessment over service compositions including multiple data processors.

</details>


### [78] [Who Judges the Judges? A Chinese Safety QA Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Responses and Safety Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01210)
*Rui Yang,Shuang Huang,Junhua Liu,Ziqi Zhao,Qingzhong Yan,Yuhang Sun,Cong Liu,Guoping Hu,Rui Mei,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出C-SafeQA，一个面向中文大语言模型响应级安全性的基准，强调应评估模型实际回答是否违反政策，而非仅判断用户查询是否有风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准常将查询风险与回答安全性混为一谈；中文中的语言变体和对抗性变换还可能掩盖原始意图，因此需要基于政策、面向响应结果的评估框架。

Method: 构建包含538条基础查询和8,877条对抗查询的数据集，由4个完整模型部署生成37,660条查询—响应记录，并标注为安全、不安全或有争议。参考标签通过多模型一致性感知裁决及3名安全专家对分层子集进行盲审获得；同时使用共享参考标签审计7个自动化安全评审器，并测试两类首字母藏头变换的影响。

Result: 基础查询上的不安全响应率为0.93%至3.35%，对抗查询上升至11.68%至30.05%。在对抗子集上，7个评审器在不安全响应召回率与条件于风险查询的安全响应误报率之间存在明显权衡，没有任何评审器在所有指标上占优。两类藏头变换均降低了所有评审器的不安全响应召回率，暴露出与变换机制相关的评估弱点。

Conclusion: C-SafeQA表明，中文安全评估应从查询级风险识别转向政策约束下的响应级判断，并同时报告模型安全性与评审器可靠性。其公开的数据记录、元数据、验证代码和评审脚本支持结果复现，但数据集构建、目标响应生成及私有裁决过程不在公开范围内。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks for large language models often assess the risk of a user query, although the outcome of question answering depends on whether the response violates a policy. This distinction is critical in Chinese harmful-content evaluation, where linguistic variation and adversarial transformations can obscure risky intent. We introduce C-SafeQA, a policy-grounded benchmark for response-level Chinese safety evaluation. It comprises 538 base queries and 8,877 adversarial queries answered by four full-model LLM deployments, yielding 37,660 query-response records labeled safe, unsafe, or disputed. Reference labels are generated through agreement-aware multi-model adjudication and blind audits of stratified subsets by three safety experts. C-SafeQA supports both evaluation of target-model safety and auditing of seven automated safety judges against shared reference labels. Unsafe-response rates range from 0.93% to 3.35% on base queries and from 11.68% to 30.05% on adversarial queries. On the adversarial subset, judges show substantial trade-offs between unsafe-response recall and risk-query-conditioned safe-response false positive rate, and no judge dominates all metrics. Both acrostic transformations reduce unsafe recall for all seven judges, revealing mechanism-specific evaluator weaknesses. Dataset records, metadata, verification code, and judge scripts are publicly released to support recomputation, while benchmark construction, target-response generation, and private adjudication remain outside the release boundary.

</details>


### [79] [What's in Your Agent's Context? Context Privilege Escalation Attacks against AI Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01222)
*Zichuan Li,Jian Cui,Ashley Chen,Xiaojing Liao,Luyi Xing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统分析真实世界AI智能体框架中的上下文收集与组装机制，发现该环节存在可导致权限提升的安全缺陷，并在12个主流框架中验证了其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高知名度AI智能体框架通常采用厂商私有或不透明的上下文组装设计，使上下文来源、拼接逻辑及相关安全边界难以审计。论文旨在填补这一研究空白，揭示由上下文组装机制引入的实际安全风险。

Method: 作者对真实智能体框架的上下文来源、消息角色映射、持久化范围及组装流程进行系统性分析，并提出两类上下文权限提升问题：消息角色上下文权限提升（M-CPE），即低权限来源的攻击者可控内容被纳入高权限消息角色；跨作用域上下文权限提升（X-CPE），即攻击者可控内容超出其原始引入范围而持续存在。随后在包括Claude Code和Codex在内的12个框架中评估这些问题。

Result: 研究发现两类攻击在现实框架中具有可操作性。其潜在后果包括智能体整体控制权丧失、远程代码执行、拒绝服务，以及工具或技能调用被操纵等，表明上下文组装并非单纯工程实现细节，而是关键安全边界。

Conclusion: 论文指出，AI智能体的上下文组装设计需要被视为核心安全机制。框架应明确标注上下文来源和可信级别，保持消息角色与数据权限的一致性，限制不可信内容跨作用域持久化，并对上下文传播和工具调用链路实施可审计的隔离与校验。

Abstract: Real-world, high-profile AI agent harnesses often rely on vendor-proprietary or opaque designs for context assembly, leaving the sources and underlying logic of assembled context poorly understood and the resulting security risks largely unexplored. In this paper, we present the first systematic analysis of context assembly designs in real-world AI agent harnesses. We study and uncover how an agent harness is designed to collect and assemble context from diverse sources, and identify a set of practical attack vectors arising from these designs. Our analysis brings to light two novel categories of attacks in the context assembly of real-world harnesses: (1) MessageRole Context Privilege Escalation (M-CPE), which occurs when attacker-controlled content originating from a low-privileged context is incorporated into a higher-privileged message role. (2) Cross-Scope Context Privilege Escalation (X-CPE), which occurs when attacker-controlled content persists beyond the context in which it was introduced. We performed a systemic security analysis of the CPE attacks against 12 real-world agent harnesses, including Claude Code and Codex. The resulting consequences include full agent compromise, remote code execution, denial of service, and manipulated tool or skill invocations, etc.

</details>


### [80] [Position Matters: Feature Inversion Attacks in ViT Split Inference with Token Reduction and Shuffling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01232)
*Stefano Leggio,Giulio Rossolini,Alessandro Biondi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉Transformer分割推理中令牌压缩与令牌打乱的隐私风险，提出空间对齐重建攻击（SARA），证明打乱令牌并不能有效阻止输入图像重建；移除位置嵌入并通过知识蒸馏适配边缘模型可在基本保持任务精度的同时显著降低泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘—云协同推理中，边缘设备会向云端发送中间令牌表示。令牌压缩可降低计算与通信开销，令牌打乱被认为能够破坏空间结构并提升隐私性，但其面对特征反演攻击时的实际防护效果尚不明确。作者关注传输嵌入是否仍隐含足以恢复原始输入的位置信息，以及如何在不修改云端模型的条件下实现有效防护。

Method: 作者首先分析打乱后的令牌嵌入中残留的位置线索，随后提出SARA统一攻击流程：预测各令牌的空间位置；依预测位置恢复令牌的二维布局；使用特征空间掩码自编码器补全因令牌压缩而缺失的嵌入；基于恢复后的完整特征重建输入图像。针对防御，作者在边缘侧移除位置嵌入，并利用知识蒸馏逐步适配边缘Transformer模块，使其输出能够继续兼容固定的云端模型。

Result: 实验表明，令牌打乱主要造成表面上的隐私保护：SARA能够从传输特征中推断令牌位置并较大程度恢复原始空间组织，从而实现有效重建。令牌压缩的保护能力相对更强，但只要保留令牌仍携带充分的语义与位置信息，仍会产生显著泄露。所提出的轻量级边缘侧防御能够明显削弱SARA的重建效果，同时保持下游任务准确率，并且无需修改云端模型。

Conclusion: ViT分割推理中的令牌打乱不能被视为可靠的隐私机制，因为位置相关信息仍可从嵌入中恢复。令牌压缩虽能提升保护强度，但并非充分防护。移除位置嵌入并结合知识蒸馏进行边缘侧适配，是一种部署成本较低、兼顾任务效用与隐私保护的可行方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are increasingly used in split-inference systems, where edge devices transmit intermediate token representations to a remote cloud. In this setting, token reduction lowers computation and communication costs, while token shuffling disrupts the spatial organization of the transmitted tokens, potentially limiting information leakage. However, their privacy benefits remain unclear against feature inversion attacks, which attempt to reconstruct the input from the transmitted embeddings. In this work, we show that, despite disrupting the spatial structure required by conventional reconstruction attacks, transmitted token embeddings retain substantial positional information. Based on this observation, we introduce the Spatially Aligned Reconstruction Attack (SARA), a unified pipeline that predicts token positions, restores their spatial layout, reconstructs missing embeddings using a feature-space masked autoencoder, and recovers the input image. Our results demonstrate that token shuffling provides only apparent privacy, as SARA largely reconstructs the original token organization. Token reduction offers stronger protection, but significant leakage persists when the retained tokens preserve sufficient semantic and positional information. Finally, we introduce a lightweight edge-side defense that removes positional embeddings and progressively adapts the edge-side transformer blocks through knowledge distillation. It substantially reduces attack performance against SARA, while preserving downstream task accuracy and requiring no changes to the cloud-side model.

</details>


### [81] [MutMem-V2: Cryptographically Authorized Mutation in Persistent Agent Memory Portable Verification and Reproducible Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01235)
*Walid Saidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MutMem V2 是对 V1 的协议化与可验证性补全：在不新增第二套记忆引擎的前提下，提供可移植的规范、独立实现一致性测试、干净环境复现路径及可再生证据表。其结论限于完整性、授权、可追溯性、合规性与复现性，不主张语义真实性、普适鲁棒性或独立复制已被证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V1 虽提出持久化代理记忆的保留性与经密码学授权的变更机制，但缺少完整的便携式验证契约，以及从全新安装环境复现系统行为的明确路径。V2 的目标是弥合这一发布与工程验证缺口，使第三方能够依据统一字节表示、承诺机制、信任锚与测试向量验证协议实现和公开主张。

Method: V2 定义规范化字节编码、带域分离的对象与包承诺、强制性回忆证据成员资格及排序、外部信任锚、身份纪元、撤销、授权、请求回执、有序披露和三类变更终态。协议发布18个带版本对象模式、39个回忆测试向量、15个变更测试向量及37类封闭式回忆失败原因。评估采用独立 Node 与 Python 实现的终态判定比对、生产一致性语料测试、Node v26.8.1 干净安装启动与重启验证、限定范围的120单元 Canary 实验，以及基于自哈希聚合物的公开表再生和独立验证器统计重建。

Result: 两套独立实现对72个结构与密码学终态的判定及首要失败原因完全一致；生产一致性语料在28个必需类别中的42个案例全部一致。干净 Node v26.8.1 安装可完成首次启动、重启及调度器就绪，且未使用实验性记忆功能。120单元 Canary 实验仅支持显式标记遍历这一有限结论。所有公开表可由自哈希聚合物重新生成，独立验证器能够重建统计结果与主张边界。

Conclusion: 该摘要显示 V2 的核心贡献是将记忆变更协议转化为可跨实现验证、可审计且可清洁复现的工程与密码学契约。证据较强地支持协议层面的完整性、授权、追踪、实现一致性和复现性；但其验证范围受既定假设、测试语料和实验范围约束。V1 的既有经验性结果仍仅具历史性质，V2 未证明记忆内容的语义正确性、面对所有环境的鲁棒性，也未完成独立复制验证。

Abstract: MutMem V1 introduced retention-preserving, cryptographically authorized mutation for persistent agent memory but did not provide a complete portable verification contract or clean-install reproduction path. MutMem V2 closes that publication gap without introducing a second memory engine. It specifies exact canonical bytes, domain-separated object and bundle commitments, mandatory recall-evidence membership and ordering, external trust anchors, identity epochs, revocation, authorization, request receipts, ordered disclosure, and three mutation terminal types. The released protocol contains 18 versioned object schemas, 39 recall vectors, 15 mutation vectors, and 37 closed recall failure reasons. Independent Node and Python implementations agree on verdict and primary reason for all 72 structural and cryptographic terminals; a production-conformance corpus agrees on 42/42 cases across 28 required classes. A clean Node v26.8.1 installation reaches first-boot, restart, and scheduler readiness with no experimental memories. A separately scoped 120-unit Canary experiment supports only explicit-marker traversal. Every public table regenerates from a self-hashed aggregate, and an independent verifier reconstructs the statistics and claim boundaries. Historical V1 empirical results remain historical. MutMem V2 supports claims about portable integrity, authorization, traceability, conformance, and reproducibility under stated assumptions; it does not establish semantic truth, universal robustness, or independent replication.

</details>


### [82] [Hidden Services Protocol for Mixnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01326)
*Nicolas Constantinides,Mahdi Rahimi,Stavros Nonis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出NymHS：面向混合网络的实用隐藏服务协议，在保持发送方匿名的同时保护接收方/服务地址隐私，并通过实现与大规模网页实验验证其可部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络通过多跳转发、随机延迟与密码变换削弱通信入口和出口的可关联性，但缺少能够同时提供发送方匿名和接收方隐私的安全、实用协议。已有基于单次使用回复块（SURB）的方案还存在由单个混合节点控制即可触发的匿名性风险。

Method: 作者分析现有SURB使用方式中的两类实际攻击，并设计相应防护机制；在此基础上构建NymHS，支持匿名服务发现、经认证的双向会话及异步SURB补充。系统在开源Nym代码库中实现，并以118个网站、27种配置、每种重复3次的网页浏览测试进行评估，共完成9,558次页面加载。

Result: 实验表明NymHS可在混合网络上高效部署。将Sphinx载荷从2 KiB增至10 KiB后，平均页面加载时延约降低5.2倍；相对于当前Nym基线，通信开销由21.7%降至4.3%。

Conclusion: NymHS填补了混合网络中接收方隐私保护的协议空缺。其安全设计缓解了SURB相关匿名性漏洞，且实现评估显示，通过增大有效载荷等配置优化，可获得较低的网页访问延迟和通信开销。

Abstract: Mix networks (mixnets) provide network-level privacy by routing each communication packet through a sequence of intermediaries, called mixnodes, that randomly delay and cryptographically transform packets before forwarding them, making it difficult for observers to link mixnet entries to exits. While this mechanism protects sender privacy from both external adversaries and the receiver, existing mixnets lack a secure and practical protocol that simultaneously protects receiver (destination) privacy, particularly from the sender. We close this gap.
  We introduce the first practical and secure hidden-service protocol for mixnets, providing receiver privacy alongside sender anonymity. Our design builds on Single-Use Reply Blocks (SURBs), which enable anonymous replies without revealing the receiver's address. We show, however, that existing approaches to using SURBs expose two practical attacks that can compromise sender or receiver anonymity when the opposing party controls only a single mixnode. We develop defenses against both vulnerabilities.
  Building on these defenses, we introduce NymHS, a secure and practical hidden-service protocol for mixnets that supports anonymous service discovery, authenticated bidirectional sessions, and asynchronous SURB replenishment. We implement NymHS on the open-source Nym codebase and evaluate its practicality through web-browsing experiments across 118 websites, measuring each of the 27 configurations three times (9,558 page loads). Our results demonstrate that hidden services can be deployed efficiently over mixnets. In particular, increasing the Sphinx payload from 2 KiB to 10 KiB reduces mean page-load latency by approximately 5.2x and decreases communication overhead from 21.7% to 4.3% relative to the current Nym baseline.

</details>


### [83] [When Safety Routing Breaks: Understanding Alignment Fragility under Benign Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01455)
*Yitong Guo,Xiaoyi Chen,Siyuan Zhang,Xiaofeng Wang,Haixu Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于Fisher几何的解释：良性微调通过重新锐化输出侧MLP中的低秩安全路由通路，导致LLM拒答行为快速失效，而通用能力仅轻微下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究主要将安全失效归因于梯度冲突，但该解释难以说明安全能力的非对称脆弱性、少量安全样本即可恢复拒答，以及安全与通用能力之间的不同退化程度。论文旨在揭示安全对良性微调敏感的内部机制。

Method: 分析安全相关Fisher信息的秩与几何形态，比较对齐前后的安全曲率及输出路由通路；使用100条良性微调样本观察输出侧MLP模块的锐化现象；进一步评估少量安全样本的恢复效果，并测试LoRA与ASAM对早期安全崩溃的缓解作用。

Result: 安全Fisher信息呈低秩特征，对齐使安全几何变得更平坦，但保留了与输出路由相关的路径。良性微调后，该路径在输出侧MLP中被选择性重新锐化，导致拒答能力显著下降，而通用效用仅温和退化。少量安全样本可以恢复拒答，表明安全相关内部表征仍被保留。LoRA和ASAM能够通过抑制输出侧锐度延缓早期崩溃，但在更大微调规模下保护作用减弱。

Conclusion: LLM安全失效更适合被理解为低秩输出路由机制受到扰动，而非单纯的梯度冲突。安全对齐应重点控制输出侧模块的曲率与锐度，并关注大规模微调条件下现有缓解方法保护能力的衰减。

Abstract: Benign fine-tuning severely weakens the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs), so we study why refusal behavior is so fragile. While prior work often attributes this failure to gradient conflict, we propose a fundamentally different Fisher-geometric explanation: safety Fisher is low-rank, and alignment makes the safety geometry flatter while preserving an output-routing pathway. After 100 benign fine-tuning examples, this pathway is selectively re-sharpened in output-side MLP modules, explaining the asymmetric fragility: safety can collapse to high attack success rates, while general utility degrades mildly. The routing view also explains why few safety examples can restore refusal behavior, indicating that internal safety-relevant representations are preserved. Finally, we show that LoRA and ASAM mitigate early collapse by suppressing output-side sharpness, but their protection weakens at larger fine-tuning scales. Overall, safety failure is best understood as a disruption of a low-rank output-routing mechanism

</details>


### [84] [Defense-as-Skill: Evolving Runtime Guard Skill for Skill-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01487)
*Xiaofang Yang,Ziqi Miao,Dianbo Sui,Jing Shao,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Defense-as-Skill范式及SkillSonar运行时防护器，将可安装、可检查、可编辑的防御技能与不可信任务技能并行运行，并依据用户任务边界对敏感操作执行允许、重新规划或确认决策。实验表明，该方法显著降低恶意技能攻击成功率，同时保持较好的安全性—效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能增强型智能体会将技能作为持久运行时上下文，使恶意技能能够在后续任务和特定工作区状态出现后实施隐蔽攻击。仅依赖安装前审查难以识别这类任务条件型风险，因此需要能够理解当前任务边界并在运行时介入的防护机制。

Method: 构建SCOPE-R数据集，覆盖6类风险和21个子类别，包括206个经攻击确认的恶意实例及43个良性任务。提出SkillSonar，在不修改底层智能体运行时的前提下检查敏感操作，并输出允许、重新规划或请求确认的决策。进一步采用基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的运行时防护技能演化方法，根据执行反馈迭代磁盘上的防护技能。

Result: 在Claude Code和OpenClaw上，演化后的SkillSonar显著降低攻击成功率并保持较优安全—效用权衡。在重复GLM-5实验中，ID攻击成功率由0.482降至0.104，OOD攻击成功率由0.606降至0.115。实验还显示其可迁移至不同受害模型、未见风险类别和外部基准，并能抵御自适应攻击者。消融实验表明，明确分配安全责任以及采用技能原生表示，都是性能提升的重要因素。

Conclusion: Defense-as-Skill为技能增强型智能体提供了一种不依赖底层运行时改造的运行时安全方案。通过任务条件化判断与可演化的技能化防护器，SkillSonar能够在降低攻击风险的同时维持任务效用，并表现出一定的跨模型、跨风险类别和跨基准泛化能力。

Abstract: Skill-augmented agents load reusable skills as persistent runtime context, improving task performance but also giving malicious skills a durable channel for steering future actions. Such skills may leak secrets, corrupt code, bypass approvals, or stage data for exfiltration only after a concrete user task and workspace state make the unsafe action appear useful. This makes pre-install vetting insufficient and calls for runtime, task-conditioned protection. We propose Defense-as-Skill, a defense paradigm that implements the runtime guard itself as an installable, inspectable, and editable skill. Our guard, SkillSonar, runs alongside untrusted task skills and checks sensitive actions against the user's task boundary, routing each action to an allow, replan, or confirmation decision without modifying the underlying agent runtime. To study this setting, we construct SCOPE-R, a task-conditioned dataset covering 6 risk families and 21 sub-categories, with 206 attack-confirmed malicious instances and 43 benign tasks. We then improve SkillSonar on the SCOPE-R training subset using runtime guard-skill evolution, a Monte-Carlo Tree Search procedure that evolves the on-disk guard skill from feedback on the rollouts. Across Claude Code and OpenClaw, the evolved guard substantially reduces attack success while maintaining a favorable safety-utility trade-off. On repeated GLM-5 runs, SkillSonar reduces ID ASR from 0.482 to 0.104 and OOD ASR from 0.606 to 0.115. Further analyses demonstrate transfer across victim models, held-out risk families, and external benchmarks, as well as retained protection against adaptive attackers. Ablations further show that explicit safety responsibility assignment and the skill-native representation are both important to the observed gains.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HyperWorld，系统比较文本世界模型中不同状态序列化方式。结果表明，以实体和关系为中心组织多条相关事实的超边序列化，在小中型模型及分布外环境中表现最稳健，并提升下游规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本环境中的世界模型需要从序列化状态描述中学习动作的符号化影响，但状态结构如何影响学习、泛化与规划能力尚缺乏受控研究。作者旨在检验更高阶的状态组织是否能提供有效归纳偏置。

Method: 在相同真实状态、训练目标和评测框架下，比较四类输入表示：原始观测、独立句子、成对三元组，以及围绕实体和关系聚合相关事实的超边单元。模型根据当前状态和动作预测符号效果，或判断动作不可行；实验覆盖不同模型规模、数据预算、分布内与分布外测试世界，并评估效果预测、可行性判别和贪心规划。

Result: 超边序列化在0.5B至1.5B模型上具有最明显优势，且在分布偏移下取得最高的事实级F1。在分布内精确匹配上，成对三元组可与超边持平或略优；随着模型规模增大，不同表示之间的差距缩小。总体而言，超边表示在小中型规模下实现了较优的动作可行性识别与效果预测权衡，并在下游贪心规划中获得最高成功率。

Conclusion: 高阶状态组织是一种简单而有效的文本符号世界模型归纳偏置。对于容量受限模型或测试环境与训练环境存在差异的场景，超边式序列化比扁平句子和成对关系表示更有利于泛化与规划。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [86] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出I-CARE方法论，用于系统定义、测量和报告生成式机器遗忘中的干扰现象，即删除目标知识时对相关但应保留概念造成的非预期损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式机器遗忘研究主要关注目标知识是否被移除，对语义相关知识的保留情况及其干扰效应缺乏统一定义和一致评估，导致结果难以比较、复现和解释。

Method: I-CARE将干扰作为独立研究对象，提供任务形式化定义、评估指标和结果报告模板。该方法不依赖特定模型、数据集或遗忘算法，并通过开源软件框架及Web图形界面支持实验分析和结果探索。

Result: 基于当前先进遗忘算法和常用数据集的可行性实验表明，I-CARE能够识别并分析多个生成式机器遗忘场景中的干扰模式，验证了其实际适用性。

Conclusion: I-CARE为生成式机器遗忘中的干扰研究建立了统一、可复现且具有长期适应性的评估框架，有助于推动不同方法和设置之间的系统比较。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [87] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究需求量确定但到达时点随机的有限期多品种产能约束批量生产问题，将其建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程，并提出基于状态反馈策略搜索的遗传算法。实验表明，该算法在保持较小最优性差距的同时，显著降低了求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统确定性批量生产模型通常将需求到达期固定化，难以反映需求在给定时间窗内随机到达所造成的产能竞争、订单级欠交及库存演化不确定性。随机到达时点会显著扩大状态与转移规模，使精确动态规划面临较高的时间和内存成本，因此需要高质量近似算法。

Method: 作者在订单需求层面同时决策生产与分配，以刻画订单之间的产能竞争、订单特定的欠交状态及依赖分配决策的库存变化。随后完整定义离散时间马尔可夫决策过程，包括状态空间、可行动作、转移核和单期成本。为量化随机时点的计算影响，论文以各需求最可能到达期替代其分布，构造确定性对照模型。针对随机模型，遗传算法在可行状态反馈策略空间中搜索，并利用马尔可夫决策过程转移模型对每个候选策略进行精确评价；对无法精确求解的实例，则通过经验贝尔曼时间回归估计精确求解耗时并外推加速比。

Result: 随机到达时点相较于最可能到达期的确定性近似，显著增加状态数、转移数、求解时间及内存压力。在330个基准实例上，当精确随机解可获得时，遗传算法平均最优性差距约为3.44%。在90个困难实例中，其最优性差距均低于5%，且平均优化加速比为6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）。

Conclusion: 需求到达时点随机性是多品种产能约束批量生产问题计算复杂度的重要来源。所提出的遗传算法能够在精确求解困难时提供接近最优的状态反馈策略，并实现明显的计算加速，因而适合用于大规模或硬件资源受限的随机到达时点生产计划问题。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [88] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多轮对话的累积式诈骗风险评估框架，在每轮新增信息后动态更新风险等级、连续风险分数、解释理由和安全建议，并比较四种小型语言模型在投资、慈善和技术支持诈骗场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统诈骗检测常将单条消息作为独立样本，难以识别从冒充或寒暄、建立信任、制造紧迫感到索取敏感信息或资金的渐进式风险。研究旨在满足多轮动态监测以及移动端和资源受限环境中的部署需求。

Method: 构建包含2至8轮对话的多轮数据集，覆盖投资、慈善和技术支持诈骗，并在每个累积阶段标注定性风险等级、连续风险分数、解释性依据和安全建议。采用统一训练框架微调并评估Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3四种小型语言模型，以实现逐轮风险重估。

Result: 微调后的小型模型能够识别诈骗相关语言线索及跨轮次风险升级模式，并保持较紧凑的模型规模。相较于参数规模，Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2展现出更强的逐轮风险估计能力。摘要未报告具体数据集规模、评价指标、数值结果或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 累积式多轮建模有助于实现随对话演化而更新的诈骗风险评估，小型语言模型具备部署于移动端和资源受限设备的潜力。研究仍需通过更大规模、更真实的跨渠道数据以及对抗性和跨领域测试，进一步验证泛化能力、误报风险和实际保护效果。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework. Fine-tuned small models capture fraud-related linguistic cues and cross-turn escalation patterns while maintaining compact architectures suitable for mobile and resource-constrained deployment settings. Among the evaluated models, Phi-4 and LLaMA-3.2 achieve stronger turn-aware risk estimation performance relative to their parameter scale. These results suggest that structured cumulative modeling can support incremental scam risk assessment in deployment-oriented settings while highlighting the potential of compact language models for privacy-aware and on-device fraud protection.

</details>


### [89] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以逐步计算MD5作为受控长程智能体评测，用196次相互依赖的工具调用检验模型能否在上下文中精确保持32位中间状态。结果表明，在保留推理上下文并对具备思考能力的工作模型进行投票时，gpt-oss-120b能在多数完整运行中得到正确摘要值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程智能体基准通常只报告端到端成功率，难以区分失败究竟源于指令理解、状态追踪、算术计算还是服务层问题；同时可能被直接猜测最终答案等捷径干扰。因此，需要一个指令解释负担低、全程可对齐真值轨迹、可逐位验证的受控任务。

Method: 作者依据RFC 1321从零实现MD5，将单次散列展开为64轮、196次依赖性调用。模型需在每轮调用之间于自身上下文中传递四个32位状态字(a,b,c,d)。每一步输出均与参考轨迹比对，最终摘要值按位校验。实验还设置了更强条件：以第二个LLM替代所有基础工具，由驱动模型和工作模型共同完成计算，且流程中不使用精确算术工具。

Result: 在温度为0和固定短提示条件下，gpt-oss-120b可在多数完成的运行中跨越全部196次调用保持正确状态并产出正确MD5摘要。成功的关键因素是每轮保留模型自身推理内容，以及通过对具备思考能力的工作模型进行投票来降低模运算错误。作者进一步将剩余错误按状态传递、算术计算和服务层来源进行归因。

Conclusion: MD5逐步计算为长程状态追踪提供了可控且可审计的评测范式。研究显示，长程任务性能不仅取决于模型参数，还显著受上下文保留和推理结果聚合等运行时设计影响；该框架可更准确地区分状态管理能力与其他失败因素。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [90] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 OpenAgentFlow：面向异构大语言模型智能体的控制平面/动作平面架构，在动作提交前统一执行策略检查，以治理 GUI、API、工具调用和模型生成调用等共享环境操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中的风险主要发生在具体动作影响共享状态时。现有防护机制通常分散于提示词、工具、GUI 或单个智能体内部，难以覆盖跨步骤流程风险，也不利于统一审计和策略动态演进。

Method: 系统将待执行动作标准化为统一的 AgentEvent 流，并在执行前经由共享策略执行点进行判定。控制平面维护事件溯源、会话状态、审计记录及可更新策略；动作平面负责实际执行。新策略可在不修改智能体、提示词、模型或执行路径的情况下生效。作者在 Android 环境完成原型实现，并通过动作事件、动态策略和端到端模拟器测试进行评估。

Result: 在 300 个动作事件基准上，系统准确率为 94.0%，攻击拦截率为 95.3%。在 30 个动态策略案例中，新规则安装后有 27 个案例符合预期。在 100 个 Android 模拟器案例中，对其中 98 个可追踪案例，原始准确率为 90.8%，经轨迹调整后的通过率为 92.9%，覆盖 GUI、API 和模型规划动作。

Conclusion: OpenAgentFlow 将安全治理从分散的组件级防护提升为动作提交边界上的系统级强制执行，能够为异构智能体提供统一、可审计且可动态更新的治理机制。实验结果表明其在 Android 场景中具有较好的有效性，但摘要尚未说明跨平台泛化能力、策略冲突处理和运行开销。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>


### [91] [SCAFFOLD: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset of Computer Science Research Figures with Diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00018)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAFFOLD，一个面向计算机科学论文图表理解的结构化视觉语言数据集，包含图像、标题、上下文、问答及逐步推理信息，并提供不同规模的数据版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学论文中的架构图、流程图和管线图承载了大量关键信息，但现有公开数据集缺乏同时配对图表、标题、上下文、问题、答案和推理过程的数据，限制了视觉语言模型对科研图表的理解与训练。

Method: 从arXiv计算机科学论文中提取图表及其标题和上下文，结合版面检测与PDF解析构建数据，并通过AI辅助的问题生成流程形成图表问答和Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹。数据以图像、标题、上下文、问答和推理组成的元组表示，发布SCAFFOLD-157K、SCAFFOLD-37K和SCAFFOLD-12K三个规模版本，并使用SCAFFOLD-12K对Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct进行基线实验。

Result: SCAFFOLD-157K覆盖3,058篇论文、29,887幅图表和157,387个数据对；SCAFFOLD-37K包含36,797个数据对；SCAFFOLD-12K包含12,000个数据对。摘要未报告基线实验的具体性能指标。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD为计算机科学科研图表理解提供了大规模、带上下文和推理标注的数据资源，可用于训练和评估视觉语言模型；其实际有效性仍需结合完整实验结果、标注质量和跨论文泛化性能进一步验证。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [92] [UI-Venus-2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00028)
*Venus Team,Zhuohan Cai,Haoxing Chen,Jiaxuan Chen,Weizhi Chen,Changlong Gao,Zhangxuan Gu,Yuan Guo,Yusong Hu,Jianrong Jiang,Jianguo Li,Runze Li,Jinzhen Lin,Zhenyu Ma,Changhua Meng,Han Peng,Xinyu Qiu,Shuheng Shen,Zhongyi Shui,Weiqiang Wang,Ming Wen,Zhuoer Xu,Hang Yan,Kaiwen Yang,Ruilin Yao,Nanjun Yu,Zhengwen Zeng,Lianrui Zhang,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhe Zhao,Beitong Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UI-Venus-2是一种面向移动端、网页和桌面环境的通用多模态GUI智能体，通过统一的闭环推理—行动框架，并扩展环境、任务与验证机制，提升真实场景中的泛化性、可靠性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体主要面向基准测试，存在环境覆盖不足、任务构造脆弱以及奖励验证不可靠等问题，难以稳定迁移到真实应用。因此，研究旨在构建覆盖更广、任务更贴近功能需求且训练信号更可信的基础GUI智能体。

Method: 提出UI-Venus-2统一闭环推理—行动框架；在环境层面覆盖170多个多语言移动应用及原生桌面操作系统；通过深度研究流程生成基于功能的指令任务；采用轨迹级和样本级评估器，结合视觉关键点与多模型投票提高验证可靠性；同时集成安全机制，对具有后果的操作实施受控执行。

Result: 论文声称UI-Venus-2具备跨移动、网页和桌面环境的通用操作能力，并通过扩大环境与任务规模、增强验证机制和引入安全控制，获得更适合强化学习训练及现实部署的基础能力。摘要未提供具体基准数据、消融实验结果或量化性能提升。

Conclusion: UI-Venus-2通过协同扩展环境、任务和验证三个维度，推动GUI智能体从基准导向走向更具泛化性、可验证性和自反性的真实应用。其实际优势仍需结合完整实验结果、开源实现和不同环境下的独立评测进一步验证。

Abstract: Multimodal GUI agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for digital task automation, yet transitioning from benchmark-oriented models to dependable real-world applications remains challenging due to limited environment coverage, brittle task construction, and unreliable reward verification. In this work, we present UI-Venus-2, a general-purpose foundation GUI agent designed to operate across mobile, web, and desktop environments through a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. To bridge the gap toward practical deployment, we jointly scale three critical dimensions: (1) Environments, expanding coverage to more than 170 multilingual mobile apps and native desktop operating systems; (2) Tasks, employing a deep-research pipeline for function-grounded instruction generation; and (3) Verification, adopting trace-level and sample-level evaluators with visual keypoints and multi-model voting to ensure reliable RL signals for training. Furthermore, we integrate safety-aware mechanisms to ensure controlled execution of consequential actions. By offering a capable, efficient, and open-source foundation, UI-Venus-2 advances the field toward more generalizable, verifiable, and self-reflective agents for real-world applications.

</details>


### [93] [EULER: Exploring Underused Links with Evidence-Checked Return for Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00032)
*Ren Zhenzhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EULER是一种以“跨数学领域桥接”为搜索单元的多智能体系统，在120个近期猜想上获得10个证明、3个反驳和45个范围受限的部分结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同数学共同体使用不同的对象、不变量和工具，将问题迁移到其他领域成本高且常被省略，因此需要系统化、可验证的跨领域问题转移方法。

Method: 围绕固定猜想，EULER并行竞争直接领域、相邻领域和远距离领域路线。桥接只有在引入源表示无法执行的新操作，并且目标领域证据能通过经检查的蕴含关系返回原命题时才保留预算。系统在昂贵搜索前使用六项有序压力测试排除无效桥接，并通过冻结猜想、污染筛查和消融实验评估方法。

Result: 在120个猜想上，系统得到10个证明、3个反驳和45个范围受限的部分结果。消融实验显示，桥接专用压力测试将错误结论从9个降至3个；桥接材料与目标领域原生操作结合产生了正向交互，额外解决4.2个任务，而两者单独使用均未产生该效果。领域距离不能可靠预测成功，真正有效的指标是可执行操作增益和有效回返。

Conclusion: EULER表明，跨领域推理的关键不在于领域距离本身，而在于桥接是否提供新的可执行操作，以及目标侧证据能否严格返回原问题。早期桥接验证和目标领域操作的组合能够提升数学猜想搜索的可靠性与效果。

Abstract: Mathematical communities work with different objects, invariants, and tools, so transferring a problem across them is expensive and often skipped. We present EULER, a multi-agent system that takes such a transfer--a bridge--as its unit of search. Around a fixed conjecture, EULER runs direct, adjacent-domain, and distant-domain routes in competition; a bridge keeps its budget only if it supplies an operation the source representation cannot execute and its target-side evidence returns to the original statement along a checked implication. Six ordered stress tests reject invalid bridges before expensive search begins.
  We evaluate EULER on 120 recent conjectures. The conjectures were frozen before search and screened for contamination, and are drawn from public papers by authors who had recently published in the Journal of Combinatorial Theory, Series A, a leading journal in combinatorics. EULER produced 10 proofs and 3 refutations, plus 45 scoped partial results. Two mechanisms held up under ablation: bridge-specific stress tests cut incorrect conclusions from 9 to 3, and bridge material combined with a target-native operation yielded a positive interaction of +4.2 resolved tasks that neither factor produced alone. Domain distance did not reliably predict success; executable operation gain and valid return did.

</details>


### [94] [When Prediction Error Is Not Enough: Evaluating Nuisance-Function Prediction for Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00071)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在部分线性模型的模拟中，预测误差不能稳定代表因果估计质量；XGBoost通常具有最低RMSE，而DML-XGBoost通常具有更好的置信区间覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究预测误差与因果估计器实际表现之间的关系，明确其在点估计、偏差、均方根误差和区间推断中的适用边界。

Method: 基于部分线性模型开展蒙特卡洛模拟，比较OLS、广义加性模型、XGBoost和结合XGBoost的双重机器学习方法。评估暴露和结局干扰函数的预测误差、因果估计偏差、RMSE及95%置信区间覆盖率，并检验基于两类估计误差绝对交叉乘积的联合误差指标。

Result: 在非预言方法中，XGBoost的RMSE通常最低，但DML-XGBoost通常具有更好的95%置信区间覆盖率。预测误差在不同方法和模拟设定下均未能稳定反映因果偏差；点估计表现最佳的方法不一定具有最佳区间覆盖率。联合误差指标与因果偏差仅呈弱关联，作为独立因果性能指标的实用性有限。

Conclusion: 预测误差适合用于评估干扰函数的拟合质量，但不应直接视为因果估计器质量的替代指标。因果分析应同时考察偏差、RMSE和置信区间覆盖率等与研究目标相关的性能指标。

Abstract: Prediction error is widely used to evaluate nuisance-function estimators in causal inference, but its relationship with causal estimator performance may differ across performance measures. We studied this question in a partially linear model using Monte Carlo simulations. We compared ordinary least squares (OLS), generalized additive models (GAMs), XGBoost, and Double Machine Learning with XGBoost (DML-XGBoost), evaluating nuisance-function prediction error, bias, RMSE, and 95\% confidence interval coverage. We also examined a simple joint-error measure based on the absolute cross-product of estimation errors from the exposure and outcome nuisance functions. Across the simulated settings, XGBoost had the lowest RMSE among the non-oracle methods, while DML-XGBoost generally provided better confidence interval coverage. Prediction error did not consistently track causal bias across methods and settings, and the method with the best point-estimation performance did not necessarily have the best confidence interval coverage. The joint-error measure was only weakly associated with causal bias and did not provide a useful standalone measure of causal performance. These results suggest that prediction error is useful for assessing nuisance-function estimation, but it should not be treated as a direct measure of the quality of the resulting causal estimator.

</details>


### [95] [MiNER: Fine-Tuned Biomedical Natural Language Processing for Malaria Disease Entity Recognition in Clinical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00073)
*V. S. Anoop,Devika N*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于BioBERT微调的疟疾文献生物医学信息抽取方法，并发布人工标注的数据集。实验表明，该方法在实体抽取任务的精确率、召回率和准确率方面优于多种对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾相关文献数量持续增长，人工提取临床实体及其关系效率低、难度大，因此需要利用预训练语言模型自动挖掘关键生物医学信息，以支持疾病机制研究、流行病学分析和治疗干预研究。

Method: 首先收集并预处理大规模疟疾科学文献，随后对具有临床意义的实体及关系进行人工标注。采用BioBERT将文本编码为上下文相关表示，并基于领域标注数据和监督学习进行微调，从而完成生物医学命名实体及关系抽取。同时，将该任务与不同文本编码方法及机器学习算法进行比较。

Result: 实验结果显示，微调后的BioBERT方法在精确率、召回率和准确率方面均显著优于所比较的编码方法和机器学习算法。研究还公开了人工标注的实体与关系抽取数据集。

Conclusion: 领域适配后的BioBERT能够有效提升疟疾文献中的生物医学信息抽取性能。公开数据集为后续模型训练、方法比较和健康信息学研究提供了可复用资源。

Abstract: Malaria remains a significant global health burden, necessitating continuous research efforts to understand its complex molecular mechanisms, epidemiology, and potential therapeutic interventions. Extracting essential biomedical information from the vast and constantly growing malaria literature is a challenging task that demands innovative approaches. Recently, pre-trained language models have revolutionized natural language processing tasks, demonstrating remarkable capabilities in various domains. This paper proposes a fine-tuned pre-trained biomedical language model for biomedical information extraction from scientific literature on malaria disease. The proposed methodology selects and preprocesses a large corpus of scientific articles on malaria, and then annotates them with entities of clinical significance. It then leverages BioBERT, a state-of-the-art pre-trained language model, to encode the textual data into context-aware representations. We fine-tune the model using domain-specific annotations and supervised learning to enhance its ability to extract relevant biomedical named entities. Extensive experiments and comparisons with different encoding and machine learning algorithms show that the proposed approach significantly outperforms them in precision, recall, and accuracy. We also publish our human-labeled dataset for entity and relation extraction to enable other health informatics researchers to train advanced models for malaria information extraction.

</details>


### [96] [AI Morbidity and Mortality: A Framework for Clinical AI Failure Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00076)
*Paulius Mui,Dean F. Sittig,Steve Labkoff,Sanjay Basu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Morbidity and Mortality（AI M&M）框架，用于系统复盘临床AI相关故障与近失事件，将个案错误转化为可执行的机构改进措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型监测可发现性能变化，传统患者安全报告可记录不良事件，但难以解释AI系统、临床人员、工作流程与机构控制措施之间如何共同产生风险，也不利于从单个事件中学习。

Method: 框架包括标准化事件采集、证据保存、研究者级事件重建、工具在环归因和纠正措施追踪。每个事件沿四个关联维度分类：触发因素、作用机制、临床路径和纠正措施。研究以5个门诊用药及临床决策支持案例进行示范，由两名临床评审者独立完成全部分类。

Result: 两名评审者共完成20项维度级分类，所有分类结果均达成一致，初步表明该框架具有较好的应用一致性和可操作性。

Conclusion: AI M&M可作为模型监测、患者安全报告和监管监督的补充，通过结构化复盘将临床工作流中的AI故障转化为机构层面的改进学习。其跨机构、跨系统和跨临床场景的前瞻性有效性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Clinical artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in real-world care, yet existing safety mechanisms are poorly suited to reconstructing and learning from individual AI-related errors and near-misses. Aggregate model monitoring can identify performance changes, and traditional patient safety reporting can capture adverse events, but neither is designed to explain how risk emerges across the interaction among AI systems, clinicians, workflows, and institutional controls. We propose AI Morbidity and Mortality (AI M&M), a structured, blameless framework for case-based review of clinical AI failures. The framework combines standardized case intake, evidence preservation and investigator-level reconstruction, tool-in-loop attribution, and corrective-action tracking. Each event is classified across four linked dimensions: Trigger - Mechanism - Clinical Pathway - Corrective Action, separating the condition that exposed a vulnerability from the process that produced risk, its consequence for care, and the remediation assigned. We demonstrate the framework using five illustrative outpatient medication and clinical decision-support cases; two clinician reviewers independently applied all four classification axes and reached agreement across all 20 axis-level classifications. AI M&M is intended to complement, rather than replace, model monitoring, patient safety reporting, and regulatory oversight by converting individual AI-in-workflow failures into actionable institutional learning. Prospective evaluation across institutions, AI systems, and clinical settings is needed.

</details>


### [97] [Different representation learning objectives recover distinct latent structures from the same psychometric data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00100)
*Cong Cao,Tassos C. Kyriakides,Pambos Vrasidas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，表示学习目标会显著影响心理测量数据中潜在结构的恢复：对齐教师—儿童数据的对比学习提升了配对检索性能，但削弱了行为表型结构；多任务学习可部分平衡两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理测量问卷包含丰富的题目层面信息，但不同表示学习目标是否能够恢复相同的潜在组织结构仍不明确。研究旨在比较教师—儿童对应关系与行为表型这两类潜在结构之间的关系。

Method: 基于塞浦路斯ProW学前试验基线阶段的757对匹配教师—儿童样本，使用儿童SDQ、ASBI和CBRS题目反应开展主成分分析与聚类，提取四类行为表型。比较PCA表示、对比学习表示及同时优化配对对齐和行为预测的多任务表示，并以教师—儿童检索准确率和行为表型结构保持程度进行评估。

Result: 对比学习显著提升了教师—儿童检索性能：Top-1准确率由0.13%提高至7.27%，Top-10准确率由1.98%提高至56.14%；但其保持行为表型结构的能力弱于PCA表示。多任务目标能够部分恢复行为组织结构，但会降低检索性能。

Conclusion: 教师—儿童对应关系与行为表型是不同类型的潜在组织结构，二者难以由单一表示完全兼顾。心理测量数据中所恢复的潜在结构依赖于表示学习目标，实际建模时应根据研究目的选择或权衡对齐、预测与结构保持目标。

Abstract: Psychometric questionnaires contain rich item-level information, yet it remains unclear whether different representation learning objectives recover the same latent organization. We investigated this question using 757 matched teacher-child pairs from the baseline assessment of the Cyprus ProW preschool trial. Behavioral structure was characterized from child SDQ, ASBI, and CBRS item responses using principal component analysis and clustering, yielding four behavioral phenotypes. A contrastive objective substantially improved teacher-child retrieval relative to PCA-based representations, increasing Top-1 accuracy from 0.13% to 7.27% and Top-10 accuracy from 1.98% to 56.14%. However, contrastive representations preserved behavioral phenotype structure less effectively than PCA-based representations. A multi-task objective jointly optimizing alignment and behavioral prediction partially restored behavioral organization but reduced retrieval performance. These findings indicate that teacher-child correspondence and behavioral phenotypes represent distinct forms of latent organization and demonstrate that the latent structure recovered from linked psychometric data depends on the representation learning objective.

</details>


### [98] [Deploying and Evaluating a Smart-Agriculture Agentic Engine for Full-Season Soybean Farm Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00106)
*Ao Qu,Panagiotis Michelakis,Linyuan Han,Yiannis Hadjiyianni,Kun Ouyang,Konstantinos Siskos,Feng Li,Ran Meng,Jingchi Jiang,Dimitrios Stamoulis,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并部署了面向真实大豆科研农场的全栈智能农业智能体系统FAIRY，用于执行和评估覆盖完整生长季的时空农事流程，并从任务成功率、时空路径正确性、令牌成本和边缘设备运行时间等维度比较九种智能体控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业智能体通常仅处理孤立决策或静态数据，难以建模跨空间、跨时间且具有延迟效应和操作耦合的完整农事流程。FAIRY旨在提供一个接近真实生产环境的执行、追踪与评估平台。

Method: 系统采用“万物皆事件”的执行范式，将环境演化、遥感观测、传感器读数、作物生长转变、机械操作和管理干预统一表示为状态变更事件，并通过共享农场流程引擎进行编排。系统集成生产级农机、土壤与冠层传感器、多光谱和热红外无人机、卫星植被产品、气象站、作物过程模型、农事记录及多年产量历史；同时包含原子化农艺技能库、多智能体控制与编排后端、前沿模型和边缘模型执行、全路径追踪日志及本地节点部署性能分析。研究在64条垄的田间空间和完整生长季流程上构建100个大豆场景，评估九种先进智能体控制器。

Result: 摘要明确说明完成了真实农场部署、全季节场景构建和多维评估体系设计，但未报告九种控制器的具体性能、排名、统计显著性或产量提升结果。评估指标包括智能体任务成功率、完整时空路径正确性、令牌成本和边缘设备运行时间。

Conclusion: FAIRY为真实农业环境中的全季节智能体研究提供了统一的事件驱动基础设施和可复现实验框架，能够同时考察农事执行的时空一致性、资源消耗与边缘部署效率。不过，依据该摘要尚无法判断不同控制器的实际优劣或系统对最终产量的影响。

Abstract: This paper presents FAIRY, a full-stack smart-agriculture agent system developed for and deployed to an operating soybean research farm at Harbin Institute of Technology's smart-agriculture site. We develop FAIRY to execute and evaluate agentic agronomic operations on full-season spatiotemporal workflows that span ridge preparation, planting, irrigation, fertilization, pest and disease treatment, harvest, grain handling, drying, and storage. FAIRY integrates APIs and infrastructure across production-grade machinery, fixed soil and canopy sensors, multispectral and thermal drones, satellite vegetation products, a weather station, calibrated crop-process models, agronomic records, and multi-season yield histories. The system is built around the novel "everything is an event" execution paradigm, which represents spatiotemporal world evolution, remote sensing and UAV observations, sensor readings, crop-growth transitions, machinery actions, and management interventions as state-changing events in a shared farm process engine. On top of this event-driven world model, FAIRY implements a complete agentic stack: a knowledge library of atomic agronomic skills; multi-agent controller and orchestration backends; frontier- and edge-model execution; full-path trace logging; and deployment profiling on local nodes. We use FAIRY to evaluate nine state-of-the-art agent controllers across one hundred full-season soybean scenarios that preserve the operational coupling between spatial observations in a 64-ridge field, temporal decision sequences, agronomic constraints, delayed effects, and final yield. We develop an evaluation suite that combines agentic success, full-path spatiotemporal correctness, token cost, and edge-device runtime.

</details>


### [99] [Recursive Criticality of AI Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00137)
*Mikhail Burtsev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个描述AI研发反馈是否会自我放大的理论模型，并提出递归再生产数𝓡_AI作为判据：𝓡_AI>1表示改进在研发周期中逐步放大，𝓡_AI<1表示其逐步衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI被用于研发更强的AI系统，可能形成递归反馈，但快速进步并不必然意味着存在自我放大机制。本文旨在区分递归放大与其他进步来源，并确定反馈转入自我放大所需的条件。

Method: 构建理论模型，将AI能力增长表示为基线研发生产率、递归反馈强度、改进向后继系统传播的效率、研发周期长度及研究难度增长的函数。通过定义递归再生产数𝓡_AI，比较反馈增强效应与后续进步难度之间的关系，并进一步扩展至多个研发主体共享改进的情形。

Result: 当𝓡_AI>1时，改进效应跨周期复合，系统进入自我放大状态；当𝓡_AI<1时，改进效应逐周期减弱。自我放大转变不依赖特定能力水平，可能在加速尚不明显前发生；反之，快速进步也可能没有自我放大。更高的基线研发生产率能加快进步，但不一定改变自我放大状态。研发周期长度限制放大速度，研究难度上升可能终止自我放大。多主体共享改进时，整体研发生态可能自我放大，即使单个主体并未自我放大。

Conclusion: 判断AI研发是否自我放大，应重点测量递归反馈强度、改进向后继系统的传播效率、研发周期时长以及研究难度的增长，而不能仅依据能力提升速度或当前能力水平。

Abstract: AI is increasingly used in the R\&D process that produces future AI systems. We study the conditions under which this feedback becomes self-amplifying. Our model describes how the rate of AI capability growth depends on baseline research productivity, recursive feedback, and the increasing difficulty of research progress. We derive a recursive reproduction number, $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{AI}}$, that determines whether improvements are amplified or damped across development cycles. This quantity compares the strength of feedback with the rate at which further progress becomes more difficult. When $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{AI}}>1$, the effects of improvements compound across development cycles, placing the system in a self-amplifying regime. When $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{AI}}<1$, their effects weaken across cycles. The transition depends on the structure of the AI R\&D feedback loop and need not occur at any particular level of model capability. A system can therefore enter a self-amplifying regime before acceleration becomes visible, while rapid progress can also occur without self-amplification. Higher baseline research productivity can accelerate progress without changing whether the system is self-amplifying, but the duration of the development cycle becomes a limiting timescale for amplification. Increasing research difficulty can end a period of self-amplification. Extending the model to multiple research actors shows that improvements shared across organizations can make the overall research ecosystem self-amplifying even when no individual actor is. The framework identifies measurable properties of AI R\&D systems that can help distinguish recursive amplification from rapid progress driven by other sources, including the strength of recursive feedback, how effectively improvements propagate into successor systems, cycle duration, and the increasing difficulty of further progress.

</details>


### [100] [IMPACT: Attention Is the Interaction Map for Scalable Interaction-Aware World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00161)
*Rongze Tang,Jianjie Fang,Zhaolu Wang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Haisheng Su,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IMPACT是一种面向动作条件世界模型的训练框架，通过重新分配去噪监督权重，增强模型对交互区域和动态物体的建模能力，无需额外标注、外部表征或推理阶段改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常采用全局平均MSE去噪目标。由于画面中静态区域占多数，其梯度信号会主导优化过程；而对物理交互最关键、但空间上稀疏的动态物体区域则被监督不足。这种监督分配失衡使模型难以生成可信的接触、运动和物理响应。

Method: 作者提出IMPACT（Interaction-aware Model training framework with Prior-guided Attention Calibration and Targeting）。该方法将与被操作物体词元相关的交叉注意力视为动作引起时空变化的内部先验；先从该先验中采样候选区域，再利用停止梯度的局部预测误差对候选区域进行校准，形成交互图；最后以交互图对去噪损失进行重加权，使训练更关注交互相关区域。该方案不依赖辅助估计器、人工标注或外部运动、几何及语义表示。

Result: 在机械臂和人手操作任务上，覆盖多种控制模态及DiT骨干网络的实验表明，IMPACT相较对应的标准MSE训练基线具有稳定优势。其主要改进体现在交互保真度、物理合理性和视觉质量。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是指出全局MSE造成的监督分配失衡，并利用模型内部交叉注意力与局部误差构建可扩展的交互感知重加权机制。该方法以较低的额外依赖提升了动作条件视频预测中的交互建模质量，适合作为世界模型训练目标的通用增强策略。

Abstract: World models have made remarkable progress in action-conditioned future prediction for embodied agents, yet still struggle to model physically plausible interactions. Existing approaches address this limitation by constraining the generation process with external representations encoding motion, geometry, or semantics. Obtaining these spatiotemporally dense representations typically requires auxiliary estimators or manual annotations, limiting training scalability. We instead revisit the training objective and identify a supervision-allocation mismatch under the globally averaged mean squared error (MSE) denoising objective: prevalent static content dominates the optimization signal, leaving sparse dynamic-object regions critical to interaction generation disproportionately under-supervised. Motivated by this observation, we introduce IMPACT, a scalable Interaction-aware Model training framework with Prior-guided Attention Calibration and Targeting. IMPACT uses cross-attention associated with manipulated-object tokens as an internal spatiotemporal prior for action-conditioned changes. It samples candidate regions from this prior, calibrates them with detached local prediction errors to construct an interaction map, and uses the map to reweight denoising supervision, requiring neither external representations nor inference-time modifications. Extensive experiments on robot-arm and human-hand manipulation, spanning diverse control modalities and DiT backbones, show that IMPACT consistently outperforms the corresponding MSE-trained baselines, improving interaction fidelity, physical plausibility, and visual quality.

</details>


### [101] [Asymmetries in Spontaneous and Instructed Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00180)
*Josiah Luikham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct中“指令诱导欺骗”与“自发（未指令）欺骗”的关系，发现二者存在中等程度的共享表征，但在检测模型与干预操控的跨场景迁移上呈现明显不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型欺骗研究较多关注通过明确指令诱导的欺骗，而模型也可能在未被要求时产生欺骗行为。作者旨在判断这两类欺骗是否依赖相同的内部机制，以及基于一种场景训练的检测或控制方法能否迁移至另一种场景。
